# GUI engine (`crates/ao-gui`, `ao_render::GuiRenderer`)

Native re-implementation of the original client's GUI.dll view system, used for the login-flow screens
(`docs/screens.md`). Evidence is from the decompiled client DLLs (addresses `0x1xxxxxxx` = GUI.dll unless noted);
anything not read from the binary is labelled **UNRESOLVED** or **[INFERENCE]**.

Pipeline: `Views/<name>.xml` → `view::build` (tree) → `layout` (preferred sizes + HV nodes) → `Gui::frame()` →
`DrawList` (`Gfx`/`Solid`/glyph commands in GUI pixels) → `GuiRenderer::draw`. Input: `Gui::input(InputEvent)` →
`Vec<Event>`. Coordinates are logical window px (the app handles display scale).
Reproduce renders: `cargo run --release -p ao-gui --example dump_view -- LoginWindow out.png --frame --sample`
(`--size WxH` for full-screen views such as `CharacterSelectionWindow`).

Preferred-size evaluation (`layout.rs`, GUI.dll `LayoutNode::CalculatePreferredSize`
0x10130ded and H/V variants 0x1012ff61 / 0x101304c1) memoizes ungrouped
clamped sizes until a frame changes. Minimum aggregation does not recurse into
maximum sizes. Width-group discovery bypasses this cache while its recursion
sentinel is active; completed groups discard temporary preferred values.
Changed frames invalidate both caches because multiline text uses the current
frame width. Regression: `layout::tests::deep_preferred_sizes_are_cached_and_frames_invalidate_groups`
(requires installed client assets).

Pure window moves update the screen origin without re-running client layout
(`WndBorder::MouseMove` 0x10159c27 → `DoSetFrame` 0x10159888); size changes
still lay out the client. Repeated unchanged text/visibility updates likewise
leave child geometry intact. Regression:
`gui::clip_tests::unchanged_updates_and_moves_preserve_child_layout`.

### GUI view lifetime

Native ownership rule (not a retail layout/behaviour claim): `Tree` retains stable
indices for live nodes and recycles emptied subtree slots. `remove_children`,
`remove_view_in`, window close and failed wrapper construction reclaim all
descendants. Cleanup removes only reclaimed IDs from hover/press/focus,
selection, tooltips (including pending timers), canvas double-click tracking,
scroll/list/header/dropdown interactions, default buttons, item roots, tab
state, deferred list measurements and chat text fades. Other live windows retain
their interaction state. Window IDs remain monotonic and are not reused.

Public `ViewHandle`s carry a slot generation: a delayed click event or retained
row handle cannot mutate a new subtree occupying an old slot. Internal `ViewId`
indices remain unchanged. Regressions: `view::lifetime_tests` (asset-independent),
`gui::clip_tests::rebuilt_subtrees_and_closed_windows_reuse_bounded_slots` and
`gui::clip_tests::recycled_views_do_not_inherit_pointer_focus_fades_or_public_handles`
(installed assets; Programs-style row replacement, chat close/reopen, options
dropdown/list widgets, wrapper errors and stale interaction state).

Shared application `hud_listview::ListView` content refreshes (NCU, Programs,
actions and missions) retain canvas/row controls when ordered item keys and
layout are unchanged. Text, icons, selection colors and tooltips update in
place; changed keys/layout use normal subtree reclamation. Chat does not use
this application list helper. The shared GUI `SetToolTip` rule refreshes changed
tooltip texts and shown metrics without discarding hover identity.
Regression: `hud_listview::tests::timer_updates_preserve_hover_and_press_identity`
(installed assets; one-second refresh, subsequent mouse move, press/release
across refresh, list double click and removal).

## 1. Skin archive (`gfx.rs`)

* `cd_image/gui/Default/Graphics.uvgi`: text; line 1 = entry count, then `name offset length`. `Graphics.uvga` = concatenated PNGs
  (Interfaces.dll `GuiResourceManager_t::ParseFile` 0x1000b86d).
* Ids: AFCM.dll `DynamicID_t` static table @0x10016060 (a table of `char*` pointing at the full names); the id of a `GFX_*` name is
  its index in the 487-name built-in list (`crates/ao-gui/data/gfx_ids.txt`, line number − 1, read from that table in full: an earlier
  copy cut at 40 characters gave 13 wrong names, e.g. 0x93–0x98 `…BIGARROW_{LEFT,RIGHT}_STATE1..3` were identical and their
  images unreachable; also 0x92 `AGGDEF_SLIDER_BACKGROUND`, the golden button slices 0x9e/0xa0/0xa7/0xa9 (`BUTTON01/03_MIDDLE/RIGHT_GOLD`)
  and 0xab/0xac `TARGET_BUTTON_STATE2/3`; test `gfx::tests::id_names_are_unique_and_arrows_resolve`); names only present in the uvgi are appended.
  GUI.dll hard-codes numeric ids (e.g. `Button_c::Initialize` 0x10128994: 0x1e..0x26 = `GFX_GUI_BORDER01_*`).
* Colour key: pure green `0x00FF00` → transparent, every other pixel opaque (DisplaySystem.dll `SpriteInfo_t::ConvertImage` 0x1007b8e2).
  PNGs may be RGB or RGBA.

## 2. Fonts (`font.rs`)

`FontSystem_t` table 0x10272df0: TOOLTIP/SHELL/CLOCK bitmap `.fnt` fonts (256×256 skin image, 255 glyph rects + widths, advance = `x1−x0+1`);
NORMAL (Verdana 13), BOLD (13 bold), SMALL (12), LARGE (16), HUGE (24 bold), TT_MIN12 (14), ITALIC (16 italic), CHAT (prefs) are GDI
TrueType, aliased 1-bpp glyphs (`CreateFontA`, `TextOutW`). We rasterise with skrifa mono hinting from the host Verdana
(client does not ship it). **UNRESOLVED platform equivalence:** native **0x1012ec5c** calls Windows `CreateFontA` / `GetTextMetricsA`; **0x1012e73c** delegates glyph rasterization to `TextOutW` in a 1-bit bitmap. The host Verdana/skrifa scan converter is an approximation; exact Windows font revision and GDI-produced glyph masks/dropout reference fixtures are unavailable in this headless macOS environment. No exact-font claim is made. **CC17 height is resolved:** assembly **0x1012efab–1012efdb** multiplies integer20 (`0x10272e4c = 0x14`) by `f32(displayHeight/768)`, then truncates; decompilation hid that multiply. `Gui::set_screen_size` updates that font and invalidates glyph/layout caches only when its integer height changes, and the production frontend now supplies screen size each frame. Normal font sizes remain fixed.
Regressions `cc17_requested_height_uses_native_display_ratio_and_truncation` and `display_height_rescales_only_cc17_and_invalidates_atlas_on_change` separately cover exact requested lfHeight768→800 (20),1536 (40), host-raster metric growth, fixed NORMAL metrics and glyph invalidation/no atlas churn. Requested lfHeight is not assumed identical to the approximate host rasterizer's actual rounded cell height. Font rasterizer byte parity is deliberately not asserted.

## 3. Colours and text (`text.rs`)

`GUIColors.xml` ids `DEFAULT 0x1000000, SELECTED 0x2000000, HOVER 0x3000000, TEXT 0x4000000, TEXT_SELECTED 0x5000000, TEXT_HOVER 0x6000000`
(`GUIConfig_c::GetColorID` 0x1012f3e2) map to the palette (defaults from `GUIConfig_c` 0x1012f342: `80e9f3, ffffcc, a5ffdb, 99ccaa …`);
other colour attributes are literal `0xRRGGBB`. Button/frame tints multiply the surface by the palette colour.
`TextView_c` = HTML subset (`br, center, font color, a, p, div, b, i, u`; others ignored) laid out by `_ReWrap` 0x10161ba4 / `_RenderLine` 0x10161112.
Feature flags (`feature_flags="TVF_…|…"`): ACCEPT_TXT_INPUT 1, ACCEPT_MOUSE_INPUT 4, ALLOW_TEXT_SELECTION 8, **PASSWORD 0x10**, MULTILINE 0x20,
WORD_WRAP 0x40, WORD_SPLIT 0x80, IGNORE_NEWLINES 0x100, DISABLE_RC_MENU 0x200, FILL_BOTTOM_UP 0x400, ENABLE_SHADOW 0x800, RENDER_SHADOW 0x1000,
NUMERIC 0x2000, ALT_ENTER_MODE 0x4000.

**Password mask**: `TextRenderer_c::_RenderString` 0x1015ffdd: `if (flags & 0x100 != 0 …) { if (flags & 0x10) ch = 0x2a; }` → every glyph of the
field is drawn as **`*`** (ASCII 0x2a) in the field font, advance of `*`. Copy/Cut are disabled for password fields.
`TextView_c::SlotTabPressed` 0x10002a83: a text field that gains focus by Tab selects all of its text.
`_AddLineDesc` **0x10161b44** advances line y by the recorded maximum run height, substituting renderer default font height (`+0x1c4`) only when that height is0. The port now preserves short image-only lines rather than imposing a font-height floor; mixed text/image lines retain the taller run. Regression `line_pitch_uses_run_height_and_empty_line_font_fallback` covers shorter/taller images and empty lines.

## 4. View XML (`view.rs`, `xml.rs`)

Root `<root>` → first child is the window root view. Elements: `View`, `HLayoutSpacer`/`VLayoutSpacer`, `BorderView`, `TextView`, `Button`,
`TextButton`, `PowerBar`, `BitmapView`, `TextInputView`, `ComboBox`, `DropdownMenu`, `StringListView`, `MultiListView` (engine-only element, §14), `ScrollView`/`ScrollViewChild`, `CheckBox`, `RadioButton`, `RadioButtonGroup`;
unknown elements/attributes are collected in `Gui::warnings`.
Common attributes (`XMLObject_c` / `View::LoadFromXML`): `name`, `view_layout` (`horizontal|vertical`), `layout_borders="Rect(l,t,r,b)"`,
`min_size`, `max_size`, `max_size_limit` (`Point(x,y)`), `h_alignment`/`v_alignment` (`LEFT|CENTER|RIGHT|…`), `view_flags`, `tab_order`.
Text: `value` (`#Key` = text.mdb label via the `localize` callback, see `docs/screens.md` §8.2), `font`, `color`, `feature_flags`.
Buttons: `label`/`text`, `color`/`hover_color`/`pressed_color` (TextButton), `bg_gfx`/`full_gfx` (PowerBar).
Integers accept decimal or `0x…` (`GetAttrInt`).

## 5. Layout (`layout.rs`, `geom.rs`)

Rects/points are **inclusive extents** (pixels − 1; empty = −1). `View::UpdatePreferredSize` 0x1014aac8 clamps by `min_size`/`max_size`/`max_size_limit`;
`HLayoutNode` 0x1012ff61/0x101300d7, `VLayoutNode` 0x101304c1/0x10130631 (children get `min…max`, the surplus is distributed by `View::SpaceOut`
0x1014a238 weights; spacers have weight); `layout_borders` shrink each child. Hidden views keep their layout space unless `VF_COLLAPSE_WHEN_HIDDEN` (0x100).
`TextRenderer_c::CalculatePreferredSize` 0x101623ea: if the min/max pref point has `x>0 && y>=0` it is returned as is; otherwise the text is laid out
(word wrap width = `min.x+1` or the current frame).
`Button` (XML) preferred size 0x10127ee4: label size with borders Rect(8,1,8,4), +10 width.

**Initial window size**: `LoginWindow_c` 0x10015331, `ProgressWindow_c` 0x100164cc and `LimboWindow_c` 0x10010311 call the root's
`GetPreferredSize(true)` (vtable +0x9c, arg 1 = *max*) and `Window::SetFrame(Rect(0,0,pref))` + `MoveToCenter`. `WindowSize::Preferred` does exactly this.

## 6. Window frame, style 1 (`Gui::open_framed_window`)

Original: `Window(rect, title, name, style 1, flags)` → `WndBorder::SetStyle` → `SetBorderGfx` 0x1015a82d (see `docs/screens.md` §9 for the ids).

* **Metrics**: `UpdateBorderSizes` 0x10159f98: style 0 → border Rect(3,7,3,3), **style 1 → Rect(3, 24, 3, 3)** (constants 0x101a96d4=3, 0x101b8238=24),
  others 0; plus the embedded `TabView` border sizes (+0x188..), which are all 0 for style 1 (`TabView::LayoutBorders` 0x10145be5 with
  render flags 0; `SetBorderGfx` calls `TabView::SetRenderFlags(0)`). `CalculatePreferredSize` 0x101596bb = client preferred size (tab view
  pref minus tab borders); `WindowToClient` 0x10158cd2 shrinks by those borders. So: outer = client + (3, 24, 3, 3).
* **Art**: outer `BorderView_c` `GFX_GUI_WINDOW3_BORDER_{TL,TR,BL,BR,LEFT,TOP,RIGHT,BOTTOM}` + `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_BACKGROUND` (corners 3×8, top 16×8),
  local alpha = layer-1 alpha 0.33; geometry from `BorderView_c::CreateGfx` 0x10125d3b. Inner border (`top_level_border`, local colour DEFAULT
  0x1000000) `GFX_GUI_TAB_BORDER_*` + `GFX_GUI_TAB_BACKGROUND`, its frame = **client frame grown by 1 px on every side** (`DoSetFrame` 0x10159888:
  `Rect::Resize(-1,-1,+1,+1)`).
* **Buttons** (`CreateBorderIcons` 0x1015aba4, only for styles 0/1/3 and `flags & 4 == 0`): icon button `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_ICON_I` (BorderID 0)
  and close button `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_CLOSE_X` / `_STATE2` / `_STATE3` (BorderID 1); the pin button only without flag 0x800 (not for the login flow).
  `Layout` 0x1015a1d9 (details and the corrections to this paragraph: §6.2): every border button is placed at **y = 5** from the window top; the left list
  starts 5 px right of the border (x = acc, `acc += 5` before and `+= w − 1` after each button), the right list runs inwards from the right edge
  (`x = R − w − acc`). Sprites are 15×15. The icon button is created and added unconditionally (so it is drawn on
  the login windows; `Window::SetIcon` 0x101542d9 is not called by them – callers are other windows), and its click only acts when an icon
  popup menu (`+0x208`) exists, i.e. never here.
* **Close**: `SlotCloseButton` 0x10159705 posts message 0x98968b to the window. Engine: `Event::CloseRequested{window}` when the mouse is released
  over the close button (the application decides: LoginWindow → quit, ProgressDialog → ignore). The `Button_c` state mapping is resolved in §6.2
  (state 1 = pressed art, state 2 = hover overlay; the old hover=`_STATE2` / pressed=`_STATE3` guess was reversed; the sprites are pixel-identical, only the tint differs).
* `set_window_pos` / `open_framed_window` take the **outer** top-left; `outer_size`, `window_size` (client).
* Hit-testing / dragging / resizing of style-0 frames: RE'd and ported for the chat windows (docs/chat/gui.md §10: `WndBorder::HitTest` 0x101593d6, `MouseMove` 0x10159c27, `DoSetFrame` 0x10159888; `Gui::set_window_frame`);
  every HUD window is a style-0 tab frame and is draggable once the application sets `set_window_frame` (§6.5); style-1 frames (modal dialogs) stay fixed. The layer-1 alpha value is the
  0.33 default (`GUIConfig_c`), not read from prefs. The window title text is §6.1 (style 0); several tabs / tab dragging: docs/chat/gui.md §11.

### 6.1 Style-0 window with a tab strip (`Gui::open_tabbed_window[_xml]`)
The in-world windows are `DockWindow_c` (`FUN_1003bdd4`: `Window(Rect(), "", "", style 0, flags 0x1000)`), and a `DockableView_c` (`FUN_10038b47`) is a *tab*: `Window::InsertTab(index,
title, view)` with `title` = the `DockableView_c` constructor argument (`FUN_100389bd`). `Window::SetTitle` (0x1015427b) only stores a string, nothing draws it. The title texts are therefore
the constructor literals: `"Planet Map"` (`PlanetMapView_c` 0x1004d26a), `"PF Map"` (`PlayfieldMapView_c` 0x100eb905), `"Skills"` (`SkillWindow` Window ctor), `"Stats"`, `"Friends"`, `"Knowledge"`, `"Missions"`, `"NCU"`,
`"Actions"`, `"Faction"`, `"Trade"`, `"Tradeskill Kit"`, `"Info"`, `"Tips"`; `GetText(10000, "Wear")` (`WearView_c` 0x100e1bc9, string at 0x101b59ac), `"Inspect"` for the inspect variant; the inventory
passes `""` initially, then sets the localized `GetText(10000,"Inventory")` title (**0x100c9fb7**, implemented in `hud_stats.rs`). **`#WindowMap` / `#WindowPlanetMap` of `ControlCenterModule_c::SetupProviders` 0x10068c38 are not titles**: the four strings of each provider
(`FUN_10018788`) are dvalue name, key-binding option label (`#WindowPlanetMap`), group (`#Window`) and the provider key (`WINDOW_PLANETMAP`, §12.1).
* Style 0 (`WndBorder::SetBorderGfx` 0x1015a82d): the same outer `GFX_GUI_WINDOW3_BORDER_*` art as style 1 (alpha 0.33) but `TabView::SetRenderFlags(7)` and no inner `top_level_border`. Outer border
  `Rect(3, _DAT_101b6e54 = 7, 3, 3)` (`UpdateBorderSizes` 0x10159f98) + `TabView` borders `(2, strip + 1, 2, 2)` (`LayoutBorders` 0x10145be5: `+= _DAT_101a8b90 = 2.0` on all four, then the top is `+0x158` =
  tab-bar height + 1) → **client insets (5, 26, 5, 5)** (strip = 17 px tab + 1). The `TabView` is the outer rect minus the outer border.
* Tab strip (`TopBorderView_c` ctor `FUN_10147935`, flags 7, layer-2 alpha 0.85): the tabs sit in a container starting `TabView::SetLeftMargin(_DAT_101b4e08 = 20)` from the left edge (the 15 px icon button fills the margin); the box
  `GFX_GUI_TAB_BORDER_*` + `GFX_GUI_TAB_BACKGROUND` (the same 9 pieces as style 1's inner border) starts at strip row 16 so its 2 px top line (`TAB_BORDER_TOP/TL/TR`, `FUN_10146e94`) is the strip's last two rows.
* **Title allocation / clipping**: `WndBorder::Layout` 0x1015a1d9 accumulates `5 + preferred button extent - 1` for every right-hand button (close, optional pin, optional help), then calls `TabView::SetRightMargin` 0x1014652b. With 14 px button extents this reserves **18 px per button** inside the `TabView`, rather than drawing a natural-width title underneath the controls.
  `TopBorderView::Layout` 0x1014725e allocates tabs with equal-weight `View::SpaceOut`, bounded by the first-character minimum (`Tab::CalculatePreferredSize(false)` 0x101461dc) and the full-title maximum, rounds the allocations, and overlaps neighbours by 6 px (`0x101b36a8`). Generic window tabs now share that allocation with embedded tabs; drawing and hit rectangles both stop at the reserved strip edge, including when minimum tab widths exceed the available space. Tab art and formatted title text are clipped to their allocated rectangles; selected tabs still render last. Regression: `gui::frame::tests::window_tabs_allocate_and_clip_before_all_right_buttons` covers close/pin/help margins, draw scissors, multi-tab allocation, and an empty-width strip.
* `Tab` (ctor `FUN_10146785`): `BorderView_c::SetGfx(l, m, r)` (3-slice, `0x195/0x196/0x197` = `GFX_GUI_TAB_ACTIVE_LEFT/MIDDLE/RIGHT` 6/16/21 × 17 selected; `0x1a1..0x1a3` inactive) over a `TextView_c(font NORMAL)`;
  `FUN_101461dc` preferred width = title width + 1 + `Rect(5,0,16,0)` at `Tab+0x1e4` (5 + 16), height = art height; `Tab::SetSelected` `FUN_10146110`: selected = active art, alpha = layer 2, text colour `0xffffff`; unselected = inactive art
  at layer-2 alpha × `_DAT_101c4978` (= 2.0, **1.7**, not clamped there: `View::_CallRender` 0x1014d2e3 multiplies `view alpha × inherited alpha` down the tree, the tab's `TextView` child inherits it, and only `ViewArea_c::_Render` 0x1015291a clamps the vertex alpha to 0..255 (`FUN_10173ad0` = float→int, the decompile hides the ×255; the `<1 → 0`, `<255 → value`, else `255` branches are literal) — so an unselected tab is `min(1.7 × window alpha, 1)`: opaque in a fully lit window, 0.51 at the chat's inactive 0.3; the selected tab 0.85 × window alpha; `Window::SetAlpha` 0x1541bd sets the root view alpha = `+0x60` × level), default text colour 0 (`<font>` runs keep theirs). Only the selected tab is used (one tab per window).
* **Title layout**: `Tab::Layout` **0x10145444** (called by `SetSelected` **0x10146110** and the frame-size thunk **0x10146baf**) places the text at left + 5 and top + `floor((tabPreferredHeight - textPreferredHeight)/2)`, clamping its width to the tab extent minus 5 and 16. The shared tab renderer now uses that vertical centre for window tabs, embedded tabs and drag ghosts. Regression: `tab_titles_use_native_vertical_centre_and_left_inset`.
* Multiple tabs, hover/drag and frame resizing are implemented in `gui/frame.rs`; the old unresolved summary predated them. The strip uses 17px art plus the native +1 extent transition (`FUN_10147c93`, `LayoutBorders` **0x10145be5**).
* Used by Planet Map, Playfield Map, Skills, Wear and Inventory; Inventory already uses the localized title from `GetText(10000,"Inventory")` (**0x100c9fb7**), not a style-1 fallback.

### 6.2 Border buttons, pin, `?`, hover fade (`gui/border.rs`)
Evidence (GUI.dll, decompiled this session):
* `BorderButton_c(a, b, c)` 0x10159ed2 = `Button_c` whose `SetGfx(0, a)`, `SetGfx(1, b)`, `SetGfx(2, c)` give the **raised / pressed / highlight** art
  (`Button_c::SetGfx` 0x10128270, `GetBorderView` 0x10128111: state 0 = `BorderView` +0x188 colour DEFAULT, **state 1 = +0x18c colour SELECTED (0xffffcc)**,
  **state 2 = +0x190 colour HOVER (0xa5ffdb)**, each at the layer-2 alpha 0.85). `StateChanged` 0x10128338: value true (pressed or toggled on) hides the raised view and shows
  the pressed one; `IsHighlighted` (pointer over) creates the highlight view *on top of* the raised one. So hover = `…_STATE3` overlay in HOVER colour, pressed/toggled =
  `…_STATE2` in SELECTED colour; `View::SetLocalAlpha(layer 1)` of the constructor only touches the button's own (empty) surfaces. Our old code drew `_STATE2` on hover, `_STATE3`
  pressed and everything at alpha 1.0; the three sprites are pixel-identical in this skin (md5 of the PNGs), so the visible effect is the tint and the 0.85 alpha.
* `CreateBorderIcons` 0x1015aba4: nothing for style ≥ 4 or `flags & 4`; else the icon button (`0x1c5` ×3, toggle, list 0), the close button (`0x1c0..0x1c2`, list 1,
  tooltip text 10000/0x2710), and, **only when window flag 0x800 is clear**, the pin button (`0x1cd..0x1cf` = `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_PIN*`, toggle, list 1, **no slot connected**).
  `WndBorder::SetHelpFile` 0x1015ae58 (called by `Window::SetHelpFile` 0x1015491a) adds, on its first call, the `?` button (`0x1d0..0x1d2` = `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_QUESTIONMARK*`, list 1,
  slot `SlotHelpButton` 0x10159e22) and stores the file name at `+0x1ec`; the click emits `GlobalSignals+0x188("file://" + file)` (the InfoView, docs/chat/dialogs.md).
  List 1 is laid out right to left in insertion order: **close, pin, `?`** — exactly the retail order `? 📌 X`. `Window::SetHelpFile` callers (strings): Skills "The Skill Window.html"
  (`FUN_100fc18e`), LFT "The LFT Window.html" (`FUN_100f03fb`), "The laser target window.html", "The Mech Select window.html", "The signup terminal window.html",
  "The Research window.html", "The teleport target window.html"; only the files present in `cd_image/text/help` are wired (Skills, LFT).
* `Layout` 0x1015a1d9 (decompiled, constants read from the PE: `_DAT_101a8b98` = float 5.0 = y, `_DAT_101a9da0` = **double** 5.0 = gap, `_DAT_101a87e8` = double 1.0): the four button
  lists (`+0x300` top-left, `+0x318` top-right, `+0x330` / `+0x348` bottom, empty for every window here) are laid out in the rectangle `View::GetBounds` 0x1014af45 returns
  (= frame minus its own top-left = `(0, 0, w, h)` of the `WndBorder` view = the **outer** window frame; the `TabView` is the child inset by the border sizes (3, 7, 3, 3) in `DoSetFrame` 0x10159888,
  the buttons are not). Top-left list: `acc += 5; Point(acc, 5); acc += w - 1`, i.e. the icon starts **5 px from the outer left edge, 5 px below the top**. Top-right list:
  `acc += 5; x = R - w - acc; acc += w - 1`: close ends 5 px before the outer right edge, pin and `?` follow leftwards. `w` is the `Point` extent of the 15 px sprite (14), which makes the pitch
  5 + 13 = 18 = the `n * 18` of `TabView::SetLeftMargin(n * 18 + 2)` in `AddBorderView` 0x1015aa95 (`_DAT_101b36c0` = 18.0, `_DAT_101a8b90` = 2.0); `+0x2ec = acc1 + acc2` is the min-width input of `DoSetFrame`.
  `TabView::SetRightMargin(acc2)` keeps the tabs clear of the right list. **Round 2 correction**: round 1 guessed a 3 px extra inset (icon / close 8 px from the outer edge, measured in the
  rescaled `ref.png`); the code gives 5 px with no inset, which is what `gui/border.rs::layout` now does. **Retail check (`ref.png` restored, crops of the Skills and chat windows)**: the `i` glyph starts ≈9 image px (≈8.4 retail px after the 0.937 scale) from the outer edge, but `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_ICON_I` is a 15×15 sprite whose opaque columns are 4..11 (measured from `Graphics.uvga`), so the sprite itself starts ≈5 px in: the round-1 "8 px" was the glyph, not the button. Code and capture agree. The first tab starts at outer x + 3 + 20 = 23, 3 px right of the icon's last column (x = 19).
* `WndBorder::GetPinButtonState` 0x10158ea0 / `SetPinButtonState` 0x1015af59 (sets the toggle, ticks the popup entry and `FadeTo(1.0, 0.2 s)`); used by `Window::_CanFade`
  0x10154885 (`flags & 0x800 == 0 && !pinned`) and `SaveWndConfig`; the port now restores and saves the pin in each plain-window archive or grouped dock configuration.
* **Hover fade** (`WindowController_c::HandleMouseMoved` 0x101583a0 → `FadeWindows` 0x1015803c): when the window under the pointer changes and no mouse button is held, the window
  left fades to `_DAT_101ae0ec` = **0.3** over 1 s (`0xf4240` µs) and the window entered to **1.0** over 0.2 s (`0x30d40` µs), each only if `_CanFade` and not modal. (Windows of the same
  `GetFadeGroup` skip it; no window sets one here.) Ported in `Gui::update_window_hover` / `tick_window_fades`; a window multiplies its frame art and content by the fade
  (`Gui::window_fade`). `DockWindow_c` windows (flags 0x1000, 0x800 clear) = every `open_tabbed_window*` window have the pin and take part; the chat windows opt out
  (`Gui::set_window_fade(w, false)`: they run their own `FadeTo` rule, docs/chat/gui.md §3). API: `set_window_pin_button`, `set_window_pinned`, `window_pinned`,
  `set_window_help`, `set_window_fade`, events `FramePin`, `FrameHelp`. Tests: `crates/ao-gui/tests/border.rs`, unit tests in `gui/border.rs`.
* `View::_CallRender` 0x1014d2e3 (alpha / colour model used throughout): the colour of a view's surfaces is `parent × MapColor(+0x190) × MapColor(+0x194)` channel-wise (`c·c'/255`),
  its alpha is `(+0x188 × inherited) × +0x18c` (children inherit only `+0x188 × inherited`).

### 6.3 Alpha blending space (`ao-render` `GuiRenderer`)
D3D7 has no sRGB framebuffer, so every GUI `SRCALPHA / INVSRCALPHA` blend works on the stored 8-bit values. The renderer used to draw into the sRGB target (linear blending: a dark
0.85-alpha window over a bright background came out much lighter than retail: our test body (25,49,65) used to be (47,71,93)). The GUI initially fixed this through a non-sRGB twin view.
World and GUI now share the primary UNORM framebuffer (`GuiRenderer::view` returns its default view), preserving straight-alpha display-space GUI blending with no shader decode or extra view format. Previously verified numerically: art
`GFX_GUI_TAB_BACKGROUND` (26,39,51) × DEFAULT (0x80e9f3) at 0.85 over the 0.33-black window (91,124,157) = (24.7,49,63.5); the shot pixel is (25,49,65).

### 6.5 Window state persistence (`play/hud_wincfg.rs`, `hud.rs`)
Evidence (GUI.dll): `Window::SaveWndConfig` 0x1548a7 = `Message::AddRect("WindowFrame", GetFrame)` + (flag 0x800 clear) `AddBool("WindowPinButtonState", GetPinButtonState)`; `Window::LoadWndConfig` 0x10154d6e = `FindRect("WindowFrame")` -> `WndBorder::SetClientFrame`, `MoveInsideScreen(false,true,true)` 0x10154abc (shifts the window so left/top, else right/bottom, is on screen; never resizes), `FindBool` -> `SetPinButtonState`. Callers of `LoadWndConfig` (31) name the store: plain windows keep a DValue archive (`SkillConfig` `FUN_100fc18e`, `PerkWindowConfig` `FUN_1006372d`, `CompassWindowConfig`, `OptionWindowConfig`, `LFTWindowConfig`, `NPCChatWindowConfig`, ...); every `DockableView_c` (inventory, team, NCU, missions, maps, faction, ...) lives in a `DockWindow_c` (`FUN_1003bdd4`, `LoadWndConfig` at 0x1003bf4f) whose dtor `FUN_1003b672` adds `selected_tab` (`GetTabSelection`) and calls `SaveWndConfig`; the `DockingController_c` (`FUN_1003a0a1` save, `FUN_1003a5e6` load, `FUN_1003a26f` names `DockArea%u`, `FUN_1003a005` = `GetCharPrefsPath() + "/DockAreas"`) writes `{dock_name, dock_type, dock_config}` per dock to `<char prefs>/DockAreas/<dock_name>.xml` (the shape of the shipped `prefs/NewChar/DockAreas/*.xml`, whose `docked_view_identities` is the dvalue name: `inventory_window`, `team_view`, `ncu_window`). The `*ViewConfig` / `*WindowConfig` archives of the dockable views (`PFMapWindowConfig`, `FactionWindowConfig`, `NCUWindowConfig`, ... refs in the view ctors/dtors) hold view state only, not the frame (the template's `NCUWindowConfig` is a `listview_config`). `CharBarWindow_c` / compass / shortcut-bar flags 0xe3c / 0xd3c / 0x183c disable **border** movement only: their child mouse handlers move the containing window directly.
* Ours: `WinCfgs` restores plain-window frame/pin archives and grouped `DockAreas` records; `Rollup` retains the owner's WindowId/view handles while moving content between the rollup and free/tabbed docks. All `Store::Dock` windows are movable/resizable, including Team; Inventory keeps its existing grid reflow. A sole dock title moves its frame, a multi-tab dock supports selection, reorder, tab tear-off, and drops into another dock. Rollup headers drag to reorder or undock, and a free tab dropped over the rollup docks there. Group snapshots include ordered identities, selected tab, frame, pin, and rollup page expansion/heights/scroll; empty former groups are saved so stale files cannot resurrect moved views. Writes occur after interaction and at logout, with foreign archive children retained. `Hud::prefs_loaded` re-applies the character's groups after open/closed DValue flags.
* Screen resize keeps a free dock touching the old right/bottom viewport edge attached to the corresponding new edge; interior windows are only shifted by `MoveInsideScreen`, never resized. This edge-attachment policy is a requested port behavior, not a separately established original-client resize formula. Team button-row changes relayout inside the existing frame; resize limits now follow content preferred sizes (§6.6), not the built template frame.
* Control-centre position archives are `CCHealthBarConfig`, `CCNanoBarConfig`, `CCXPBarConfig`, `CCAlienXPBarConfig`, `CCFriendlyHealthBarConfig`, `CCHostileHealthBarConfig`, and `CompassWindowConfig` (shipped `prefs/NewChar/Prefs.xml` archive names; compass destructor `FUN_1006d697`). Shortcut positions use character-owned `Containers/ShortcutBar_<n>.xml` (`ShortcutBarWindow_c` 0x100d94e9); shipped container children are `IsPrimary`, `position_map`, `selected_bar`, `bar_orientation`, and `WindowFrame`. `WinCfgs::attach_hud` reuses `Cfg` without adding framed-window chrome, restores only position for these preferred-size windows, and reloads after character preferences arrive. Saving merges `WindowFrame` into the existing archive/container, retaining unrelated XML children. Target placement and compass resize follow default anchors until the engine's sticky user-movement flag is set; restoring a saved position also sets it. Automatic layout changes do not create a saved user position. Regression checks `hud_wincfg::tests::{hotbar_frame_save_retains_container_fields,hud_defaults_are_not_saved_until_moved_and_reload_marks_saved_position}`, `hud_compass::tests::resize_keeps_moved_compass_and_repositions_default`, and `hud_target::tests::moved_target_bars_survive_updates_and_resize` were added but not executed by this worker.
* HUD movement is ordinary **left drag**, not Shift/Alt drag or a global GUI-unlock mode. `CharBarWindow_c` 0x1006d7c7 connects child down/up/move signals: 0x10067b8c tests button 1, captures and records mouse; 0x10067bca releases; 0x10067bf4 calls `Window::MoveBy` and `MoveInsideScreen`. This includes the target health windows constructed as `CharBarWindow_c` by 0x100746ec. `CompassView_c` vtable mouse slots 15/16/17 point to 0x10066fc4 / 0x10066ff6 / 0x10067000, the same button-1 capture and containing-window `MoveBy` path. `ShortcutBarSpinner_c` 0x100db4d6 captures button 1; 0x100db335 moves only while `LockHotbars` is false; 0x100db319 releases. Its arrow buttons retain paging, and slots retain item interaction. `WndBorder::HitTest` 0x101593d6 never sees these view-owned gestures. The port's frameless move regions reuse engine frame capture without adding invented title/border chrome; only actual user movement sets the persistence flag.
* Target bars now remeasure their preferred window size when the live target rows/width change, rather than merely relayout inside the constructor's two-row 64 px frame. Keeping that stale frame vertically centred a single visible row below the native y=5 origin and clipped wider target bars; placement and the movable hit region now follow the measured content. `target_bar_window_adopts_live_width_and_visible_rows` covers width containment and one/two-row transitions.
* The dock wrapper's stacked body must extend its maximum size in both axes, not only specify its minimum height. Otherwise `View::CalculatePreferredSize` for a no-layout-node view makes its maximum width zero and its maximum height fixed; the surrounding vertical layout then collapses the owner horizontally and centres it vertically on resize. The parent-observed 1280×800 Team screenshot exposed this shared wrapper bug (rows/bars left of the frame). `hud_team::tests::opens_inside_the_screen` now covers real Team registration, full client-body width/height, and row containment before/after growing both axes; this author did not run it.
* Dock-drag evidence: `RollupController` 0x10048d3f creates `dockableview/view` with `dock_name`, `view_id`, `view_index`; drop completion 0x1003a82c creates a free `DockWindow`. The grouping/persistence evidence is `DockWindow` 0x1003bdd4 / dtor 0x1003b672 and `DockingController` 0x1003a0a1 / 0x1003a5e6. Frame movement/resize follows `WndBorder::MouseMove` 0x10159c27 / `DoSetFrame` 0x10159888 (the opposite resized edge stays fixed).
* Transient TradeView docking uses the original empty view identity, not an invented persistent key: `Hud::register_transient_dock` preserves owner handles, appends it in RollupArea and forwards its header close to the shop owner for abort. Empty identities/page nodes are excluded from snapshots; a selected transient tab saves the existing `-1` sentinel, and empty former dock records still clear stale members. Reopening starts in RollupArea rather than restoring a transient free dock. Evidence: `FUN_10038c98` adds at index `-1`; `FUN_100392a0` resolves an unknown identity to append; `FUN_10049897` lays out then invokes the reveal virtual. Rollup's DockArea vtable `0x101b0da8 + 0x10` points to `FUN_10048996`, which scrolls the new page's top/bottom into the area. Regression `hud_rollup::docks::tests::transient_trade_docks_without_archiving_an_identity` covers overflow reveal, close identity, grouping/selection snapshot filtering, and reopening. No builds/tests/live checks were run by this author.
  Registration must apply `restore_docks(gui, &[])` before reveal, as the persistent external-view API already does: it assigns Rollup membership and replaces the free frame with page header/body geometry. Merely laying out a freshly registered page leaves its initial `docked=false` state untouched. The parent-exercised transient regression exposed this missing transition; the assertion was retained and the shared transition reused without a worker rerun.
  The subsequent reveal failure came from `WindowSize::Preferred` requesting the maximum preferred extent (`layout::pref(..., true)`): the owner wrapper's elastic `max_size` made it 16000 pixels tall. Shared page sizing now temporarily bounds the wrapped body to its `PageConfig.height` for preferred sizing, then restores the elastic range for free-window resize. Dock, undock, expansion and restore use this same transition; the transient regression retains its reveal assertion and additionally checks the undocked body grows with its free frame.
  Width follows the same shared transition: a docked page always remains 192 px (`InitialiseMessage` 0x1006a968, inclusive `Rect(W−191,20,W,H−225)`); an undocked page retains its current client width. Previously only initial docking/restore corrected the preferred width, so a header collapse/expand requested the wrapper/header's 16000 px maximum and broke Team's row layout. `hud_rollup::docks::tests::wrapped_dock_toggle_keeps_column_width_and_owner_rows` covers repeated toggle and undock; not executed by this worker. The supplied `pasted-image-a330c71f6923c46a.png` shows the damaged Team header/rows below Stats; `pasted-image-ba7bc439cfc00e5e.png` establishes Wear's `i`, title, cream collapse and cyan close header art.
* Regression checks: `hud_rollup::docks::tests::{dock_tabs_undock_restore_and_keep_owner_handles,screen_resize_keeps_free_dock_bottom_right_anchor,single_dock_title_drag_and_border_resize_dispatch,template_dock_names_and_negative_selection_survive_lazy_open}`, `hud_wincfg::tests`, `hud::tests::dragged_windows_are_saved_and_restored`, and `hud_stats::tests::wear_and_inventory_show_items` (all four tab hit areas share a row and stay inside the Wear body). Templates reserve their dock names even before their windows open; a frame-less template state initializes its frame from the module on opening, and negative `selected_tab` sentinels remain intact until selection changes. These checks are runnable; this edit's author did not execute builds/tests/live checks.
* External dock registration is idempotent by `WindowId`: `flow.rs` enumerates chat-owned windows every frame, so `Hud::register_dock` restores membership only on first registration, not over a live drag/resize/pin change. Character RollupArea archives may omit already registered pages; shared wrapped-page sizing then retains the current window content dimensions instead of requiring a `PageConfig` or recomputing preferred size from the elastic wrapper. This preserves height with either visible or hidden rollup headers and leaves the elastic body range intact. Regression checks: `hud::tests::repeated_external_dock_registration_preserves_live_frame` and `hud_rollup::docks::tests::user_config_can_omit_a_registered_wrapped_page` (not executed by this edit's author).

### 6.6 HUD resize policy and content limits

GUI.dll `Window::Window` **0x101563ff** maps window flag **0x1000** to root View flag **2**; `WndBorder` **0x1015b44b** passes that flag to its BorderView and calls `SetClientFrame` with the constructor rectangle. `View::_Attached` **0x1014f10e** installs `GetPreferredSize(false)` / `GetPreferredSize(true)` as the window limits when internal flags `+0x19c & 2` is set. `SendPreferredSizeChangedNotification` **0x1014b649** repeats this with cached minimum/maximum at `+0x44/+0x4c`; `Window::SetSizeLimits` **0x10154288** forwards to `WndBorder::SetSizeLimits` **0x1015902f**. Thus a constructor rectangle, saved frame, and content preferred limits are three different things.

`WndBorder::SetSizeLimits` **0x1015902f** does not merely store the range: after updating minimum/maximum it routes the existing frame through virtual `SetFrame` (`vtable+0xf0`), immediately reclamping and relaying out current content. This matters when a dynamically populated Perks grid needs more space than the constructor rectangle: recording limits only for the next mouse resize left the first layout squeezed. The engine applies updated limits to the existing frame, preserving its top-left; no Perks-only resize workaround is used.

`Gui::set_window_preferred_size_limits(w, true)` opts into this content-derived policy; resize/relayout refresh the limits. `window_preferred_size_limits` exposes the measured client range. Chrome and the live button-row floor are applied separately (`Layout` **0x1015a1d9**, clamp **0x10159888**). Storage type does not determine resize eligibility: Skills/Pet/Perks now restore size as well as position from their value archives. Team's template frame no longer becomes a permanent minimum; Options no longer invents 200×120 or 120px outer floors. Normal chat has flags **0**, not 0x1000, and therefore retains default zero client limits rather than the guessed 50×60 floor.

Perks enables preferred limits in its constructor as well as the shared persistence attachment, so standalone opening and later population cannot squeeze the fixed rank grid below its native preferred range. Zero-client chat sizing is preserved end-to-end through frame application and restore; a zero-height client leaves31px of style-0 chrome, not an invented one-pixel client floor. `CanvasView` now loads declared children and layout using the common `View::LoadChildViews` path, like the existing BitmapView wrapper; Team's custom-painted highlight can therefore contain the native horizontal text/spacers and vertical health/nano children instead of silently discarding them.

Initial placement uses final preferred-clamped dimensions before `MoveInsideScreen` (Team), not the unclamped template height. Options completes population and the native category-width cap before installing preferred limits and loading the requested frame; temporary uncapped category width must not enlarge a restored window. Dock geometry refresh records automatic content-clamped frames without marking the controller user-dirty; explicit frame/pin/tab gestures retain the existing dirty/save paths. No initialization marker or deferred-baseline workaround is retained. Options restore regression checks both the saved archive bounds and the reopened preferred-constrained frame.

The free dock wrapper measures its preserved owner content through a layout-bearing elastic body instead of freezing the current client height as a minimum. The removed floor also mixed pixel count (`window_size`) with inclusive extent (`min_size`), adding one pixel on every restored registration. Saved frames remain independent of content limits; the existing full HUD resize/restore regression checks exact dimensions across a fresh login.

Inventory below covers all 21 current `WindowKind::ALL` entries plus separately owned HUD surfaces. “Content-derived” specifies the established retail rule, not a claim that every reconstructed view's pixel metrics are already exact.

| LFT / private tell | `chat/social_win.rs`; LFT **0x100f03fb**, flags0x1000 | LFT uses populated `LFTView.xml` preferred bounds; `MultiListView` **0x1013314d** adds the19px header to its scrolling rows; description extents80..500 wide. Private-tell numerical ranges remain outside this audit | Social owner archives |
| Health / Nano / XP / Alien XP bars | `CharBarWindow` **0x1006d7c7** | Preferred artwork; left-drag only, no scaling | `CC*BarConfig`, position |
| Compass | `CompassView` **0x10066fc4/10067000**, 137×22 preferred | Preferred artwork; left-drag only | `CompassWindowConfig`, position |
| Shortcut bars | `ShortcutBarWindow` **0x100d94e9**, spinner drag **0x100db335** | Preferred slot count/orientation; movement gated by `LockHotbars`, not free-form scaling | `Containers/ShortcutBar_N.xml`, position/orientation/selected bar |
| Trade / shop and transient panels | Empty identity **0x10038c98**, free dock **0x1003a82c**; Trade list setup **0x100dfeca/100e0053/100e0211**, grid conversion **0x101331dc/10133255** | Inherited 48px icons: ctor **0x10136423** writes extent47 from **0x101b0288** to +180; spacing10 **0x101a98e4**, borders6 **0x101b5234**. One cell **60×60px**, 50 cells **2902px**; 54px slot art does not replace the 58px cell pitch. `SetViewCellCounts` **0x10132d68** clamps requested1000 to50. Stock/partner max50×50, offered/bought max50×1; vertical bar reserves11px, horizontal disabled. Shipped TradeGUI.xml contributes enclosing labels/buttons/borders. Grid reflows on resize and scrolls overflow; list mode retains header/content constraints. NPC **0x100575a1** fixes counts to `((max!=1)+1,(max+1)/2)` with both scrollbars disabled. Regression: `trade_native_trays_resize_reflow_and_scroll_without_a_fixed_column_count`, `shop_native_stock_and_offer_preferred_limits_follow_mode_and_width`, `container_grid_follows_the_item_count` | Transient identities excluded from persistent dock snapshots |

Trade's partner tray retains its 50-row preferred ceiling even when empty: the inner canvas advertises that maximum separately from current painted content height. Offered items retain their one-row viewport and use the scroll child for overflow. The resize regression asserts both behaviors.
Trade and shop CanvasWheel adapters forward the ordinary three×13px wheel delta to their enclosing ScrollView through the existing clamped offset API; canvases emit application events before generic ancestor-wheel handling. The trade overflow regression and `shop_grid_wheel_reaches_its_enclosing_scroll_view` dispatch emitted events through the live owner adapters and assert nonzero scroll offsets.

`ScrollingTextView` must not inherit a TextView's natural-width limit: native preferred callback **0x10008fd2** returns x=`-1` for minimum and x=`16000` for maximum (`0x101a913c`), with y cached from its empty child TextView by XML constructor **0x10008d03 → 0x10008aab**. This keeps the trade owner's range independent of partner/shop name length. Regression `scrolling_title_preferred_width_is_independent_of_assigned_name` covers short/long assigned titles. Timed scrolling animation remains separately unported; this fix does not claim that animation.

**Resize hit-test correction (Bug 6).** Fresh `re.sh GUI DumpFn.java 0x101593d6` shows that **all eight** edge/corner hit regions require `(Window::GetFlags() & 0x30) == 0`. Neither 0x10 nor 0x20 independently enables a resize axis. `DockWindow` **0x1003bdd4** passes flags **0x1000**, so apparent vertical-only behavior must be explained by content min/max, not by adding a per-axis frame API. The engine comment now matches that trace.

**Width-constraint audit.** Inventory, NCU, Missions, Actions, Programs and Friends have elastic scrolling/list viewport maxima; list *columns* and individual icon cells remain fixed-sized, while grids reflow with available width. Team's horizontal spacers and elastic header/separators permit width growth. Skills, Options, Research, Vehicle and Raid use their shipped view hierarchies; fixed child labels/art and category/button groups still contribute preferred bounds. Perks' **0x100611dc** puts natural-width TextView headings above each scroll/rank column, while **0x1005e4b1/1005ec59** preserves fixed rank cells: a scroll child alone does not imply an unlimited column width. Genuine fixed-content owners are Wear, Stat and Faction; Pet has the explicit dynamic width ceiling above. Target, health/nano/XP bars, Compass and shortcuts are movable artwork surfaces, not free-resize windows. Chat modes 1/2 disable every border resize hit; normal chat remains unconstrained by preferred content. Trade/shop limits remain owner-specific and unresolved rather than being declared elastic solely because they are dockable.

**Removed port-only constraints.** `Rollup::open_page` had retained its 192px column and archived page-height overrides after undocking, including during restored free-dock activation. Free pages now clear those wrapper overrides and keep the underlying owner's preferred range; docking reinstalls column/page dimensions. The page body is a layout-bearing CanvasView: its single owner child supplies preferred bounds, and the same canvas paints the docked page background. A no-layout `stacked` body cannot measure its owner; merely clearing its overrides collapsed freed pages to zero height. Native `RollupPage` **0x10049389/100490e4** lays its child into current page bounds; free docking **0x1003a82c/1003bdd4** uses a DockWindow, not the archived RollupPage size. Both map canvases now allow viewport growth, retaining constructor dimensions only as the port's existing minimum pending a complete native numerical-limit trace.

New targeted checks (not run by this worker): `hud_rollup::docks::tests::free_page_releases_column_limits_but_keeps_owner_width_constraints` covers elastic and genuinely fixed-width owners across free/dock/restore; `hud_map::tests::map_viewports_grow_in_both_axes_and_buttons_follow_width` checks viewport growth, fixed button height and live-width button hit regions. No build, test, formatter or real-window run was performed.

Regression additions: `hud_wincfg::tests::plain_window_resize_restores_size_and_relayouts_content` covers all three value-backed windows; chat's existing border/menu regression covers shrinking below the removed floor and restoring that size. Existing Team containment and dock/undock regressions exercise the preferred-range transition. Perks checks selected-rank column placement and unchanged rank dimensions after growing the window. Engine regressions cover root preferred min/max refresh, zero-client frame application and CanvasView child layout. This policy worker ran no checks. **Post-fix real-window acceptance was not performed:** the user is using the Mac, so no application windows or synthetic input were opened; these source regressions are not visual retail-parity evidence.

## 7. Widgets (`gui.rs`)

* **Button** (`Button_c`): raised border set 0x1e..0x26 (`GFX_GUI_BORDER01_*`, colour DEFAULT), pressed set 0x2f..0x37 (colour SELECTED),
  hover overlay 0x27..0x2e (colour HOVER) – `Initialize` 0x10128994 / `StateChanged` 0x10128338; layer-2 alpha 0.85; disabled tint 0x909090
  (enabled 0xffffff); label centred (`Rect::TranslateCenter`). Click = release inside; `Event::Clicked{window, view, item}`.
* **TextButton** (`CharacterSelectionItem` `name_btn`): text drawn in `color`, `hover_color` while hovered, `pressed_color` while pressed **or toggled on**;
  `ButtonBase_c::SetToggleButton` → click switches it on. `Gui::set_item_selected(h, bool)` mirrors `CharSelectItem_c::SetSelected` 0x1000cb1a
  (toggle + `detailed_view` instead of `summary_view`; the client swaps them in the row container, we collapse the hidden one).
  `set_toggle_in`, `set_color_in` (status colour 0xEE4444).
* **TextInputView / ComboBox editor**: caret (blink), selection (shift+arrows, mouse drag), Home/End, Delete/Backspace. Native `TextRenderer_c::MouseDown` **0x101637ef** always places the caret/begins selection on left-down; it has no double-click word-selection branch, so none is added;
  Ctrl+A/C/X/V (events `Copy(text)` / `PasteRequested` → `InputEvent::Paste`), password mask `*`, horizontal scrolling to keep the caret visible,
  click-to-focus + caret placement, `TextChanged`, `EnterPressed`. **Tab / Shift-Tab** cycles the enabled, visible text-input fields of the top
  windows in tree order (original `TextView_c::SlotTabPressed`; buttons are not tab stops – **[INFERENCE]**, no focus ring exists in the art);
  Enter activates the window default button (`set_default_button`, `Window::SetDefaultButton`); Escape → `Event::Escape`.
* **ComboBox** (`ComboBox_c`, editable): clicking the editor opens the popup (`ComboBox_c::MouseDown` 0x10001ec0) with the items below the field (arrow
  glyph changes while open), picking sets the editor text and emits `ComboChanged`; a click elsewhere or any key closes the popup and is processed
  normally. Items via `combo_set_items`, `combo_selected`. Removing an entry is application logic (the `Remove` button of LoginWindow).
  `PopupMenu_c::Go` **0x1013bf93** builds a style-3 borderless Window containing BorderView skin **f9/fb/f4/f6/f7/fa/f8/f5/1bf** (WINDOW border/background), with white tint and alpha1 (View extra defaults **0x1014fa1e**, no popup override). Both ComboBox and generic menus now share that skin; `Highlight` **0x1013dbc0** uses opaque **2020c0**, disabled labels **0x1013e730** use **a0a0a0**, and right-tail check/submenu art comes from **117/118** (**0x1013d8bd/1013dd2b**), not squares/ASCII. Regression: `popup_uses_native_border_and_opaque_blue_highlight`. Remaining menu geometry/slider reconstruction is not certified by this skin correction.
* **ScrollView** (`characters_view`): vertical auto scrollbar (`GFX_GUI_SCROLLBAR_GRAY_*`, 10 px extent) only when the content exceeds the view;
  wheel, thumb drag, track click paging; contents clipped.
* **PowerBar** (`ProgressDialog`, `LimboWindow`): `bg_gfx` stretched, `full_gfx` clipped to `value` (`set_progress`, 0..1).
* **BitmapView, CheckBox, RadioButton(Group)**: static/basic; not used by the login flow.
* Images: `add_image(name, rgba, w, h, smooth)` for runtime textures (e.g. the loading screen).

## 8. Application API summary

`Gui::new(client_dir, localize)`, `open_window` / `open_framed_window(view, pos, WindowSize::{Preferred,Fixed})`, `frame(dt) -> DrawList`,
`input(ev) -> Vec<Event>`, text/visibility/enable setters by name (`set_text`, `set_visible`, `set_enabled`, `set_progress`, `focus`),
instance API for repeated rows (`add_view`, `remove_children`, `*_in(handle, name, …)` – **every view of that name in the instance** is affected),
`wants_mouse` (does the GUI own this pointer position), `resize_window`, `relayout_window`.
Events: `Clicked`, `TextChanged`, `EnterPressed`, `ComboChanged`, `Copy`, `PasteRequested`, `Escape`, `CloseRequested`.
Pointer ownership includes the whole visible framed-window outer rectangle (empty client space and root TextViews included), matching frame/context dispatch, and remains captured during engine frame/tab/menu interactions. Unframed transparent root views still do not capture the full screen. The parent-observed Brandon right-click at `(596,384)` opened both NPC dialogue and Chat's context menu because the old `covers` path excluded Chat's root TextView while context dispatch accepted its frame. Regression `ao-gui/tests/frame.rs::framed_root_text_right_click_captures_before_context_dispatch` covers that coordinate, the bottom/title frame, hidden windows, transparent roots, and capture during a frame drag. This author did not run it; chat `is_clickthrough` remains stored-only (no pre-existing engine routing was changed).
Raw inventory/wear, Programs, Actions and shortcut-bar press initiation uses `Gui::pointer_owner`, the same clipped window z-order and per-window interactive/framed/move-region/cover rules as `wants_mouse`; an empty framed Chat above an item cannot inspect, activate or arm a drag beneath it. Popup presses have no underlying window owner. Existing captured drag movement/releases remain independent of press ownership. Regression: `hud_stats::tests::item_inspection_and_right_activation_inventory_and_wear` adds an empty framed Chat covering a filled inventory cell and asserts ordinary/qualified/right presses produce no item effects. This regression is added but not run here; the parent observed the raw right-click leak during offscreen item acceptance.

## 9. UNRESOLVED (summary)

GDI rasterizer/dropout byte parity (§2), IME, border-button row origin (3px screenshot inference, §6.2), and palette/skin of the older retail build (§6.4) remain unresolved.
NewChar template bootstrap has no established native consumer (§6.4); see the searched preferences routines and missing first-launch provenance there.
Tabs, tab title centering (§6.1), window drag/resize and pin archives (§6.5), PopupMenu art (§7), tooltips (§10), `_AddLineDesc` pitch (§3), and rollup expanded/toggle mapping (§6.4) are implemented; the old summary predated these paths.
Double-click word selection is absent in the native text input handler, not an unimplemented retail feature.

## 11. Stat tables and the skills / inventory / wear windows (`ao_formats::stats`, `play/hud_stats.rs`)

Evidence: `GUI.dll` (GUI) and `Gamecode.dll` (GC) addresses, decoded with Ghidra headless (`/tmp/aomac-ghidra/dsky/gui` = GUI.dll, `/tmp/aomac-ghidra/proto` = Gamecode.dll);
anything not read from the binaries is marked **UNRESOLVED**.

### 11.1 Stat id → name table (`crates/ao-formats/data/stat_names.txt`, `stats::name`)
`n3EngineClientAnarchy_t::N3Msg_GetStatNameMap` [GC 0x10027483] is a `std::map<int, const char*>` filled by **`FUN_1002f009` [GC 0x1002f009–0x100321a9]** as a straight line of
`MOV [EBP-4], id` / `CALL 0x1008a3e8` (map `operator[]`) / `MOV [EAX], &name` triples (a few ids are cleared with `AND [EBP-4], 0`, the store of the string then follows
the *next* instruction; the extraction attributes the string to the id live at the `CALL`). `FUN_1003227a`, `FUN_100324d2`, `FUN_10035c99` are only the readers (`fStatToString`),
not table builders. Result: **523 inserts, 521 distinct ids** (id 0x85 `LR_EnergyWeapon` and 0x2b2 `EquippedRHWeapon` are inserted twice with the same name), max id 1002; earlier docs said 524.
Corrections to earlier notes: id **0 = `Flags`** and id **26 = `Energy`** (docs/zone/world.md listed "0 Energy").
The file (9 KB, id + internal name, no game art) is committed; the extraction script (`StatTab.java`) walks the Ghidra function body (not committed, ~20 lines).
Display strings come from `text.mdb` (`TextDb::by_id(cat, stat)`): **2000** internal CamelCase, **2001** description (shown in the skill details), **2002** long name ("Body Development"),
**2003** short name ("Body Dev.", "Matt.Metam", "Time&Space") – the `StatRow` ctor `FUN_100fe956` reads 2003 (`LDBface::GetText(0x7d3, stat)`); category 10010 = skill group labels
(`#10010:n` in `Skills.xml`, now resolved by `TextDb::label`: `#<category>:<id>` = `GetText(category, id)`).

### 11.2 Own stats in `Zone` (`play/zone.rs`)
`Zone.stats: HashMap<u32,i32>` is filled from the own `FullCharacterIIR_t` raw groups `stats_a`, `stats_b`, `stats_u8` and `stats_i16` (skipping the `0x499602D2` marker).
It is updated by every own `StatIIR_t` (`who.instance == char_id`); other dynels' `StatIIR` never touch it. Test `own_stats_from_full_character_and_stat_deltas` (capture `zone_newchar_ithaca.rec`:
Level 1, IP 1500, Cash 1000, 6 in every ability, 5 in every skill).
`FullCharacter.stat_map` is retained separately in `Zone.stat_adjustments`: Gamecode `0x10073a2f`
passes the message's `+44` map to `0x1006416c`, replacing modifier-object `+8`, not the raw stat array.
`Zone.skill_values` is the shared mode-2 projection; `Zone.stat` remains raw to avoid applying trickle-down twice.

### 11.3 Skills window (`Views/Skills.xml`, `SkillWindow` ctor `FUN_100fc18e` GUI 0x100fc18e)
* Window: `Window(Rect(200,180)..(850,700), "", "Skills", style 0, flags 0x1000)`, one tab "Skills", help file "The Skill Window.html", loads `%sViews/Skills.xml`. `WndBorder` interprets the constructor rectangle as a651×521 **client** frame; the port uses the style-0 frame/tab strip and content preferred limits (§6.6), with full frame persistence.
* Groups `FUN_100fb596` [GUI 0x100fb596]: 11 groups (`abilities body meleew melees rangedw rangeds nanocast exploring combatheal traderepair disabled`, text `10010:0..9, 9999`) and the 75 skill
  ids pushed per group (`FUN_100ff558(11)`, then `FUN_10064bee` with ECX = `window+0x8c + 0x10·group`; decoded from the asm, `stats::SKILL_GROUPS`; 6+7+10+5+11+6+7+5+6+10+2 = 75, each once).
  The skill names equal the `ipdist.xml` stat names, except `ipdist` still says "Parry" for id 145 "Deflect".
* Rows: class `StatRow` (`FUN_100fe956`): a `ButtonBase` over a `BorderView` (borders hidden) holding name `TextView` (border left 3), spacer, value `TextView`; the *info* rows (flag 1) add a `PowerbarView`
  (`GFX_GUI_HOR_BAR_SMALL_EMPTY/BLUE` = ids 0xe0/0xe1) and two arrow `Button_c`; value = `N3Msg_GetSkill(stat, 2)`. Per group two row sets are created: `<g>_view` rows (compact/"minimised" mode, accordion
  via the group button) and `<g>_group` rows (normal mode). `FUN_100f9552`: in normal mode a group button sets `infoselect` = 1 and `groupselect` = group index (right panel shows the rows);
  `Minimized` mode toggles the `<g>_view` instead. We build the `_group` rows (name button + value text) as XML at run time (`Gui::add_view_xml`).
* Other code: remaining IP `remaining_ip` = `N3Msg_GetSkill(0x35, 0)` (`FUN_100f96c2`); "Reset all skills (n)" `FUN_100fa45c`: `n = (GetSkill(0x15c) & 4 == 0) + GetSkill(0x2b3 FullIPRPoints)`, label
  text.mdb 502/96620988 ("Reset all skills") + " (" n ")", enabled iff n ≥ 1; "Suggested IP distribution" (`suggest_ip`, handler `FUN_100faec0` → `FUN_100fac49`, `RecommendIPUse` `FUN_100f8d69` loads `%sdata/ipdist.xml`) is
  available until level 20 (inittext). `ipdist.xml` = 14 professions × 75 stats `{levelrange min max pri 0..3}`; parser `stats::parse_ipdist` (tested against the client file).
* **N3Msg_GetSkill(stat, mode)** [GC 0x10026d66]: mode 0 (and unknown) → stat object `vtable+0x3c(stat, 2)`; 1 → `FUN_100654e1`; 2 → `FUN_1006554c(stat, 1, 0)`; 3 → `FUN_10064800`; 4 → `FUN_1006554c(stat, 1, 1)`.
  `GetSkillMax` `FUN_100651b5`, `GetSkillCost` `FUN_10061fdb` (float), `GetSkillCostLevel` `FUN_10062398`; they depend on profession (stat 0x3c), level (0x36), breed and `GameData` cost tables (`FUN_1013ecf0`,
  `FUN_100c4ab1`, `FUN_100c499b`) – **not traced**.
* Implemented: all of `Skills.xml` with the client skin, group labels, rows with live values from `Zone.stats` (test drives an own `StatIIR`: Strength 6 → 15 and IP 1500 → 1490 change the labels; a
  foreign dynel's `StatIIR` does not), group selection, row click → details panel (`statName` long name, `statdesc` text 2001, `base`; frame close and `Close`.
* **Now implemented** (§11.10): per-row power bars and arrow buttons with pending IP, "Save Changes", "Reset all skills" / "Reset this skill" (with confirmation), "Suggested IP distribution", the
  details panel numbers (base / buffed / maximum / delta / unit cost / total cost). **Still UNRESOLVED**: (the buffed value is done, §11.14), the hold-to-repeat timers and modifier-key bulk
  clicks of the arrows, the `SkillMaxFeedback` texts, the compact `Minimized` mode (`<g>_view` rows), window position persistence (`LoadWndConfig`).

### 11.4 Wear window (`WearView_c` `FUN_100e1bc9` GUI 0x100e1bc9, window name `wear_window`, title text.mdb 10000 "Wear")
A `TabView` with four `MultiListView_c` item grids built by `FUN_100cc1b3` (`InventoryViewBase_c`, base `FUN_100cdb3a`). A fresh read-only GUI.dll trace identifies the exact category-10000 title keys: `Weapon` at 0x100e2640, `Tab_Clothing` at 0x100e269c, `Tab_Implants` at 0x100e26f2, followed by literal `Social` at 0x100e2748; all four are appended to the same `TabView`. Every grid is `SetViewCellCounts(3,5)`, `SetGridIconSpacing(11, 9)` (floats 0x101bee04 / 0x101bfc88), with background `GFX_GUI_WEARVIEW_BG1` (0x1b4) / `BG2` (0x1b5, implants) and slot
frames are 54×54; `UpdateBackgroundSurfaces` `10134023` chooses `0x11d + (label count != 0)`, hence labelled Wear grids use `SLOT_48_OPEN` (0x11e), not CLOSED. Slot tables (static initialisers `101a5464/101a5684/101a5860/101a5a80`, entries `{id, IPoint(x,y)}`): armor 15 cells row-major `1..15`
(`100+id-1` = text.mdb 505 "Neck, Head, Back, Right Shoulder, Chest, Left Shoulder, Right Arm, Hands, Left Arm, Right Wrist, Legs, Left Wrist, Right Finger, Feet, Left Finger"), implants 13 cells (`200..212`,
last at (1,4) = Feet), weapons 15 cells (`300..314` Hud 1-3, Utils 1-3, Right Hand, Deck, Left Hand, Deck 1-6). Social is row-major `1..15` (`101a5a80`), using clothing label keys except slots13/15: `100e22aa..100e22c7` selects weapon keys306/308.
The port uses the actual **178×288 BG1/BG2** assets (Graphics.uvgi entries at offsets1116264/1157831; PNG IHDR178×288), at native size (`SetBackgroundBitmap` `1013305e`), with separate slot surfaces, text labels and item art. No composite WEAPON/CLOTHING/IMPLANTS skin or baked tab strip remains. Native 6px borders and 48px cells with spacing11×9 give pitches59×57; the 54px frames start at `(3+59c,3+57r)` in the body. All four tabs occupy the same 17px generic TabView strip above it; total305px. Existing `Gui::set_view_tabs` preserves native equal-weight tabs, 2px margin and 6px overlap.
`LabelsOnTop` is the original category10000 menu entry (`100e17e7`); toggling it (`100e1397`) affects all four tabs. `SetGridLabelsOnTop` `101338f9` uses white front labels or 0xc0c0c0 back labels; `SetGridLabel` `101360fd` uses font5 and feature flags0x1020. `WearViewConfig.labels_on_top` defaults true and is retained in its existing archive, matching `100e13f1`; the existing tab clicks select background, label and inventory-slot mapping together. Tooltips retain the shared full formatter and ItemDescriptionTooltips option.

### 11.5 Inventory window (`InventoryView_c` `FUN_100cc2ca` GUI 0x100cc2ca, `inventory_window`)
`FUN_100cc2ca(type 0)` reads `inventory_window` window config + `item_position_map` (slots `i` → `IPoint(i%3, i/3)`, 21 positions), builds `FUN_100cc1b3 → ItemContainerView_c` (`FUN_100cdb3a`): a
`MultiListView_c` with columns Icon / Name / Count (the `InventoryViewMode` pref selects list vs grid), `SetMaxItemCount(0x1e)` = **30 slots** for the character's own inventory (`0x15`/`100`/`0x66`
for container / bank / reclaim kinds). The port draws 30 empty CLOSED slot frames (there are no grid labels in Inventory). Native constructor `10136423` defaults icon47, border6 and spacing10 on both axes; flags0 from `100cc2ca`→`100cc1b3` leave horizontal fill active in `101349f6`. Three columns follow from the default viewport; rows are `ceil(30/columns)`, not an invented fixed3×10 grid.
Vertical spacing is10 (not the Wear-specific9), and content bounds are `2*6 + rows*48 + (rows-1)*10`; ten rows require582px. This does not change published Trade/Shop/NPC48px cells, Actions32px cells, or the separate bank168px viewport handoff.

### 11.6 Engine additions (ao-gui)
`ViewSelector` (children share the bounds, `Gui::select_child(w, name, Some(i))` shows one and collapses the rest), `Gui::show_collapsing`, `Gui::add_view_xml`, **width groups**
(`width_group` / `width_group_owner`: every member under the owner ancestor gets the widest preferred width of the group; `layout::group_width`), a `ScrollViewChild` with several children
(the skill window makes the child itself the scroll client, `FUN_100fc18e`: implicit inner view), XML numeric character references (`&#8216;`), `TextDb::label("#cat:id")`.
Word-wrapped text sizes itself from its current frame, so the skills window is laid out twice after opening.

### 11.7 Reproduce
`AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/x cargo test --release -p aomac hud_stats` writes `skills-0-initial/1-abilities/2-after-delta/3-nano.png`, `wear-0..2-*.png`, `inventory-wear-both.png`
(real client XML + skin, stats from the `zone_newchar_ithaca.rec` capture). Other stat users: the HUD bars (`play/hud.rs`), docs/zone.md §6.

## 10. In-world control centre (`play/hud.rs`, `play/hud_bar.rs`, `ao-gui` `expr.rs` / `gui/cc.rs`)

Evidence (GUI.dll, project copy of `/tmp/aomac-ghidra/dsky/gui`):
* `ControlCenterModule_c` ctor 0x1006c64d; `LoadMainConfig` 0x1006c371 loads `Variables.xml`, `MainPrefs.xml`, `LoginPrefs.xml`, `CharPrefs.xml` (defaults of every `dvalue:cc_*`: all true except `cc_mini_toolbar`; `NumHotbars` 1; windows false) then the user prefs. `SlotPlayerCharacterAlive` 0x1006afed creates the bars once the character is alive.
* `FUN_1006f098` (`ControlCenterWindow_c`) loads `Views/ControlCenter.xml`, finds the docks by name and fills them: Left/RightWingDock = `CollapsingBitmapView_c` (`GFX_GUI_CONTROLCENTER_WING_LEFT` 0xb5 / `_RIGHT`), LeftBarDock = `LeftBarView_c` 0x1006f7c5 (bg `BOTTOM_LEFT` 0x99, HLayout `NCU`, `used/max` = stats 0xb4/0xb5, spacer, `CRED`, cash = stat 0x3d via `FormatNumeric`), RightBarDock = `RightBarView_c` 0x1006ff09 (`BOTTOM_RIGHT` 0x9a, `DEF`, `Slider_c` AGGDEF −100..100, `AGG`), Left/RightTargetCtrlDock = `CCTargetControl_c` 0x100746ec (HudTarget), RollupControllerDock = `RollupArea` `DockArea_c` (`InitialiseMessage` 0x1006a968: Rect(W−225, 20, W, H−191)). `FUN_1006ee1e` shows each view whose `activate_criteria` holds (`View::Show(CriteriaMonitor_c::Evaluate())`) → `Gui::apply_criteria` + `ao_gui::expr` (`dvalue:`, `stat:`/`s:`, `id:`, `&& || ! == != < > <= >= & | + -`).

Values: health stat 27/1 (0x100666b6), nano 214/221 (0x100667a8), XP (stat 0x34 − 0x39)/(0x15e − 0x39), level ≥200 uses 0x23d/0x240 (0x100668aa), alien XP 0x28/0xb2 (0x100669f7, created only if stat 0x185 (Expansion) & 0x18). Tooltip `View::SetToolTip(LDBface::GetText(0x2710, key), "<tvoptions wordwrap=\"no\">%d&nbsp;/&nbsp;%d")` with the keys `Health` (0x101b4300), `Nano` (0x101b4334), `Experience` (0x101af930, shared literal, referenced at 0x100669a4) and `AlienExperience` (0x101b433c, referenced at 0x10066aa5) = text.mdb category 10000 `by_key` → "Health", "Nano", "Experience", "Alien Experience" (`Hud::bar_titles`, test `bar_titles_come_from_the_text_db`).
* Menus: `CCMenu` → `ControlMenu_c` 0x10071524, entries `CCMenuEntry_c` 0x100660f2 (attrs `label bgicon invoke active_value criteria button_mode golden_button tooltip tooltip_body sub_script`); entries are stacked vertically, pitch = height + 4 (0x1007086f), invisible (criteria) entries skipped. Sub menus open in a style-3 window (flags 0xd3c) placed by 0x100653b6 (right of the button unless its centre is in the right half, bottom 7 px above the button bottom). Entry = `Button_c` with 3-slice art raised `BUTTON01_*` (0x9b/9d/9f, golden 0x9c/9e/a0), pressed `BUTTON03_*`, hover `BUTTON02_*` (0x10065b4e); `bgicon` 48×22 at layer alpha 0.85; `UpdateBgIconFade` 0x10127bdd: icon tint = 0x20 + (1−fade)·223, label shown when fade > 0.8, fade ±0.075 per `SlotFadeTimer`.
* Shortcut bar: `ShortcutBarWindow_c` 0x100d94e9 / `ShortcutBarView_c` 0x100d8c12: radio (0x159/0x15a) + `TOOLBAR_CONTROL_H` 32×38 with up/down buttons + 10×1 `MultiListView` of `SLOT_32_CLOSED` (38×38, pitch 36) = 403×38. Config path `…/Containers/ShortcutBar_<n>.xml`.
* **Mini toolbar above Wear (bug 11):** retail `pasted-image-cf5fb9f9b633eadc.png` shows this icon strip, distinct from the Wear header in `pasted-image-ba7bc439cfc00e5e.png`. `Views/ControlCenter.xml:19` registers `CCMiniToolbar` with `cc_mini_toolbar && cc_rollup_panel && cc_section1`; `CharPrefs.xml:67` defaults the setting to false, so the capture is a nondefault setting. The unsupported XML element was an empty view in the port. It now keeps the native criteria and receives the native buttons.
  GUI.dll `MiniToolbar_c` ctor `0x100726b3` defines, in order, Wear (`wear_window`, SB_WEAR), Controls (`specialaction_window`, SB_ACTION), Knowledge (`knowledge_window`, SB_REF), Mission (`mission_window`, SB_MISSION), Team (`team_view`, SB_TEAM), Map (`map_window`, SB_MAP), Friends (`friends_window`, SB_FRIENDS), Programs (`nano_window`, SB_NANO), Stats (`stat_window`, SB_STAT), NCU (`ncu_window`, SB_NCU). Names in parentheses are exact DValues, and the displayed names are the constructor's tooltip strings (empty tooltip body). `ToolbarButton_c` `0x10072b33` binds the DValue, toggle value and tooltip; assembly `0x1007272b..0x10072743` passes raised `0x173` = SB_WEAR_OFF and pressed/hover `0x172` = SB_WEAR. Each entry uses its corresponding ON/OFF pair. Click callback `0x10072429` writes the bool DValue; `0x10072475` applies DValue changes back to the button. Layout `0x100724e8` places the first at (0,0), advances by inclusive Width+1, and reports summed width/max height; no invented spacing or icons.
  Existing `Hud::toggle_dvalue` opens/closes implemented windows, including the Knowledge reference grid; Friends remains chat-owned. Regression `hud::tests::native_mini_toolbar_setting_order_and_window_toggles` covers disabled default, enabled strip, native icon widths/contiguous order, all ten DValue actions, implemented-window membership and rollup criteria.
* **Mini-toolbar/Wear gap (bug23):** `ControlCenter.xml:15–23` places
  `CCMiniToolbar` immediately before `RollupControllerDock`, without a spacer
  or intervening top borders. Fresh GUI.dll layout `0x100724e8` caches the
  maximum button-frame height at `this+0x138`; preferred-size callback
  `0x1007249f` returns the cached point when visible. `RollupController_c`
  layout `0x10048232` begins pages at controller-local y=0. The port retained
  the module's initial screen-space y=20 after XML layout. Rollup windows now
  synchronize their top to the laid-out `RollupArea` after criteria refresh,
  retaining scroll, clipping and bottom behavior.
  `mini_toolbar_and_rollup_share_the_xml_boundary` covers toolbar enabled,
  disabled and re-enabled states and contiguous inclusive boundaries.
  The generic toolbar adapter also retained its icon height while hidden.
  Native preferred-size callback `0x1007249f` returns a hidden-height sentinel;
  the adapter now collapses its hidden row while the fixed-width adjacent
  RollupControllerDock retains the column width.
* **NewLevel notices** (`play/level_notice.rs`): fresh GUI.dll string xrefs resolve
  `got_ip` at `0x101aec6c`, `got_perk` at `0x101aec98`, and `got_tech` at
  `0x101aeca8` to `0x1002de8e`, `0x1002dfe7`, and `0x1002e140`.
  These create `NotifyButton` (`0x10052fa0`), a style-3 borderless window
  (flags `0xd3c`) with a native icon button, **not a chat toast or golden CC entry**.
  Raised/pressed/hover all use `GFX_GUI_NEW_IP` (`0xaf`), `NEW_PERK` (`0xb0`),
  or `NEW_TECH` (`0xb1`). Origin is `(screenW-60, screenH-273/333/393)`;
  exact f32 constants are `0x101aec64=60`, `0x101aec68=273`,
  `0x101aec88=333`, `0x101aeca4=393`. No flash timer is created.
  Click handler `0x10052f39` sets `skill_window`, `perk_window`, or
  `research_window` true. Login pref `ShowIPPerkbutton` (default 1) gates
  the notices; disabled callbacks clear `got_*`. Opening Skills/Perks/Research
  clears their flag (GUI `0x100fe956`, `0x100611dc`, `0x100f6bf4`);
  the Research callback additionally suppresses a notice while Research is open.
  Fresh decompilation confirms `0x1002de8e`/`0x1002dfe7` never read the
  Skills/Perks window DValues; only `0x1002e140` reads `research_window`.
  A new IP/perk notice therefore survives while its target is already open;
  the separate opening action still clears the previous notice. Regression
  `native_notice_assets_geometry_click_and_preference` covers this distinction
  and Research suppression.
  `DValues::new` registers these nonpersistent booleans; HUD refresh and
  click handling use the original assets/positions and existing window consumers.
  Research uses the existing `hud_research_vehicle.rs` consumer (see
  “Native Research and Vehicle windows” below), reached through `research_window`.
  `hud_keys.rs` also registers native `WINDOW_RESEARCH` (GUI `SetupProviders`
  `0x10068c38`); shipped `CharPrefs.xml` binds Shift+O (`Input 131168`,
  `Provider 4237151846`). No new level text is generated.
  Regressions `level_notice::tests::opening_notice_target_clears_only_its_flag`
  and `native_notice_assets_geometry_click_and_preference` cover independent
  dismissal, actual asset IDs/natural dimensions, borderless window sizing,
  native origins, click targets, and the disabled preference.

**Notice palette / Actions backdrop (bugs 9/13):** `NotifyButton` `0x10052fa0`
constructs a Button and sets states 0/1/2 to the same NEW_IP/PERK/TECH art.
The generic icon-button renderer previously bypassed native surface styling,
drawing white at alpha 1. It now follows `GetBorderView` `0x10128111` /
`SetGfx` `0x10128270`: raised DEFAULT, pressed SELECTED, HOVER overlay,
layer-2 alpha .85 (`GUIColors.xml:3–5`: 80e9f3/ffffcc/a5ffdb).
`notice_art_uses_native_button_palette_and_hover_overlay` covers all states.
Fresh `ControlMenu_c` `0x10071524` decompile constructs a View and borderless
style-3 Window (flags 0xd3c), without a background surface; the `CCMenu`
definitions in `Views/ControlCenter.xml:27,31` specify no background.
No enclosing Actions backdrop is therefore added.
`settings_nested_popup_and_pet_actions` asserts the borderless dimensions and
absence of an enclosing solid backdrop.
  No tests, builds, or live checks were run by this worker.
* `prefs/NewChar/*` is the install's new-character template, but its retail bootstrap consumer remains unestablished. DLL/EXE ASCII and UTF-16 searches found no `NewChar` string; traced `GetCharPrefsPath` **0x1006bacd**, `GetCharacterPath` **0x101245f9**, `Preferences::LoadCharPrefs` **0x101246af**, and `SetDefaultCharPrefs` **0x1012447a**. They establish the character-ID archive path, archive loading and registered defaults, not a NewChar-directory copy. Missing prerequisite: provenance of first-character preference creation (original launcher/installer bootstrap implementation or a pristine first-launch filesystem trace); this is not an undecoded preferences loader. The port's explicit template adoption is an intentional behavior, not proven retail parity. Arbitrary NewChar bar/hotbar frames are saved custom positions, not a screen-independent first-login layout. Pool bars use `SlotPlayerCharacterAlive` **0x1006afed**, compass **0x1006d433**, hotbar `(20,20)` **0x100d94e9**; character-owned frames override those origins and are clamped by `MoveInsideScreen`.
* **Item inspection (bug 7):** `hud_stats/item_ui.rs` routes Shift-left-click and right-click on filled wear/grid cells, and inventory list rows, through `Hud::take_info_urls` to the existing chat InfoView (`Views/InfoView.xml`, `BrowserView`, Back/Forward; docs/chat/dialogs.md §1). URLs carry the runtime inventory identity from GC `FUN_10046d8e(slot)`, not a template ID or the entry's unresolved `id` words. The qualified click never arms drag or enters double-click use; missing slots produce no URL. Shift info signals are evidenced by `MultiListView_c` handler `FUN_10040a17` (§10.5); direct right-click inspection is the requested interaction, while the complete retail item popup remains unresolved. The inventory tab's **“i”** is the window-icon menu (`WndBorder::SlotIconButton` 0x1015a74e; `Event::FrameIcon`), not an item-information button; it is intentionally not rebound to inspect an arbitrary item. Regression `item_inspection_does_not_use_or_drag_inventory_and_wear` covers filled wear/grid/list routes and absence of outbound use/move messages; not run during this edit.

Not done / UNRESOLVED: fade timer period (50 Hz assumed); label-only entry width; RollupArea page parenting / clip-flag setup (area containment is [INFERENCE], shared View clipping is traced, §10.8); alien bar default position; the compass / slider / hotbar gaps are listed in 10.1 – 10.3. Shots: `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/x cargo test --release -p aomac hud::tests` writes `hud-1280.png`, `hud-1920.png`, `compass-*.png`, `hud-aggdef-*.png`, `hud-hotbar*.png`.

### 10.1 Compass (`play/hud_compass.rs`)
* `CompassWindow_c` factory `FUN_1006d433` (GUI 0x1006d433; the dtor `FUN_1006d697` saves the window into the DValue `CompassWindowConfig`): `Window(Rect(0,0,0,0), "compass_window", style 3, flags 0xd3c)`, criteria `dvalue:cc_section1 && dvalue:cc_compass` (`CriteriaMonitor_c`, both true in `CharPrefs.xml`; `FUN_10067c45` shows / hides the window when the criteria flip), child `CompassView_c` (`FUN_100677b3`, 0x160 bytes). Position: `MoveTo(floor(screenW · (double)0.73f − Width(frame)/2), 5)` (`_PTR_101b45b0` = 0x3fe75c29_00000000, `_DAT_101a8b98` = 5.0; `Width` = inclusive extent 136), then `LoadWndConfig` (nothing saved on a first login) and `MoveInsideScreen`. Check: the install's template `prefs/NewChar/Prefs.xml` `CompassWindowConfig` `Rect(1800,5,1936,26)` is exactly this formula for a 2560 px screen (`floor(2560·0.73 − 68) = 1800`), so the template is *not* an extra input; per resolution: 1280 → x 866, 1920 → x 1333 (test `origin_follows_the_screen_width`).
* Surfaces (`ViewSurface_c::Load(id, flags)`): frame `GFX_GUI_COMPASS_FRAME` 0x8e (137 × 22, green-keyed centre), strip `GFX_GUI_COMPASS_SCALE` 0x8f with flags 0x10 (256 × 14, tiled: N at x = 0, E 64, S 128, W 192, tick marks every 16 px), waypoint marker `GFX_GUI_COMPASS_WAYPOINT` 0x90 (7 × 9). `GFX_GUI_COMPASS` (256 × 16) and `_BORDER` (134 × 20) are **not referenced** by this view (ids 0x8b / 0x8c; other users in the DLLs were not searched). Strip and frame get `SetColor(DEFAULT 0x1000000)`, the view `SetAlpha(GUIConfig::GetAlphaValue(layer 2))` = 0.85. Strip is vertically centred in the frame (`MoveDstTo(0, floor(Height(frame)/2 − Height(strip)/2))` = y 4); paint order = `AddRenderSurface` order: strip, (waypoint), frame (`MoveToFront` after the waypoint is created).
* Scrolling (`FUN_100670f8`, per frame): `N3Msg_GetGlobalCharacterRotation` → quaternion `q`; `v = q · (0,0,1)` (`FUN_10043401`); `a = atan2(v.z, v.x)` (`_CIatan2` 0x10173c64, first operand z), `a += 2π` while `a < 0`; `frac = −(a − π/2)/2π`; strip `MoveSrcTo(GetTextureSize.x · frac − Width(frameDst)/2, 0)` with `GetTextureSize` = inclusive extent 255, i.e. the window's centre column is `255·frac`. Handedness: a character walks along `(sin yaw, cos yaw)` in server x/z (zone.rs `scene_forward`, verified against NPC walks), so `v = (sin yaw, 0, cos yaw)`, `a = π/2 − yaw`, `frac = yaw/2π`: heading 0 (+z) centres N, +π/2 (+x) centres E. The engine's global rotation is server-space (not the mirrored scene space). The one-pixel inconsistency of 255 vs 256 is the original's (test `strip_is_centred_on_the_heading`: S / W land 0.5 / 0.75 px beside the exact column). We feed it `zone.own().yaw` (0 when the server sent no rotation).
