* Waypoint: GlobalSignals+0x158 carries **`(Identity quest, Vector3& local_position, uint playfield_instance)`**, not a playfield identity. GUI`0x1006749b` stores source identity`+0x144`, vector`+0x14c`, PF`+0x158`, active flag`+0x15c`. Nonzero PF emits category10000 `MissionWptUploaded` then `MissionShownAsDot` when PF=current or `MissionNotInArea` otherwise, on+0x17c with color16; zero clears and emits `NoWptInfo`, color12. Keys are at`0x101b4398/4384/4370/43ac`; exact branches`0x100675a3..0x100676d3`. +0x15c clears matching quest identity (or zero) without these feedback lines (`0x10067092`). Missions and terminal producers now carry that identity and local vector; quest removal clears only the associated marker, whereas `/waypoint` replaces its source. The HUD forwards both native feedback colors to the existing chat consumer. Regression`waypoint_feedback_and_removal_retain_native_quest_identity` covers same/other PF, manual clear, source replacement and quest deletion. Marker rendering remains current-PF-only: `d=waypoint-own`, bearing=`atan2(d.z,d.x)`; x=`floor(256*(heading_angle-bearing)/2π+68)` wrapped into[0,256], destination`floor(x-(Width+1)/2)`, y6; view clips to137px (`0x100679da`, `0x100670f8`). Existing compass math and offscreen-shot regressions cover direction/clipping.
* Tests: `cargo test --release -p aomac hud_compass` (maths) and `hud::tests::compass_shots` (4× crops `compass-{north,east,south,west}.png`, `compass-wp-{ahead,right,behind,other-playfield}.png`).

### 10.2 AGG/DEF slider (`play/hud_aggdef.rs`, `ao_net::n3::outgoing::set_stat`)
* `RightBarView_c` ctor `FUN_1006ff09`: `BitmapView(BOTTOM_RIGHT 0x9a)`, HLayout `[TextView "DEF"][Slider_c][TextView "AGG"]` (`SetBorders` 5 px between), the slider created with `Slider_c(Rect, "", −1, min −100.0 (_DAT_101b5358), max 100.0 (_DAT_101adfa8), 0, 0)` (ctor 0x1014448a; its value starts at `min`), slot `FUN_1006e9c5` connected to its change signal, and `GlobalSignals::GetCharStatSignal(0x33 = AggDef)` → `FUN_1006e968` = `slider.SetValue(N3Msg_GetAggDef(), notify = false)`, called once at construction and on every change of the stat. `N3Msg_GetAggDef` [IF 0x100087bd] → `n3EngineClientAnarchy_t::N3Msg_GetAggDef` [GC 0x10026c2f] = own character `GetStat(0x33, mode 2)`, 0 without a client character. **Default = the stat the server sent** (0 until then); knob at 0 sits at x = 58 (`floor((0+100)·117/200)`), as the old static layout had.
* `Slider_c` internals: background `BorderView` (gfx 0x92 = `AGGDEF_SLIDER_BACKGROUND`, 128 × 18), knob `BitmapView` (0x91 = `AGGDEF_SLIDER`, 11 × 18, tinted DEFAULT). `_Layout` 0x10144141: knob x = `floor((v − min)·((Width+1 − borders) − (Width(knob)+1)) / (max − min) + border)`, y centred. `MouseDown` 0x10143efc: a press inside the knob frame sets the dragging flag (+0x170) and the grab offset = pointer − knob left-top, captures the mouse; `MouseMove` 0x10143f8a: `v = (max − min)·(x − grab − border)/travel + min` → `SetValue(v, true)` (clamped to [min, max], `_Layout`, emits `(v, !dragging)`); `MouseUp` 0x101443b3 clears the flag and emits `(v, true)`. Slot `FUN_1006e9c5(value, …, bool)`: flag false (while dragging) → `SetValue(floor(value), false)` snaps the knob to whole numbers without a signal; flag true (release) → `N3InterfaceModule_t::N3Msg_SetAggDef((float)value)` [IF 0x10008797] = `N3Msg_SetStat(ftol(v), 0x33)` [GC 0x10026c49]. So nothing is sent while dragging, one message on release.
* Message: `SetStatIIR_t` (key `0x6E5F566E`, vtable GC 0x10161370; ctor `FUN_1007737e` stores `+0x18 = value`, `+0x1c = stat`; `Write` `FUN_1007730c` = `i32 value, i32 stat`; `Read` `FUN_1007732f` rejects `stat 0x33` outside −100..100). `N3Msg_SetStat` → `EnqueueSelfMessage` [N3 0x1000624f] → `n3Engine_t::DoExternalInput` [N3 0x100065d1] applies it to the own dynel (we write `Zone::stats[0x33]` at once) and, since the IIR's pass-on byte is 1 (`n3InfoItemRemote_t` ctor 0x10009860 default), `SendIIRToObservers` → `SendIIRToServer` [N3 0x10007762]. `ao_net::n3::outgoing::set_stat(char_id, stat, value)` encodes `6E5F566E 0000C350 <char> 01 <value> <stat>` (test `set_stat_bytes`); `Hud` queues the frame (`take_outbox`), the flow sends it. Verified: `hud::tests::aggdef_drag_sends_set_stat_on_release` (press knob, drag 30 px → value 50, one frame on release, clamp at 100, press beside the knob grabs nothing). The server's reply to a stat change (echoed `StatIIR_t`) is whatever the existing stat path applies. **Not captured live**: no capture of this message exists; the layout is from the DLL only.

### 10.3 Hotbar contents and interaction (`play/hud_bar.rs`)
* **Shortcut stacking / count (bug 7):** GUI `ShortcutBarWindow_c` **0x100d94e9** constructs an ordinary style-3 `Window` with flags **0x183c**: neither frontmost **0x100** nor backmost **0x200**. `Window::Window` **0x101563ff** translates those layer flags to View flags 0x10/8; `WindowController_c::AddWindow` **0x101577bf** attaches the same border root as other windows, and `View::_LinkChild` **0x10149d2b** inserts it in the normal layer. `WindowController_c::HandleMouseDown` **0x10157ab0** calls `Window::MoveToFront` **0x10155564** before dispatch unless flag **0x400** prohibits raising. The port incorrectly used immutable creation order, making a newly added bar permanently cover older Options. Both drawing and input now use mutable per-layer stacking; clicking exposed Options raises it above bars, while clicking a bar can raise it normally. New windows still initially appear in front, as retail does; no shortcut-only layer exception was invented.
  `ShortcutBarContainer` **0x100d9a81** binds **NumHotbars**, creates at least one bar on initialization, and supports ten shortcut/page bindings. Its change handler **0x100d9959** destroys excess bar windows from the end (show false, destructor, pop), or constructs and shows added windows; it does not merge them into one always-on-top surface or change Options' layer. The existing port's live count update already follows that create/remove policy. Regression: `mouse_down_raises_windows_without_pinning_shortcut_bars` checks newly added bar order, exposed-window raising, input order, right-click raising and layer preservation.
* **Spinner movement and page label (bug 15):** GUI `FUN_100db4d6` records the mouse and captures on plain left down; `FUN_100db335` moves only while `LockHotbars` is false; `FUN_100db319` releases capture. The movement surface is the complete horizontal spinner (local x 11..42), excluding its arrow buttons, never the radio or shortcut slots; no Shift/Alt or general-window-lock requirement. `ShortcutBar::update` enables that region only when unlocked; an arrow press disables it before the same event reaches GUI input, and the next update restores it. Spinner ctor `FUN_100daec6` creates the NORMAL (font id 5, as mapped in `ao-gui/src/font.rs`), yellow TextView; `FUN_100da8eb` sets `(row + 1) % 10` (1..9,0), measures its preferred size and floors its centred position between the arrows. The XML `page` TextView uses that preferred-size centring and updates with the visible row. Sources: Ghidra artifacts `20113:260-325`, `20157:235-305` and movement functions above. Regression `hud_bar::tests::spinner_page_and_plain_drag_respect_lock_and_arrows` covers row text/centring/font height, locked versus plain unlocked movement, slot exclusion and arrow paging without movement; not executed as part of this change.
* **Arrow wrap:** observed GUI.dll assembly `100dae98..100daec5` adds 9 (up) or increments (down), divides by 10 and passes the remainder to `100daa9e`: `(row + 9) % 10` / `(row + 1) % 10`. Existing arrow input now wraps instead of clamping; the spinner regression presses the actual up/down hit regions to check displayed `1 → 0 → 1` with no window movement. Mouse-wheel paging is unchanged.
* **Page x scalar:** GUI data `101beda0..101beda7` is LE words `0x00000000, 0x40330000`, i.e. f64 **19.0**. `FUN_100da8eb` positions the horizontal label at spinner-local `floor(19.0 - (preferred_text_width + 1) / 2)` (preferred width is an inclusive extent), not the spinner's geometric centre 16. The preferred-size XML label's 6 px left layout border shifts its centre by 3 px while leaving the spinner movement region unchanged; the regression checks this exact x formula.
* First login (`FUN_100d94e9`, GUI 0x100d94e9; the block is guarded by the IndependentPrefs int `IsFirstTime` ≠ 0, then sets it 0 and saves): slots (IPoint x, 0): 0 = `{0xdeb0, 0xc1a5}`, 1 = `{0xdeb0, 0xc1a2}`, 2 = `{0xdeb0, 0xc1a8}`, 3 = a text macro created by `TextMacroSystem_t::CreateMacro("Follow", "/follow", 0, true)` → `{0xc789, id}` (the template's `prefs/NewChar/TextMacro.bin` = `00000001 00000001 00000006 "Follow" 00000007 "/follow"`: id 1), 9 = `{0xdeb0, 0x14124}`. Slot item class `InventoryListViewItem_c` (`FUN_1003d830(type, identity)`), type by identity kind in `FUN_100d78e5`: 1 item, 2 = 0xdac3, 4 nano 0xcf1b, **6 = 0xdeb0 special action**, **7 = 0xc789 macro**.
* Identity kind 0xdeb0 is a special action and resolves to **rdb 1000020** (`GetItemByTemplate` GC 0x10017aae: `if (kind == 0xdeb0) kind = 0xF4254`), i.e. the same template records as items; name = `N3Msg_GetName`, icon id = stat `Icon` (0x4f, `FUN_1003eb30`; fallback GFX 0xb9 `DEFAULT_ICON48`), the image = **rdb 1010008** record `Icon` (`ViewSurface_c::Load(id, 0x80)` → `GuiResourceManager_t::GetRDBTexture` [IF 0x1000b6e7]: "Unable to find the image with id e_RDB_Res_Icon:%d"), PNG 48 × 48 keyed like all GUI textures, stretched to the item view's 32 × 32 bounds (`FUN_1003f2ea`). Records: `0xc1a5` "Start Combat" icon 49565, `0xc1a2` "Walk" 49560, `0xc1a8` "Sit" 49564, `0x14124` "Suspended Animation" 82199 (every record also has the description text; stat 588 = 3/2). Macro: icon GFX 0xe3 (`GFX_GUI_ICON_MACRO`, `FUN_1003eb30` type 7), label `<font color=yellow>Follow</font>` in a `TextView_c` of font 7 (SMALL) centred (`FUN_1003f8e4`, `FUN_1003f2ea`). Icons sit at +2 / +3 px of the 36 px cell (centred in the 38 px art).
* Slot signals (ctor wires `MultiListView_c` signals +0x2d8 drag-begin → `FUN_100d7ec1`, +0x2dc drop → `FUN_100d8047`, +0x2e0 use → `FUN_100d79c9`, +0x2e4 info → `FUN_100d765d`; emitted by `MultiListView_c` item handler `FUN_10040a17`): **use** `FUN_100d79c9`: type 1 `N3Msg_UseItem`, 4 `N3Msg_CastNanoSpell`, **6 `N3Msg_PerformSpecialAction(identity)`** [GC 0x100272fd] → `FUN_1004256c` (looks the identity up in the character's special-action list `+0x84`, `Feedback_ActionIsNotAvailable` when absent; then by the entry's `Action_e`: 0x4e / 0xb `DefaultAttack`, 0x4c / 0x4d `SitToggle`, 0x4f `MovementChanged(0x24)`, 0x51 / 0x52 camping, …, docs/zone/combat-net.md §5.3), **7** emits the macro text on GlobalSignals +0x180 (typed into the chat input). **Info** (Shift+click) `itemid://kind/instance` into `InfoViewModule_c::ShowURL` (not for macros). **Drag** begins unless DValue `LockHotbars` (false in `CharPrefs.xml`); drop on a slot of this bar = `MultiListView_c::MoveItem`, the displaced item starts a new drag; after any drag `FUN_100d76cf` deletes the item if it still sits in its source slot, so dropping outside the bar removes the shortcut. Icon timers (`IconTimers`, `IconTimerText` true): `N3Msg_GetActionProgress` overlay for type 6; item disabled tint from `N3Msg_GetSpecialActionState` (`FUN_1003ebd0`).
* Ours: `ShortcutBar` paints the bar canvas (chrome + 10 slot frames + icons) under ten 36 × 38 `slotN` canvases (+ `labelN` text views) carrying tooltip, click and drag source/target. Short tooltips show the item name; long tooltips use the shared item-information formatter when `!ShortcutShortTooltips && ItemDescriptionTooltips` (GUI `100d77b3`, `1004178f`; regression `long_shortcut_tooltips_share_the_item_info_formatter`). Press/release and hold-to-drag semantics are documented in §10.5. An occupied drop starts a new drag of the displaced shortcut, rather than swapping it back into the source; a drop outside deletes (`LockHotbars` honoured). Tests: `hud::tests::hotbar_first_login_and_interaction`, `occupied_shortcut_redrags_displaced_item_except_macros`.
* `/macro` drag (`GlobalSignals+0x1a0`, slot `FUN_100d82d4` of `ShortcutBarWindow_c`): `CreateMacro(name, command, 0, true)` then `View::BeginDrag(DragObject_c "inventory/textmacro" {item_id = Identity{0xC789, id}})`. `Chat` queues `Requests::macro_drag`; the HUD starts the macro ghost at the mouse. Releasing on a slot stores it there (label = name, use runs its command); **the textmacro branch explicitly deletes an occupant**, unlike ordinary inventory/nano/action drops (`GUI 100d8047`). Releasing outside discards the drag while retaining `TextMacro.bin`. Tests: `macro_drag_drops_on_slot_or_is_discarded`, `occupied_shortcut_redrags_displaced_item_except_macros`.
* Inventory shortcuts: inventory/wear releases routed through `HudStats::take_drops` are claimed by the shortcut cell under the real pointer. They retain the inventory slot identity, template name and rdb icon without moving the item. Activation uses the same `GenericCmd` 3 path as inventory item use, not the double-click wear/unwear policy (`FUN_100d79c9`, type 1). Regression: `hud::tests::inventory_shortcut_drop_and_use`; real-window acceptance is reported separately.
* **Programs drag and restart (bug 18):** GUI `FUN_100d3807` begins a nano drag, `FUN_100d38ef` emits its `itemid://` inspection URL, and `FUN_100d3b11` casts. Programs qualified clicks inspect instead of casting; held/moved ordinary presses start the existing shortcut drag. `programs_shift_inspect_and_drag_to_hotbar_cast` covers this path. Retail `ShortcutBarWindow_c` ctor `0x100d94e9` loads `Containers/ShortcutBar_<n>.xml` (bar 0 also tries the old `ShortcutBar.xml`); `ShortcutBarView_c` ctor `0x100d8c12` reads `position_map` as repeated `Identity item_id` / `IPoint item_pos` pairs. Save helper `0x100d7db1` writes every identity/grid position, `selected_bar`, `bar_orientation` and `IsPrimary`. The port loads character contents after preferences and saves all rows on HUD close, retaining the existing archive's WindowFrame/orientation children; nano identities resolve through NanoDb and still activate as `SlotUse::Nano` after restart. Regression `nano_contents_survive_restart_and_activate` covers row 7, native nano identity, cast dispatch and unrelated archive preservation. No tests/builds/live checks run for this change.
* **Rendering notes** (headless `hud-hotbar.png`, 4x crop inspected): the special-action icons are the real rdb 1010008 art (crosshair, red footprints, sitting figure, "EXIT"); the Follow macro icon is `GFX_GUI_ICON_MACRO` (0xe3, `Graphics.uvga` offset 919300, 32 × 32, extracted and viewed): it really is a **black square with grey text lines**, with the yellow macro name over its centre. Two layout bugs were fixed: (a) the slot row's `layout_borders` is ignored under the `stacked` parent (`layout::layout` sets stacked children to the parent bounds), so the 43 px chrome offset was lost and the 10 cells spread over the whole bar at a 40 px pitch (the painted icons are on the 36 px grid, so labels / hit canvases sat up to 30 px off) — the offset is now a fixed 42 px spacer view; (b) a `TextView` draws from the top of its frame, so each label sits in a vertical layout whose child frame is one `SMALL` line high (centred). Tests: `hud_bar::tests::slot_views_follow_the_painted_grid`.
* **Use consumers** (`play/hud_use.rs`, after `Hud::update`): the current `SpecialList` entry supplies `Action_e`, not item stat59 or an action name. `GC100428ad` explicitly maps Fling `0x96→0x14120`, Full Auto `0xa7→0x14121`, Brawl `0x8e→0x14122`, Burst `0x94→0x14123`, Camp `0x51→0x14124`, Dimach `0x90→0x14125`, Fast Attack `0x93→0x14126`, Aimed Shot `0x97→0x14127`, Disrupt Camp `0x52→0x1412b`, Stop Sneaking `0x4f→0x1412c`. These skill-action templates are not learned perk catalogue items. Dynamic perks use the server's action→template registration (`10042e16`, §11.13); no by-name `perk_action` fallback remains. Attack/sit/walk/camp consumers are described in §10.7, along with the implemented sneak, crawl, forage and Search consumers. Macros run through `Chat::run_line`.
  **Dormant Trade action, not a missing trade-start route:** a fresh GC `FUN_1004256c` trace has no `0x4b` branch: the `< 0x50` section handles 0x14, then 0x4c/0x4d and 0x4e, skipping Trade. GUI `FUN_100d79c9` forwards all type-6 hotbar slots directly to `N3Msg_PerformSpecialAction`, with no Trade-specific interception. The template value is not evidence of an implemented consumer. The port therefore does not invent a Trade special-action route; player trade still starts through the native default-action path (docs/zone/interact.md §10.2). Unsupported special actions retain the existing diagnostic.
* **Occupied shortcut drops** (`GUI 100d8047`, drag-end `100d76cf`): same-bar `MoveItem` then `100d7ec1` re-drags the occupant; external inventory/nano/action drops construct a new occupant ghost, delete its old cell, and insert the incoming shortcut. Cross-bar drops use the same replacement path after the source release is consumed, so the new drag cannot consume its own initiating release. Macro drops use the native delete-occupant exception above. Regression `occupied_shortcut_redrags_displaced_item_except_macros` covers same-bar, cross-bar, displaced release and macro replacement.
* **Persisted inventory metadata**: `position_map` retains inventory slot identities; the native `100d78e5` constructs each identity's item view, whose refresh `1003d50c` reads live metadata. Restored shortcut names/icons now resolve from the current owned slot/template, and refresh across visible and hidden rows on template replacement; unchanged templates reuse cached metadata. Regression `persisted_inventory_shortcuts_refresh_every_row_after_template_replacement`.
* **Legacy position fallback** (`GUI 100d8c12`, `IconPositionSystem_t::Load` **1001e53a**, `Save` **1001e148**): absent `position_map` loads `<character>/IconPositions.bin` map identity **{0,0}**, map type **6**, with linear position `x = pos % 10`, `y = pos / 10`. File_t words are big-endian: total file size, map count; each map has owner kind/instance, type, entry count, then repeated position/item kind/item instance. NewChar's 136-byte file starts `00000088 00000008 00000000 00000000 00000006 00000000`, an empty shortcut map. An XML map (including an empty one) takes precedence. The loader rejects size/count/truncation/out-of-grid errors without partially changing the bar; ordinary saves migrate contents to XML. Regressions `legacy_positions_validate_and_select_native_shortcut_map`, `legacy_shortcut_fallback_defers_to_xml_position_map`.

## 13. Tooltips (`ao-gui` `gui/tooltip.rs`) and the target controls (`play/hud_target.rs`)

### 13.1 Tooltips (GUI.dll)
Evidence (decompiled GUI.dll, all addresses `0x1xxxxxxx`):
* **Source of the texts**: `View::SetToolTip(title, body)` 0x1014dadf stores two strings in the view's extra data (`+0x140` title, `+0x15c` body); `View::GetToolTipText`
  0x1014db1a returns them (or asks the virtual `ProvideToolTipText` 0x1014a750, which is `false` for plain views; `PowerBar_t::ToolTipCallback` 0x10020686,
  `MultiListView_c::SlotProvideItemTooltip` 0x100092ce and `TextRenderer_c::ProvideToolTipText` 0x1016317a are the dynamic providers). XML views carry `tooltip="#Key"` /
  `tooltip_body="#Key"` (ActionMenu entries, `OptionPanel/Root.xml`); `#` keys resolve through text.mdb like every other attribute.
* **Timing**: `WindowController_c::UpdateToolTip(false)` 0x101577fd (mouse moved) walks the *topmost window under the pointer* (`FUN_10156d66`: children from the top child
  down, then the view itself) to the deepest view whose `GetToolTipText` is non-empty; if it differs from the stored view (`+0xa0`) the shown tip is closed; then the texts
  are stored and the deadline `timeGetTime() + 500` ms re-armed — **every mouse move over a tipped view restarts the 500 ms rest timer**. `WindowController_c::Render`
  0x101572a1 creates `new ToolTip_c(title, body)` when the deadline passed, closing a tooltip that is still shown (so after the pointer rests again the tooltip jumps to the new
  position). `HandleMouseDown/Up` 0x10157ab0 / 0x10157c34 call `UpdateToolTip(true)` (close + reset); `ToolTip_c::SlotGlobalMouseDown` 0x101499ab hides it on a click outside its
  frame. `InputConfig_t::GetTooltipTime` (0x1000b5d2, a float at `InputConfig_t+0x20`) has no caller in the paths above (item tooltips): not used here.
* **Window**: `ToolTip_c::ToolTip_c` 0x10149a0d = `Window(Rect(0,0,10,10), "", "info_window", style 2, flags 0x105)`; style 2 draws `BorderView_c::SetGfx(…, 0x1bf)` =
  `GFX_GUI_WINDOW_BACKGROUND` (black, 15×15) at the layer-2 alpha **0.85** (`WndBorder::SetBorderGfx` 0x1015a82d) and no frame/buttons; the view is `InfoContainer_c`
  (`FUN_101492c6`, view name `info_view`), inset by 1 px on every side when a body exists (`Rect::Resize(1,1,-1,-1)`).
* **InfoContainer layout** (inclusive extents, padding `p = 1` without a body, `p = 2` with one): title `TextView_c("title_view", font 5 = NORMAL)`, body `TextView_c("text_view",
  NORMAL)` with HTML flags `0x60` (MULTILINE | WORD_WRAP) and `SetAspectRatio(2.0)` (wrap width `ftol(sqrt(textArea) · 2)`, at least the title width: `TextRenderer_c::
  CalculatePreferredSize` 0x101623ea). Preferred size: `x = max(titleW, bodyW) + 4p`, `y = titleH + 4p` (no body) or `titleH + bodyH + 1 + 7p`. `ViewSurface_c`s
  (`ViewSurface_c(Rect, flags)`, flags = anchoring: bit0 left, bit1 right, bit2 top, bit3 bottom, `_LayoutSelf` 0x101509e1): four edge lines of thickness `p` (flags 0xd left, 7 top,
  0xe right, 0xb bottom) in the **HOVER** colour, plus – with a body – a separator line `p` thick under the title (flags 7, HOVER), without a body a solid black fill (flags 0xf).
  Title frame `(2p, 2p, R−2p, titleH+2p−1)`, body frame `(2p, titleH+5p, R−2p, B−2p)`. Without a body the title is drawn in the DEFAULT colour
  (`TextRenderer_c::SetDefaultColor`); with a body both texts retain **white** (`TextRenderer_c` constructor **0x10163412**, `+0x1bc = 0xffffffff`; `TextView::Initialize` **0x10164b88** never overrides it).
* **Placement** `Window::MoveToMouse(false)` 0x10154e16: `x = mouse.x + 16`, or `mouse.x − (width+1)` if `mouse.x ≥ screenW/2`; `y = mouse.y + 32`, or `mouse.y − (height+1)` if
  `mouse.y ≥ screenH/2`; then `MoveInsideScreen`.
* **Window extent and wrap area resolved:** assembly **0x10149b42–10149b74** adds `Point(2,2)` to the view preferred size unconditionally, including title-only tooltips; the body-only inset remains separate. `HTMLParser_c::_UpdateWordSizes` **0x1015d779** stores the sum of all glyph/image advances × **13** at parser `+0x44` (renderer `+0x18c`), not maximum unwrapped line width × current font height. `CalculatePreferredSize` assembly **0x10162475–10162486** takes its square root, multiplies aspect2 and truncates. Regression `native_tooltip_window_padding_and_body_default_colour` checks both body states and emitted glyph colours.

Engine API (`ao_gui::Gui`): `tooltip=`/`tooltip_body=` on any XML element → `View::tip`; `set_tooltip(window, view, title, body)` / `set_tooltip_in(handle, …)` for dynamic
texts; `set_screen_size` (default: largest visible window); `show_tooltip(title, body)` shows a `ToolTip_c` at the pointer immediately (`tooltip_shown`, `tooltip_rect` for
tests). The timer runs on `Gui::frame(dt)`; `input()` feeds `UpdateToolTip`. Tests: `crates/ao-gui/tests/tooltip.rs` (500 ms rest, closing, placement, flip). Reproduce:
`cargo run --release -p ao-gui --example dump_view -- LoginWindow out.png --size 640x400 --tooltip "Skills|Body text" --mouse 100,60`.

### 13.2 Target selection (GUI.dll `TargetingModule_t`, `InputConfig_t`)
* The selection is **client-local**: `TargetingModule_t::SetTarget(id, forced)` 0x100257b0 → `InputConfig_t::SetCurrentTarget` 0x10019df0 (identity at `InputConfig_t+0xc0`,
  `GlobalSignals` emit) + `AFCM::Send(0x13, 0x126, id)` (received by `TargetingModule_t::SetTargetMessage` 0x10025c14 and `N3InterfaceModule_t::SetTargetMessage` (Interfaces
  0x1000907a) → `n3EngineClientAnarchy_t::N3Msg_SelectedTarget` Gamecode 0x10017728 → `n3Camera_t::SetSelectedTarget` N3 0x10020358) + the selection `Indicator_t` (`FUN_100255be`,
  not for targets with skill-flag 0x400) + the tip events `OnSelecting` / `OnSelectingNPC` / `OnSelectingPlayer`. **Nothing is sent to the server.**
  `FrameProcess` 0x10025fa4 drops the target when the dynel disappeared (`N3Msg_GetPos` fails or `N3Msg_GetParent` ≠ 0) unless it was forced; `RemoveTarget` 0x2598b remembers it
  as `m_cLastTarget`; `SelectSelf` 0x100259b1: no target → self; target is self → previous target; else remember + self.
* Keys (GUI.dll default hotkeys, `InputConfig_t::SetDefaultHotkeys` 0x1001ad88 text, `ParseFile` 0x1001ac19 parses the built-in table, the file name `hotkeys.txt` is not read): `TAB` next hostile,
  `SHIFT+TAB` previous hostile, `CTRL+TAB` next friendly, `CTRL+SHIFT+TAB` previous friendly → `TargetingModule_t::Get{Next,Prev}{Hostile,Friendly}TargetMessage` 0x10025adc… →
  `N3Msg_GetCloseTarget(current, friendly, forward)` (Gamecode 0x1001c411) → `SetTarget(id, false)`. **Order** (decompiled): candidates = dynels within **100 m** (`_DAT_10155eb0`) of the own
  character except it and the current target, visible, stat `InPlay` (0xc2) ≠ 0; class = `Side` (0x21, `0x29c` in a battle station), players in the own team and pets (stat `PetMaster` 0xc4) of
  the own team count as the own class; *friendly* = same class, *hostile* = different. Metric `m = d² − d²(current)` (`FUN_10023a81` is the squared length; 0 without a current character
  target), `+1000` (`_DAT_10157870`) once if negative or if 0 for a candidate listed before the current one; next = the smallest `m` (start `FLT_MAX`), previous = the largest (start
  `FLT_MIN`). So Tab walks outwards by distance and wraps. The locality worker (GC `0x10025b96`, vtable `0x1015745c+4`) accepts only kind 50000 and world `LineOfSight` between both feet raised by **1.6 m** (`DumpFloat 0x10157454`); it does not test camera facing/frustum, Health or CanAttack. `hud_target::cycle` applies these filters using the player's playfield collision, including doors and reverse surface rays; headless callers without a player omit collision. The locality query's list order remains unresolved (ids are used).
  **BattleStation/team/pet classification [fresh CODE, 2026-10-15]:** GC `0x1001c411` selects stat668 instead of Side iff N3 `IsBattleStation` `0x1000c5fa` sees playfield `0x111c..=0x1124`. Non-NPC (`char+0x21c == 0`) identities are friendly when `0x1006581f` finds them in the own subgroup or `0x10065865` finds them in any of six raid groups. The latter requires own subgroup `+0x38 >= 0` (`0x100657e9`); ordinary teams use group zero (`0x100657b6`). NPC identities do not get that override directly: stat196 must exist and be nonzero, then its kind50000 master must be self or satisfy those same team/raid predicates. No visible master dynel is required. `TeamMemberIIR` apply `0x1007a033` → `0x10065e1b` stores the own subgroup; negative group indices resolve through `0x100657b6`. The port retains these indices in the existing `TeamState`, feeds a gameplay projection from zone messages, and preserves full-update BattlestationSide alongside PetMaster. Regression `close_target_native_battle_team_raid_and_pet_classification` covers ordinary/raid memberships, direct NPC exclusion, own/raid pets, member removal, and both station range boundaries. This is headless regression coverage, not a live BattleStation acceptance claim.
  **Absent station-side default [CODE]:** `SimpleChar_t` constructor GC `0x1005cb6a` explicitly initializes stat668 to **2**. Both own and remote classification retain that value when the optional full-update field is absent; explicit zero is not equivalent to missing. The classification regression covers missing2 versus explicit0; `full_update_preserves_battlestation_side_for_own_and_remote` mutates the captured zero-flags3 envelope to carry side7 and verifies own/remote retention, including replacing an older own stat.
  **Verification:** headless `cargo test --release --workspace -j 2` passed **1939 tests, 62 ignored, 40 suites**; `cargo clippy --release --workspace --all-targets -j 2 -- -D warnings` passed. The same completed gate includes the BattleStation nametag wiring documented in `docs/zone/motion.md`; no live session, desktop window/input or capture was used.
  Visibility is N3 byte `+0xc9` (`IsVisible` GC thunk `0x10131e9c` → N3 `0x10019552`), separate from InPlay. Full updates derive InPlay from the message's **main flags bit 1** (GC `0x10077af2`); remote **non-NPC** InPlay=0 hides the character (`0x10077e13`/`0x100783a1`, guarded by `+0x21c==0` and not-own), and CharInPlay enables visibility (`0x1007264d`). Death construction (`0x1007b2ba`) does not clear either state: a zero-health dying NPC can still be cycled, even after its death clip finishes. Death update (`0x1007b58c`) hides a remote NPC only with **run flag 0x200 set and animation completed**. Corpse creation (`0x1009f5a4`) sets that flag on the owner; the port already removes the owner when its corpse arrives, so it must not hide an NPC merely on clip completion before that message. Corpse props are kind `0xc76a`, not candidates. Selection persists through death/Health=0 until the character disappears (GUI `0x10025fa4`).
  **Offscreen live acceptance (2026-10-15, Aomacfixr / 33588, ICC 4582):** four Tab selections returned living, InPlay=1 characters: Island Reet 1022472, then after the kill 1022472 / 1022372 and Beach Leet 1022455 (all 12/12 HP). The separate hunt killed Beach Leet 1022451 with two rifle hits, 11+2 damage; subsequent Tab did not select its corpse. Inspected `selected`, `attack` and `postkill` offscreen frames: living selection brackets; Attack bar only during confirmed combat; after kill the bar was absent, Island Reet selected, and chat showed “You can loot these remains.” This does not reproduce or prove the earlier inferred corpse identity/refused-request association. The 86.81 s harness session camped back to login cleanly; no desktop window/input was used.
* **Escape is a broadcast, not clear-target-first consuming priority.** `EscapePressedMessage` [GUI 0x1002899e] calls `GUIModule::GetInstance` and emits its signal through `FUN_1002bb28`. The `FlowControlModule_t` constructor [GUI 0x1002abd6] connects `StopAllActionsMessage` [0x10027de4] via `FUN_1002768a`; that listener sends module `0x13` messages `0x139` / `0x168` and module `0x1e` message `0x112`. The latter is `RemoveTargetMessage` [0x10025c70] → `RemoveTarget` [0x1002598b] → null `SetTarget`, retaining the previous selection. Wear's captured `esc_wear=true` listener hears the same signal: one Escape both deselects and closes Wear; `esc_wear=false` leaves Wear open but still deselects. `TextInputMode` suppresses the global hotkey; `InfoViewMode` routes Escape to `CloseInfoSystem` only, rather than broadcasting. The port uses `HudTarget::select(None)` in the shared HUD Escape broadcast; regression `escape_broadcast_removes_target_and_respects_wear_and_text_input` covers both Wear options and text focus. Earlier claims of consuming deselection/window priority are unsupported and superseded by this signal trace.
  **Shared-signal identity proof (fresh native trace):** `GUIModule_c` constructor [GUI 0x1012f943] initializes `SignalBase` at object offset `+0`; its copy constructor [0x1001b407] also initializes that base. `FUN_1002768a` constructs the static `SlotStat0` and calls `SignalBase::_Connect`. The window connection helpers `FUN_1012a8ef` (dialogs), `FUN_1005a123` (options), and `FUN_10049cea` (nano) call `_Connect` on the same `GUIModule` pointer. These are listeners on the emitted signal, not a separate target-clear priority chain.
  **Flow mode gate:** the in-world Escape prebranch runs only outside GUI text focus. `Chat::info_view_open` identifies the InfoView mode independently of its captured `esc_infoview` close policy; `Play::input` snapshots that mode before dispatching the chat Escape and returns for InfoView even if its close policy does nothing. Dialog-only Escape continues into the HUD broadcast. Regression `flow::tests::escape_info_mode_is_close_only_text_suppresses_and_dialogs_broadcast` covers InfoView retaining selection, text input suppressing both dialog close and deselection, and dialog close sharing target removal.
  **Unresolved outside the deselection fix:** the semantic names of destination `0x13` messages `0x139` / `0x168` have not been established. No attack-stop or movement-stop behavior is inferred from the name `StopAllActionsMessage` or added to this fix.
* **The click** (found): `ActionViewMouseHandler_c` (ctor `FUN_1002c66b`, GUI) connects `WindowController_c` mouse signals: press `FUN_1002c2ee(Point, button, clicks)`, release
  `FUN_1002c469(Point, button)` and `FUN_1002c14d` (ends the look, `FUN_1002c0e5`), and `GlobalSignals` mouse-move `FUN_1002c17b` (accumulates the pointer path in `+0x1c` while a look is
  active, `MouseTurnSensitivity`). Release, with no drag object and `(look inactive || path < 0.02)` (`_DAT_101aeaf4`; units raw counts / 1000 = 20 counts; `play/controls.rs` has the same rule):
  the object under the mouse is `InputConfig_t+0xb0` (set by `CheckObjectUnderMouse`); void (`0x9c47`, `0x9c52`, 0) = **nothing happens (no click-on-ground deselect exists)**. Left button, no
  Ctrl-on-a-character: no Shift → `Send(0x1e, 0x126, N3Msg_GetNextTarget(current target))` (= `n3Camera_t::GetNextTarget` N3 0x10020723: the object after the current target in the camera's
  hit list `+0x244`, wrapping; the first one if the current target is not in it; void → the hovered object), Shift → `InfoViewModule_c::ShowURL("charid://50000/<id>")` / `itemid://` (no
  selection); Ctrl on a character → `Send(0x1e, 0x126, id)` + `N3Msg_SwitchTarget(id)` (= `DefaultAttack(id, true)`). Right button: character → `N3Msg_DefaultActionOnDynel` (Gamecode
  0x100291da: item/corpse pickup, trade, use), else `N3Msg_UseItem`. Press: a left **double click** on an object other than the own character runs `DefaultActionOnDynel` when
  `DoubleclickAction` (LoginPrefs, default true); with `LMBMouseLook` (default true) the left press arms the camera look. Message 0x126 on module 0x1e = `TargetingModule_t::SetTargetMessage`
  (`SetTarget(id, false)`: only if `N3Msg_isIDOnGround(id)`). The deselect `SetTarget(0,0)` (`RemoveTarget` 0x2598b) comes from `FrameProcess` (dynel gone) and from the AFCM messages 0x112/0x113, which
  `FlowControlModule_t::StopAllActionsMessage` (GUI 0x10027de4), `TeleportStartedMessage` (0x1002910e) and Gamecode (`FUN_1006880d`, `FUN_1002f009`, `FUN_1005cb6a`) send; no mouse path.
  `hud_target::{pick_all, object_under, click_target, world_click}` (box pick and modifier branches below).
* The pick (**box pick, derived**; `play/hud_pick.rs`): `InputConverterModule_t::InputGameSubmodeActionViewONMessage` 0x1001bcae (every frame) → `N3Msg_SetMousePos` (Gamecode 0x1001613b) → `n3Camera_t::SetMousePos` (N3 0x10020571:
  ray `(tan(fov/2)·x, tan(fov/2)/aspect·y, 1)` through the camera matrix, scaled by `VisualCamera_t::GetLengthOfViewcone` = far − near) into the camera's `n3CameraCollLine_t`. `n3Playfield_t::RunCameraSelectionCollChecks`
  (N3 0x1000d8d1, every 0.1 s, not for a zero mouse ray) runs an `n3SelectionCollider_t` (a `LocalityWorker_i`, vftable 0x1003d2c8) over the camera locality (radius 200 m, `_DAT_1003ce54`); its worker `FUN_1000fc2b` takes
  every `Vehicle_t` whose body is an `n3VisualDynel_t` and calls `FUN_10020a3c`: the client character only when the camera is third-person (`camera+0x1ec`), the dynel visible, then
  `vtable+0x94(line, BoundingBoxTargeting, &distance)` = **`n3VisualDynel_t::FineCollisionCheck`** (N3 0x19452; GVT over Gamecode: slot 0x94 of `SimpleChar_t`, `Corpse_t`, `Door_t`, `VendingMachine_t`, … vftables). It casts the
  `CollLine_t` to the body mesh: a character is a `VisualCATMesh_t` → `VisualCATMesh_t::IsLineIntersecting` (DisplaySystem 0x100736fe, passes the flag through) → **`RCATMesh_t::IsLineIntersecting` (randy31 0x10056786)**: the line
  goes into the model frame by the inverse of the mesh's world matrix (position, heading **and body scale**); with the flag (`BoundingBoxTargeting`, LoginPrefs.xml default `true`) the test is the ray against the axis aligned box
  `RCATMesh_t+0x1fc` (min) / `+0x208` (max) by `FUN_10050358` (Graphics Gems candidate-plane test: an origin inside the box hits at distance 0, a hit beyond the line length is rejected), the distance is the model-space length
  `|hit − origin|` (`FUN_10058382`). The box is `FUN_1005470d`: min / max over the **skinned vertices of the current pose** of the mesh's own parts (the body; mounted heads and weapons are other meshes), refreshed when the pose version
  (`FUN_10055a23`) changes (`FUN_10055c1c`). Without the flag, the same function retains the box broad phase then walks posed body triangles in native CAT order: `normal·direction <= 0`, plane intersection, two edge-cross-product dot tests against exactly zero (`float` at randy31 `0x1009fda8`), and intersection distance no greater than the model-space line length. It returns the **first** accepted triangle, not the nearest triangle within the body. `ActorRig::body_triangles` preserves that order despite renderer material batching; `Dynels::pick_character_hit` uses the current composed pose only after a broad-phase hit. The live Root Variant `BoundingBoxTargeting` defaults true; false selects this triangle path for hover, click and double-click through `interact_use::pick_objects`. Plain meshes (`VisualMesh_t`: items, doors, vending machines; `FUN_1006bb2a`) ignore the flag and test a bounding sphere
  and the triangles. The hits are pairs `(vehicle, distance)` `std::sort`ed ascending by that distance (`FUN_1000fc8d` → `FUN_1000f8e5`/`FUN_1000ed15`: compare on the float at +4) and copied to `camera+0x244`;
  `GetObjectUnderColLine` (0x1002069c) = the current target if it is in the list, else the first entry. The mouse world position is the line's hit (`+0x190`).
  **Implemented**: `Char::bounds` = `bounds_of` over the vertices of the last `ActorRig::pose` (`dynels.rs`), `Dynels::pick_bodies` → `hud_pick::{PickBody, ray_box, hits}` (the transform is the inverse of `ActorFrame::transform`, the
  line length `lens.far − near`, 799.5 m default), `hud_target::{pick_all, object_under, click_target}`. The capsule guess is gone. Tests: `hud_pick::tests` (box faces, scale, heading, ordering, bind-pose box of a real solitus-female rig, plus box-only empty-space rejection versus triangles, front-face rejection, current pose and model-space scale/line length) and `hud_target::tests`. **Not ported / UNRESOLVED**: the early-out sphere before the box (`RCATMesh_t+0x1cc` / `+0x1d0`, mesh data `+0x5c → +0x14` radius: writers not traced; it can only reject
  rays that miss a sphere around the feet) and the 0.1 s refresh (the list is computed at the click / hover). Own-character third-person selection borrows `Player` at each pick, calls `Avatar::pick_hit` on its retained rendered body pose and inverse actor transform, and merges its native model-space distance with remote bodies and props; first person excludes it (`N3 0x10020a3c`). `ActorFrame::skin` is an `Arc<Vec<Vertex>>`: Avatar and remote Char retain that same allocation after GPU upload consumes its reference, so picking neither copies nor reskins vertices. `avatar::tests::own_body_pick_uses_posed_triangles_and_world_transform` checks shared allocation identity and distinguishes actual own body geometry from empty box space on a transformed retail model.
  Character selection remains character-only; world hover uses `interact_use::pick_objects` to include prop / corpse boxes (docs/zone/interact.md §8).
* **Modifier clicks** (`FUN_1002c469`, see the click above): `HudTarget::world_click(…, mods)` with the modifiers of the new `ao_render::Host::mods` (set by the viewer for every input event, also modifier-only changes): plain → the next
  entry of the hit list (`click_target`); **Shift** → no selection, `WorldClick::Info(id)` of the object under the pointer (`Hud::take_info`; `charid://50000/<id>` requests the server character info packet and renders the original NPC/player HTML through `chat/info.rs`, see `docs/chat/dialogs.md` §1.3);
  **Ctrl / Alt** → that object itself is selected (not the next one) and `flow.rs` attacks it (`N3Msg_SwitchTarget`, `Hud::take_click`); right button / double click (`N3Msg_DefaultActionOnDynel`) are `interact_play.rs`.
  Tests `hud_target::tests::{clicks_walk_through…, modifier_clicks_follow_the_release_handler}`.
* **Hover pointer** (`InputConfig_t::CheckObjectUnderMouse` 0x10019f00 → `MousePointerModule_t::SetMousePointer(Pointer_e, DetailLeft, DetailRight)`, drawn by the software cursor `MousePointerModule_t::FrameProcess` 0x100202dd /
  `FUN_10020120` → `FUN_1001fddd` at `InputConfig_t+0x118`; implemented in `play/hud_cursor.rs`, the rule and all numbers are in its header). Sprite rows (5 dwords `{hotspot x, y, colour 0xffffff, gfx id, sprite*}`): pointers @0x10263168 — `Pointer_e` 0
  STANDARD 0x135, 1 BLUE 0x136, 2 GREEN 0x13a, 3 YELLOW 0x139, 4 PURPLE 0x138, 5 RED 0x13b, 6 GRAY 0x137, then 4WAY_DRAG 0x13c (16,16), NE_SW 0x13d (12,12), NW_SE 0x13e (12,12), VER 0x13f (6,16), HOR 0x140 (16,6), IBEAM 0x141 (5,8), HAND
  0x142 (5,1), WAIT 0x143 (16,16), colour pointers hotspot (0,0); details @0x10263298 — `Detail_e` 0 ATTACK 0x146, 1 PICKUP 0x147, 2 OPERATE 0x144, 3 LOOKAT 0x148, 4 QUESTION 0x149, 5 DENIED 0x145, 6 TALK 0x14a, 7 TRADE 0x14b (hotspot
  (0,0)); overlays LEFTCLICK 0x14c / RIGHTCLICK 0x14d (offset columns (10,20)). All 32×32 except the overlays 16×16 (`Graphics.uvgi`). `FrameProcess` resets the request every frame (`Pointer_e` 0, details −1) and applies `MouseCursorMode`
  (default 2 in LoginPrefs.xml; 0 clears both details, 1 keeps the left detail only and draws no overlays). **Character rule** (decompile of 0x10019f00; `hud_cursor::choose`): the identity must be the one under the mouse (`+0xb0`);
  NPC (`N3Msg_IsNpc` 0x1001666c): `Features` (stat 0xe0) bit 0x20000000 = not attackable or the `Flags` value `(f>>22&2)+(f>>22&1)` = 2 → BLUE (value 2 adds TALK on the right) else RED; player: right detail TRADE, left TRADE with
  `DoubleclickAction`; same non-zero `Team` (stat 6) → GREEN, else same `Side` (0x21): PURPLE if attackable (`Features & 0x4000001` or `HasVulnerableFightMode` 0x10016fea) else YELLOW, other side: RED if attackable else YELLOW; Ctrl →
  left detail ATTACK; Shift (applies to any object) → left detail LOOKAT; the own character: `SetMousePointer(2, −1, −1)`; items / doors by skill 0x1e (bit 0 → BLUE + PICKUP right, left PICKUP with `DoubleclickAction`; else bit 3 → OPERATE
  likewise). This object pointer branch is reachable through `interact_use::pick_objects`, preserving the current target when it is under the pointer, otherwise the nearest hit. The separate 0x100000 → `Send(0x1e, 0x126)` notification is not implemented here. **Placement** (`FUN_1001fddd`): the pointer at `pos − hotspot`, the left detail at +(18, 0) (`IPoint(0x12, 0)`), a different right detail
  27 px further (`IPoint(0x1b, 0)`), the overlays at the detail + (10, 20) (not for DENIED, not in mode 1), with the same detail on both sides the left overlay 9 px left of the right one (`IPoint(−9, 0)`); a negative position hides it.
  **Implemented** in the world only: `HudTarget::draw_cursor` appends the sprites to the frame's draw list (`flow.rs` after the combat overlay) and sets `Host::hide_cursor`, which the viewer applies with `set_cursor_visible` (with
  the mouse look) — over GUI windows and while looking, the OS cursor stays. UNRESOLVED: the overlay offset is [INFERENCE] (the decompile's `IPoint` operands share stack slots), others' `Team` is unknown (never a team mate) and the
  playfield PvP rule behind `HasVulnerableFightMode` (`FUN_1003e228`: 2 sides differ, 3 teams differ, 4 always) was not traced (taken as 0); whether the original also draws its pointer over GUI windows (`ActivatePointerMessage`
  scope) was not traced. Tests `hud_cursor::tests`; live: `hoverdyn=<id>` step prints the pointer sprites.
* The **selection indicator** (found): not a ground decal. `SetTarget` creates `Indicator_t(id, attacking = 0, health = 1)` (`FUN_100255be`, vftable 0x101ae0d4) unless the target has skill flag
  0x400: a world billboard `VisualSprite_t(width/128, 0.3, "[3] TargetIndicatorMat")` (priority 6) over the head anchor `N3Msg_GetIndicatorPosition` (same anchor and text rules as the name tags,
  docs/zone/motion.md §6) showing a 128×32 (256 wide for long text) plate: the left and right half of `GFX_GUI_INDICATOR_SELECTED` (0xe6, corner brackets; 0xe5 `…_ATTACKING` for the attacked
  dynel) at its ends, the tag line (`FUN_10024e14`: `** name **` for GM flags, `= name =`, titles, breed, colour by flags) in font 2, and the 64×4 health bar of `FUN_10024c03`
  (y 14..18, `ftol(Health/MaxHealth·64)` px, rest 0x333333). Implemented by `play/tags.rs` through `Dynels::name_tags`: world-space camera-facing actors, perspective-scaled,
  with the `Consider` colour on the bar only. Selection and attacking indicators share this path; no extra projected-GUI label is drawn. Org line data remains unavailable.

### 13.3 Target controls (`CCTargetControl_c`, GUI 0x100746ec; `play/hud_target.rs`)
* `ControlCenterModule_c::CreateTargetMenus` 0x1006a0d4 builds two controls (friendly = `0x154` flag 0, hostile = 1) twice: the **dock** version (`LeftTargetCtrlDock` /
  `RightTargetCtrlDock` of `ControlCenter.xml`, `Rect(10,0,0,36)`/`Rect(0,0,10,36)`) = `HLayout[left arrow 8×41 (0x93/0x95/0x94), target button 48×48 toggle (0xaa/0xac/0xab) with a
  centred icon (friendly 0xae `TARGET_ICON_SELF` 38², hostile 0xad `…_OTHER` 40²), right arrow (0x96/0x98/0x97)]` (`Button_c::SetGfx(state 0 normal, 1 pressed = …STATE3, 2 hover =
  …STATE2)`), and the **bar window** `CCFriendlyHealthBar` / `CCHostileHealthBar` (`CharBarWindow_c`, style 3, flags 0xe3c, criteria `dvalue:cc_section1 &&
  dvalue:cc_{friendly,hostile}_health_bar`) at `y = 5`, `x = (screenW − 192)·{½·½, ½·3/2} − width/2`. Constructor 0x100746ec sets the **maximum interior width** `(screenW − 192)/2 − 50`, not the active displayed width.
  `TargetHealthBar_c::SetTarget` 0x1007313a (assembly 0x10073202–0x10073249) reads stat 1 and computes `floor(64 + (maxWidth−64)·sqrt(2·sqrt(MaxHealth))·0.01)`, clamped to `[64,maxWidth]`;
  64 is float at 0x101b5ebc, 0.01 is double at 0x101b5c60. Preferred-size code 0x100734bc–0x1007352b adds the two 9-pixel caps. This explains the retail small top-centre bar without invented fixed widths.
* Bar window = `TargetHealthBar_c` (`FUN_10073598`; `GFX_GUI_TARGET_HB_LEFT/RIGHT` 9×11 caps, background DEFAULT, background and slider tiled 16×11 with Load flags 0x1f/0x10) above the target's name.
  `TargetHeader_c` 0x10073884 is called with `!barWindow`: only the **dock** version creates corner frame 0x88/0x89/0x86/0x87 and bold caption; top windows have neither, just name directly below the bar.
  Slider tint is `N3Msg_Consider` at 0x1007313a, reusing `nametag::{consider_ratio,con_color}` (GUI 0x10024af1 / Gamecode 0x10017496). Background starts one pixel after the left cap and ends one pixel before the right cap (0x10073598).
  Default `Graphics.uvga` PNG IHDRs at offsets `1090174+16` (background) and `1090936+16` (slider) both encode 16×11. The port emits source-bounded tiles for both: an oversized background source rectangle sampled adjacent cap entries in the GUI atlas, producing false separated segments as the slider depleted. Regression: `health_bar_tiles_stay_inside_the_atlas_entry` checks full/half/low/empty fills and contiguous, ≤16-pixel source spans.
  `FUN_10072e49` tints caps `0xff2222` only when attacking flag `+0x161` and in-attack-range boolean `+0x162` are both true; 0x1007353f receives the latter from `GlobalSignals+0x64`.
  Gamecode 0x10004711 / 0x100047a0 emit true/false; 0x10068969 tests occupied
  attack slots 8/6 (bare-hand slot 0 only when neither is occupied), via
  0x100687e5 → 0x100679c1. This is **not a blink**. The producer reads the
  selected identity at targeting-controller +0x5c, not the fighting target;
  absent selection leaves the global signal unchanged.
  0x100679c1 rejects self/different playfield and compares centre distance
  minus cached character radii strictly below slot-owner range (objects
  subtract only the owner's radius). Cached radii (`SimpleChar+0x1ec → +0x30`)
  begin at 1 metre (`0x10044702`) and are updated by `0x10044c46` /
  `0x10059e6a` from integer CharRadius (421, mode2) times
  max(MonsterScale (360, mode3), 1), divided by 100. They are not CAT torso
  spheres. Wire stats override retained NPC template stats. Range changes
  now repaint stationary bars as well as moving targets.
  Missing CharRadius retains the constructor's cached 1-metre radius directly,
  rather than inventing a backing-stat default and scaling it.
  Range helper 0x1009a68e reads item `AttackRange` 0x11f mode 2 and owner `RangeIncreaserWeapon` 0x17c mode 0; assembly 0x1009a6c8–0x1009a6e8 computes item range plus scaled percentage bonus, converts to integer, caps at 40.
* **Logout label (bug6):** `TimerBar` constructor `0x100512ae` creates its
  TextLine in font1 (TOOLTIP) at local IPoint(0,1), not a centered NORMAL
  label. The port now follows that font/origin and retains the native art.
* Which control shows a target: attackable → hostile, otherwise friendly. Attackable (`FUN_100744ae`): NPC and `Side` (stat 0x21) differs from the own side; a player needs
  `N3Msg_CanAttack` (not modelled).
* `FUN_10073d0f`, `FUN_100744ae`, `FUN_1007460c`: friendly control shows a nonattackable selection only if it differs from fight target. Hostile control shows fight target plus a distinct attackable selection.
  Equal character selection/fight merges into `"Nano / Fighting<br>Target"`; otherwise character captions are `"Nano Target"` and `"Fighting Target"`. Non-character selection uses `"Selection"` on the friendly control (`FUN_10073d0f`, kind != 50000; `FUN_100744ae` clears attackability). Fight name is red `0xff4444`, selection name white. Object bar tint remains white (`FUN_1007313a`, `Consider != 3`); its MaxHealth/Health use object stats. Hostile top constructor swaps the two row pointers, placing fight first.
* **Dock handlers** (disassembled, GUI): left arrow `LAB_10072fcb` = `AFCM::Send(0x1e, flag ? 0x106 : 0x105)` (`GetPrev{Hostile,Friendly}TargetMessage`), right arrow
  `LAB_10072ff1` = `Send(0x1e, flag ? 0xe9 : 0xe8)` (`GetNext…`), target button `LAB_10073017` = flag ? `N3Msg_PerformSpecialAction(0xb)` (**Attack**,
  docs/zone/combat-net.md §5.3; routed by `Hud::take_slot_use`) : `TargetingModule_t::SelectSelf`. (`FUN_1007303d` only sets the button's toggle value from a `GlobalSignals` int.)
  `hud_target::cycle` is `N3Msg_GetCloseTarget` (Gamecode 0x1001c411, see §13.2).
* **No tooltips**: `FUN_100746ec` (`CCTargetControl_c`), `FUN_10073598` (`TargetHealthBar_c`), `FUN_10073884` (`TargetHeader_c`) and `FUN_10075342` (`TargetTargetButton_c`) never call
  `View::SetToolTip` (searched every decompiled function 0x10072000–0x10075fff: the only `SetToolTip` is `ToolbarButton_c` `FUN_10072b33`), so the target windows show none.
* **Target-of-target button** (`TargetTargetButton_c`, `FUN_10075342`; first child of the *hostile* bar window, `View` flags 0x100): `BorderView` with the header's frame gfx
  (0x88/0x89/0x86/0x87), `SetColor(0x7fffff)`, a vertical layout, caption `"Fighting Target:"` (`TextView`, borders 3,3,3,0) and the name (borders 3,0,3,3), window border 5 below.
  `FUN_10073b9e(show)`: only when the pref `Targetstarget` (`LoginPrefs.xml`, "Show Target's Target", default **false**) is set and `N3Msg_GetTargetTarget(target)` (the fight
  controller target of the selected dynel, `SimpleChar+0x1d4` `+0x4c/0x50`; Gamecode 0x1001641d) is non-zero: name = `N3Msg_GetName`, button shown with the window. Click `LAB_10073554`:
  if `N3Msg_CanClickTargetTarget(target, targetsTarget)` (Gamecode 0x10016451, `hud_target::can_click_target_target`) → `Send(0x1e, 0x126, targetsTarget)` = select it. Implemented
  (`HudTarget::targets_target`, `tot`, `Zone::fight_target` from the relayed `AttackIIR_t` / `StopFightIIR_t`); the button follows the displayed primary target, not necessarily the selection.
  Caption colour `0xff4444` of `FUN_10073d0f` belongs to the whole control's fight header; target-of-target caption keeps default text colour.
* NOT implemented: the NCU windows (`NanoTargetNCUWindowConfig`, `FightTargetNCUWindowConfig`). Red cap prerequisite: expose the effective attack-slot range and both actual retail collision radii to the HUD; neither is currently stored in `Armory::Item` / `Zone`. Caps retain DEFAULT rather than inventing a range or radius.
* Data: `Zone.target` (instance id), `Zone.fight_target`, centrally synchronized `DynelState { side, level, health, max_health }`; own health/max/level and consider range use effective `Zone::skill_value` (mode 2 as in 0x1007313a / 0x100744ae), not raw stat deltas. Tests in
  `hud_target.rs` (ray math, box pick, hit-list click cycling, close-target metric, `SelectSelf`, target-of-target, windows follow selection; `target_health_width_tint_and_control_routing` covers the derived width, con tint, merged/distinct control identities, and central health consumption).
  `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR` writes `target-hostile.png`, `target-friendly.png`; dock arrows use the corrected id table (§1).


## 12. Map windows (`play/hud_map.rs`, `ao_formats::{planetmap, topdown, landcontrol}`, `ao-gui` `CanvasView`)

Both windows are built in code in GUI.dll (no `Views/*.xml`): `ControlCenterModule_c::SetupProviders` 0x10068c38 registers `planetmap_window` (text `#WindowPlanetMap`, `WINDOW_PLANETMAP`) and `map_window` (`#WindowMap`, `WINDOW_MAP`); menu entries `ActionMenu/MapMenu.xml`. Help text (`text/help/The Map Window.html`, `The PlanetMap Window.html`): Map = **Ctrl+6**, Planet Map = **P**; yellow dot = you; map upgrades turn it into an arrow and show machines/monsters/people; drag pans, moving the character re-centres; zoom in/out and "centre on character/mission/shop" buttons on the planet map. Hotkeys: §12.1.

**Planet map** (`PlanetMapView_c` ctor 0x1004d26a): default client 300×400 (`_DAT_101b183c/1840`), first-time frame `Rect(100,100,500,500)`; buttons `ZoomIn`/`ZoomOut`/`Character`/`Quest` (`Button_c`, art `GFX_GUI_PLANETMAP_ZOOM_IN/_OUT/CENTER_PLAYER/CENTER_MISSION`, 27×27), marker `GFX_GUI_PLANETMAP_PLAYER_MARKER`. Index file option `PlanetMapIndexFile` default `Normal/PlanetMapIndexNormal.txt` (`ShadowlandMapIndexFile` → `Shadowlands/ShadowlandsMap.txt`). `FUN_1004bc1f`: zoom level clamped to `0..levels`, ZoomIn disabled at the last level, ZoomOut at 0; initial level 0. Marker position `FUN_1004b81a` (see formats.md *Planet map*); marker hidden when the playfield has no coordinates entry (indoor playfields: 4604, 4582-style).
Planet map without a coordinates entry starts at **scroll zero**, not at the entire atlas's centre. `0x1004bc1f` constructs `BitmapTileView` via`0x1004bb02`/`View::View` (`0x1014e084`, data ctor`0x1014fa1e`) with zero-initialized scroll points; `0x1004b6d4` does not scroll when its player marker is hidden. The port initializes its centre-representation to half the viewport, producing origin`(0,0)`. Regression `planet_map_unmapped_playfield_starts_at_zero_scroll` covers first open in4582.
**Playfield map** (`PlayfieldMapView_c` 0x100eb905 → `ScrollView_c` + `PFMapRenderer_c` 0x100eaebe + `MapControlView_c` 0x100ec4e0): default client 179×199; "Map Not Available" text (`FUN_100eacda`) when no ground map and no `GetMapCharacters` success. Icon atlas (`FUN_100e9ba2`): 16×16 cells of gfx 0x10a.. (MAP_ARROW, ARROW_SELECTED, BIG_ARROW, towers). `MapData_c` asks `GetMapCharacters(range 500)` (`FUN_100425eb`), `GetLandControlBitmap`, `GetCurrentRoom` (`FUN_100428e1`/`FUN_100427a1`: current room white, visited grey 50 %, unexplored black — implemented). Heading = `atan2` of `GetGlobalCharacterRotation` (`FUN_100e9f73`).
**Implemented**: native-tabbed Planet Map / rollup PF Map, tiles, zoom/pan/player and mission markers, Shadowlands pick, terrain image reused from the existing world loader, and room exploration lighting. Terrain scale is 4px/cell. Planet tooltips come from GUI `0x1004de3c..0x1004df61`: `Click2ZoomEtc` 193692387, `Click2ZoomOutEtc` 159921171, `CenterOnChar` 64930882, `CenterOnMission` 128361390. Button labels are `PlanetMap_ZoomIn/ZoomOut/CentreOnChar/CentreOnQuest` (31206174/230869204/253005794/22653524), with normal/pressed skin art. Double-click left/right changes level through `0x1004b447/1004b468`; `0x1004b422` stores the clicked point for `0x1004bc1f`. Mission-centre `0x1004b72b` follows marker handler `0x1004b9c9`; `0x1004bfbf` clears it. The player arrow's actual gate, hidden two-row controls and LC overlay are documented below. World-loader scene reuse (`Bg::Ground→Hud::provide_ground`) avoids decoding the playfield again. Existing `hud_map`, `hud_compass` and ao-gui canvas regressions cover these consumers; no new screenshot/live verification is claimed.
**Remaining map uncertainties**: dungeon room-map scale (terrain uses decoded4px/cell); pairing of pressed art by skin name; double-click interval (500ms/4px host convention); unused shop-centre art (ctor creates only four buttons); wheel zoom is the port's addition. PF first-time position is **resolved**: ctor`0x100eb905` calls DockableView config loader`0x10038c98` with `"RollupArea"`, using the existing rollup dock, not an invented free position. Map ownership is proven in§12.2; mission markers have real producers in§11.12.
  Own-marker gate correction: `PFMapRenderer` main View vtable is`0x101c0724`; draw slot`+0xf4=0x100eb216` always calls`0x100e92ef` for own position. That helper selects dot/arrow from MapData`+0x81`, which `0x100425eb` sets to1 unconditionally after polling. MapNavigation140 is not a gate; the steady-state marker is the heading arrow even at zero. Regression `own_heading_arrow_does_not_require_map_navigation` checks0/1/100 against actual canvas marker art.
  Planet controls reconstructed: ctor`0x1004d26a` builds two HLayout rows, each with a zero-minimum/16000-maximum weight1 spacer, two27px buttons and an equal spacer. ZoomIn/Out form row1; Character/Quest row2. `ShowButtons` defaultsfalse and the frame's literal `"Show buttons"` check menu reveals/hides both rows. Drawing, hit testing and tooltip rectangles use each row's actual27px vertical interval and live width. Existing resize, real-input zoom, pressed-art and tooltip regressions now explicitly reveal the native-default-hidden controls.
`N3Msg_GetLCDistrictInfo` GC`0x1001cf60` resolves the own character's zone through the existing `PlayfieldDistrictInfo_t`, returns its name`+8` and level range`+0x44/+0x48`. `FightLevels::lc_district` now exposes that canonical Districts/ZoneLocator result; Player publishes `Zone.lc_district` only when the tuple changes, while deriving the existing `own_lca` gate from the same lookup without repeated parsing or per-frame name allocation. The authored-district regression checks the exact name/range tuple against the mapped district.
**Engine additions**: `<CanvasView>` / `Kind::Canvas` / `Gui::set_canvas` / `canvas_size` / `canvas_items` / `open_framed_window_xml` / `open_tabbed_window[_xml]` (§6.1), `Gui::set_canvas_tips(w, name, Vec<CanvasTip>)` (tooltip rectangles of a canvas: the tooltip search of §13.1 returns `(view, rectangle)`, so moving from one rectangle to another restarts the 500 ms timer), events `CanvasDrag` (now with the pointer position), `CanvasPress { button, clicks }` (left and right, `clicks` 2 on the second press within `DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME`), `CanvasRelease`, `CanvasWheel`, `CanvasClick` (`gui/canvas.rs`); `Gui::text_focused()`.

### 12.1 Window hotkeys (`play/hud_keys.rs`, `Hud::input`)
The `WINDOW_*` providers of `ControlCenterModule_c::SetupProviders` 0x10068c38 (provider key = `hash_combine` of the name, `controls::provider_hash`) have default bindings in `CharPrefs.xml` `KeyBindings` (`Input` = key id | SHIFT 0x20000 / CTRL 0x40000 / ALT 0x80000; digits `1..9` = 108.., `0` = 117, letters 82..). All matched by hash (test `hud_keys::tests::shipped_bindings_resolve_windows_and_bar_keys`) and equal to the help texts (`text/help/The * Window.html`): **`I` Inventory (90), `U` Skills (102), `P` Planet Map (97), Ctrl+1 Wear (108), Ctrl+2 Special actions (109), Ctrl+3 Knowledge (110), Ctrl+4 Mission (111), Ctrl+5 Team (112), Ctrl+6 Map (113), Ctrl+7 Friends (114), Ctrl+8 Nano/Programs (115), Ctrl+9 Stats (116), Ctrl+0 NCU (117)**, Shift+Ctrl+R raid (`WINDOW_RAID` 393315), Shift+O research (131168), Shift+F LFT (131159), Shift+T `WINDOW_TS` (131173), `Y` shortcut bar (106), `F10` options (48). The fixed command table (GUI.dll text, `[ & AnyPlayMode ! TextInputMode ]`) adds **Shift+P Perks** (`KEY_OPEN_PERK_WINDOW`), Shift+V vehicles, Shift+C chat config, Shift+`|` control-centre toggle. We wire the ones with a `WindowKind` (Inventory, Skills, Planet Map, Perks, Wear, Mission, Team, Map, Friends, Nano, NCU) to `Hud::toggle`; the others have no window yet. The saved `KeyBindings` of a character (rebinding) is not read for windows (defaults only). `! TextInputMode`: `Gui::text_focused()` (a focused text field, e.g. the chat input) swallows them. `ao_gui::Key::Letter` also carries digits; `ao_render::viewer` sends a `Key` press before the `Text` of a key that types a character, and drops the OS key repeat of `Key` events so a held key toggles once.

**Knowledge/CreateRef roundtrip:** `WINDOW_KNOWLEDGE` resolves to the native `knowledge_window` consumer. `ACTION_CREATEREF` follows GUI `SlotCreateReference` `10068119`: reject NPC current references, request the server's existing InfoPacket through `InfoTextCreator_c` `10031011`, and retain the returned client-local query ID. Requests share the ordinary character-info transport; no cached/local character reference is manufactured. Player formatter `10033db4` emits every pending query for the matching reply identity and erases those queries. `SlotCreateCharacterReference` `1006a882` accepts only the latest pending CreateRef ID, clears it before rechecking NPC status, and sends headline + dated row + response body to `RefMan_t::AddCharRef`.

The dated row is exactly `<div indent=wrapped><font color=CCInfoHeader>Dated: </font><font color=CCInfoText>…</font></div>`. Surprising native detail preserved, not corrected speculatively: GUI assembly `1006a8eb..1006a8f3` pushes flags `0`, timestamp high `0`, timestamp low `2`, then the hidden result buffer. Utils `GetDateString` `10001399` uses localtime and flags-zero format `%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S`, so the date is local Unix epoch + two seconds, **not now**. `create_reference_waits_for_matching_player_info_and_uses_response_stats` is an offline packet regression for no local snapshot, unrelated-response rejection, authoritative reply stats, dated-row placement, one-shot completion, and NPC rejection. Test execution is reported by the combined feature gate owner.

**Shortcut/tradeskill provider consumers:** GUI `SetupProviders` `10068c38` pairs `WINDOW_SHORTCUTBAR` with `shortcutbar_window` (`10068e8e..10068f1b`) and `WINDOW_TS` with `tradeskill_window` (`1006983c..100698b7`). Both now use the existing module-DValue toggle route: shortcut bars retain their objects/content while visibility changes, and the interaction layer opens/closes the real `TradeskillPanel.xml` analyzer. No replacement window/template is introduced. Regressions `module_hotkeys_reach_real_shortcut_and_tradeskill_consumers` and `module_window_and_function_key_row_bindings_resolve` cover saved bindings through both consumers and F5 bound to `SHORTUCT_BAR_ROW_7`.

**Daily Login prerequisite GAP (not a key-only feature):** native `WINDOW_DL` maps to `dailylogin_window` (`100698c5..1006994c`). `BrowserModule_c::SlotDailyLoginWindowActivated` `1010afea` constructs/deletes `BrowserWindow_c` mode 4; constructor `10110f19` titles it “Daily Login Rewards” and, when `Client_t::s_nEventServerType == 0`, resets that DValue false and skips initialization/navigation. Otherwise `SlotHomeButtonPressed` `1010f8f8` uses `GetHomeURL` `1010f881` → `https://daily.project-rk.com/`; `BrowserView_c::NavigateTo` `1010d3a7` invokes `awe_webview_load_url`. `BrowserModule_c::Reinitialize` `1010b838` initializes Awesomium with `AwesomiumProcess.exe`, a character-owned `Browser` cache/cookie directory, and cookie clearing. The port has neither an embedded webview backend nor the event-server-type browser gate. Therefore this provider is intentionally **not** registered as a DValue-only no-op or redirected to an external browser. A functioning embedded browser/session integration is the missing prerequisite; the native URL and lifecycle are recovered, not guessed. No live endpoint/login probing was performed.

**Port focus-loss reset (bug 3b):** the observed source path in `ao-render/viewer.rs` previously cleared only host fly keys and cursor look on `WindowEvent::Focused(false)`. `play/controls.rs` retained held keys, modifiers and pending/active look, so a movement press after refocusing was discarded as a repeat. The viewer now forwards `GameInput::FocusLost` and clears host modifiers; `Play::game_input` routes this cancellation even while new game input is disabled. `Player` clears its GUI-button press ownership, and `Controls::on_focus_lost` reuses `release_one` and `end_look` to stop held movement/camera inputs without generating a click or attack. Text-field focus is unchanged. This is a port source diagnosis, not newly established retail focus-loss evidence. Regression tests: `controls::tests::focus_loss_releases_movement_camera_and_modifiers` and `focus_loss_cancels_pending_look_and_releases_remapped_turn`.

### 12.2 Who owns a map (`ao_formats::map_areas`, `data/map_areas.txt`, wired into `hud_map.rs`)
**Ownership and scale.** The 128-entry table (GC `0x100324d2`, assembly `0x10033c51..0x10034c3f`) is `data/map_areas.txt`; `map_areas::owns_map` applies it. `N3Msg_GetMapCharacters` (GC `0x1001bf31`) requires `0x10057ca5`; GUI `0x100425eb` stores success at MapData+0x80. Availability `0x100eacda` is `owns || (RenderLandControlData && bitmap_present)`. Without either, show native MapNotAvailable; character entries require ownership even when LC makes the map visible. `0x100e923a` only sets centre; `0x10042996/100e9729` prove 4px/terrain-cell, or `4/Report::cell_size` px/metre. Dungeon scale remains unresolved 1.0. First-time placement is the existing RollupArea dock (`0x10038c98→1003a26f`), not a new free window.

**Land-control overlay implemented.** The native main View vtable is `0x101c0724`; slot+0xf0 points to `0x100eb328`, not the unrelated targets obtained from SignalTarget's vtable. It reads the static rdb 1000008 bitmap (LSB-first, stride ceil(width/8)), flips rows and writes A4R4G4B4 texels: set bit `0xf392`; unset owned `0x4000`, unowned `0xf555`. Randy `0x100262dc` proves TextureFormat10=A4R4G4B4. RGBA expansion is therefore (51,153,34,255), (0,0,0,68), (85,85,85,255). The port caches the authored full-cell texture, draws it at world origin 0 with 4px/cell and ordinary SRCALPHA/INVSRCALPHA blending; native UV scale is 1/128 (GUI `0x101ae108`). Unowned maps additionally draw `0x100e9ebb`'s 64×64 map-locked mask (one green row/column, texel `0x83e0`, UV scale 1/64 at `0x101c0838`); DisplaySystem `0x1007b8e2` Format2 uses bit15 alpha. No overlay/marker placement was invented.

**Map controls and district text.** `MapControlView_c` `0x100ec4e0` creates Info/Build/War, 5px flexible spacers, then coordinate inputs and literal Add/Remove Waypoint, 3px borders. The outer controls use weight0 and collapse while hidden; `PFMapWindowConfig.ShowButtons` defaults false (`0x100eb905`), menu `0x100eb854` is literal "Show buttons". Info toggles `RenderLandControlData` (default false), gated with Build/War by effective Expansion389&1. Tooltip keys ShowLConMap/OpenLCbuild/OpenLCwar and labels Info/Build/Plafieldmap_War are native. Info starts immediate/500ms district refresh (`0x100eb1b2/100ea734`); current authored LC name/range is drawn at (5,5), with `"%s<br>Lvl Rng: %d - %d"` only when both bounds>0. Add Waypoint `0x100ec302` uses C-atof positive X/Z and current PF; bad coordinates emit literal "Coordinates not in range.", color 0x52. Remove emits +0x15c zero identity. Both map ShowButtons values and RenderLandControlData persist while preserving unrelated archive/window fields (`0x100eb72c`, PlanetMapViewConfig).

**Native-dormant Build/War boundary, not a fabricated opener.** `0x100ec048/100ec0dc` emit GlobalSignals+0x170 true/false. Full scalar 0x170 scans found no observer in GUI (119 hits), Interfaces (10), Gamecode (36), AnarchyOnline (0), GameData (0), Utils (0), N3 (4, camera only), SandyInterface (6, audio only). The connection-offset convention was verified at `0x1004e4b1` (+0x158). Existing TowerBuildMenuView `0x1005c35b` subscribes other signals, not +0x170. The buttons retain that shipped dormant behavior; no fake generic TowerUi handler was added.

Regressions: `native_land_control_texels_and_flipped_rows` checks exact owned/unowned texels and orientation; `land_control_controls_availability_district_poll_and_waypoint_signals` exercises expansion gate, bitmap-based availability, cached raster, 500ms district changes, dormant Build/War, positive C-atof coordinates, error color and remove; `native_map_flags_roundtrip_without_losing_window_fields` preserves geometry/ZoomLev and native flags.

Headless final workspace verification (2026-10-16): `cargo test --release --workspace -j2` and `cargo clippy --release --workspace --all-targets -j2 -- -D warnings` both passed with the private target. Map LC/control/config, native viewport, zero-scroll, own-arrow and mission cell/order/feedback/waypoint regressions passed. The final compile repair uses ao-gui's existing `Clicked { window, view, item }` event convention. No login or desktop-window verification was attempted.
After additive integration of Progress/CRT/ActionStates, release-focused `play::hud_map::` (10 passed), `play::hud_mission::` (6 passed), `play::fightmode::` (2 passed) and strict all-targets workspace clippy passed. Existing nano-feedback handling and the upstream post-movement district-publication point were preserved.

**Ctrl+2 Actions window and the special-action consumers: done, see §10.5 / §10.7** (`play/hud_actionwin.rs`).

Old RE notes:
`FUN_10057ca5(n3Dynel_t*)` [Gamecode 0x10057ca5]: `true` if stat `GmLevel` (215) ≠ 0; `false` in playfield 8060 (0x1f7c) or without a playfield; otherwise the playfield id is looked up in a static table (`FUN_100366ce`, filled by `FUN_100324d2`, 128 entries of `{playfield id → area index or -1, name}`): index −1 or an absent playfield → `true`, else bit `idx % 32` of stat `MapAreaPart1` 471 (idx 0–31), `MapAreaPart2` 472 (32–63), `MapAreaPart3` 585 (64–95), `MapAreaPart4` 586 (96–127). `N3Msg_GetMapString` [0x1001ad38] prints the owned areas (`FUN_10036714(idx)` = name, "Missing map: %u" otherwise, text 0x3ed "NoMapArea" when all four stats are 0). All Rubi-Ka outdoor playfields (505 Avalon … 665 Broken Shores, 566 Newland City, …) have index −1 (always owned); only Shadowlands areas have indices (e.g. 4001 Jobe Research 79, 4310–4313 Nascense 80–83, 4006/4320/4321 Penumbra 84–86, 4880/4881 Scheol 87/88, 4872 Adonis 89, 4683–4699 the Gardens 98–115, 4561 a Jobe shop 70; the first parse found 103 of the 128 entries, 32 with an index). The function has no direct caller (virtual); that it feeds the `+0x80` "has map" flag read by `FUN_100eacda` is **not proven**.


## 11.8 Skill rules (`ao_formats::stats::skills`, tested against the client's rdb) — HudStats2
`GetSkillMax` `FUN_100651b5`, `GetSkillCost` `FUN_10061fdb`, `GetSkillCostLevel` `FUN_10062398`, ability max `FUN_100626d8`, trickle-down `FUN_10064ac2`, `N3Msg_GetSkill` mode 1 `FUN_100654e1` [GC] are ported; the float math is read from the asm (`FUN_1013ecf0` = truncation). Tables (little-endian i32 streams, loaders `FUN_100c646d` dispatch): rdb 1000206[prof] = 77 cost factors in the stat order `COST_ORDER` (`FUN_100c4afd`); 1000209[class 1..5] = `T1..T7`, `B`; 1000200[prof] = title-level start levels `s[4(t-1)]` = 1,15,50,100,150,190,205; 1000204[stat 100..168] = 6 trickle percentages; 1000027[breed] = base/cap abilities; 1000210[breed] = ability cost class (stream order Str,Int,Agi,Sen,Sta,Psy). Cost of one more point from value n: skills `trunc(f·n/10)`, abilities `class·n` (`FUN_100f90ef`). Max skill = min of the T/B/title-level terms, `8·trickle`, … (see code). **UNRESOLVED**: modifier/buff containers (`FUN_1006460b`, `FUN_10063d39`, mask-4 map `FUN_1008a3e8`) – buffed = base.
* **Save Changes** (`FUN_100fa5dd`) → `N3Msg_ClientIPAdjust` [GC 0x10026e7e] → `SkillIIR_t` `3E205660`, pass-on byte 0, body `i32 n; (i32 stat, i32 pending+raw)*` ascending: `ao_net::n3::outgoing::skill_ip_adjust` (byte test).
* **Reset**: "Reset all skills" confirms, then `N3Msg_ResetSkill(0)`; "Reset this skill" → `N3Msg_ResetSkill(stat)` = `CharacterActionIIR_t` action `0x9a`. Layout **verified from the disassembly of [GC 0x1001cdae]**: the pushes before
  `FUN_1007253f(hdr, identity_a, param, action, identity_b, text)` are `""`, `&{0, stat}`, `0x9a`, `0`, `&{0, 0}` ⇒ `identity_a = {0,0}`, `param = 0`, `identity_b = {kind 0, instance stat}`, pass-on 0, `SendIIRToObservers`.
  `ao_net::n3::action::reset_skill(char_id, stat)` (byte test `client_bytes`). Texts (text.mdb 502): "Do you really want to reset the skill %s?" (89070509), the full-reset question (109178077), "Reset all skills" (96620988).

## 11.9 Stat window `stat_window` (`StatView_c` `FUN_1007ff6e`) and inventory data
Menu `CommandMenu.xml` entry `stat_window` (SetValue toggle), key Ctrl+9 (`WINDOW_STAT`, `hud_keys.rs`, hash-checked against `CharPrefs.xml`), `WindowKind::Stat`. Layout/handlers: `play/hud_stats/stat_view.rs` (header "Name:/Level:", bars health 27/1, nano 214/221, XP 350/57/54/52 (`GUI 0x1007fd80`), alien XP 40/178 if Expansion&0x18, PvP 684/682/683 with `pvp_score_for_rank`, rows 22,91,90,92,96,93,94,95,97 from effective `Zone.skill_value`, refreshed every frame even with Skills closed). Labels use a centered HLayout over each PowerBar for both-axis centering, not alignment on a stacked full-frame child. Tests: `hud_stats::tests::stat_window_follows_the_own_stats` (modifier refresh, XP StatIIR and label geometry).
**Stats settings and retail four-bar baseline.** The actual registry default is the empty `cd_image/gui/Default/CharPrefs.xml:109` `StatViewConfig` archive, not the install's `prefs/NewChar/Prefs.xml:905–911` all-true saved template. `StatView_c` reads five independent booleans in menu order: `AIXPBar`, `XPBar`, `DuelScoreBar`, `TeamScoreBar`, `SoloScoreBar` (`GUI 0x100800c7–0x1008011b`). The original constructor does **not** initialize the five bytes at `this+0x1ec..0x1f0`; its factory `0x1002d565` calls the CRT `operator new` thunk `0x10173896`, and base constructor `0x10038b47` only initializes base members through `+0x198`. `Utils::Message::FindBool` `0x10008fe6` returns −1 without writing its output when the field is absent. Consequently no deterministic missing-key default can be recovered from this build.

The port's explicit missing-key policy matches the retail screenshot's four-bar count: health and nano always shown, XP/alien XP/duel off, solo/team on. **[INFERENCE]** The two `0 / 2000` rows are assigned to Solo/Team: all three zero-score PvP bars have identical art and labels, so pixels alone cannot identify which two were enabled. This is a retail-screenshot-derived baseline, **not** a claim that the uninitialized original bytes have these values. Stored fields override it independently. Right-clicking Stats exposes the original literal menu labels and order (`0x10082714`: “AI XP Bar”, “XP Bar”, “Duel Score Bar”, “Team Score Bar”, “Solo Score Bar”); AI XP is offered only when its expansion-gated view exists (`0x1007f7b3`). Toggling shows/collapses its own row and refits (`0x1007f878`, `0x1007f7f8`), and writes all five fields back into `StatViewConfig`, retaining dock/window fields (original destructor save `0x1007f652`: `AddBool` then `SetDValue`). The HUD consumes registry changes live and the existing character-preference save path persists them.

Regression checks: `stat_view_archive_roundtrip_preserves_independent_flags_and_dock_fields` (independent keys and archive save/load), `stat_view_config_menu_controls_bars_and_persists` (GUI menu toggles all five, refit, save/load/reopen, and Expansion gate). The offscreen test emits `stats-default.png` and `stats-configured.png` when `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR` is set.
Stats content is top-aligned and capped to its current natural preferred height (`Gui::fit_view_height`, using the existing layout preferred-size calculation). The dock's saved outer page height remains intact, but excess page space no longer stretches the bars or AC rows. The GUI regression also checks that health/nano bar pitch remains unchanged when the optional bar count changes.

The shared model recomputes attack rating from buffed MartialArts 100 + AMSModifier 276 when
unarmed (`GC 0x10068359`), or the weapon's separately truncated skill-weight terms (`0x10081c67`).
`HudStats` computes raw pool maxima from buffed BodyDevelopment/NanoPool (`0x1006208d`) and
publishes effective values before the remaining HUD consumers. The new-character wire capture
contains raw Life/MaxNano 1, absent AMS 22 and NextXP 1450: raw wire fields alone are not the
displayed derived values.
The same effective projection feeds the wing/bar callbacks (`GUI 0x100666b6`, `0x100667a8`,
`0x100668aa`, `0x100669f7`), NCU title (`0x100d6d14`, stats 180/181), faction
(`0x1005d68c`), own Team pools (`0x100775ac`) and Perk expansion (`0x100611dc`).
Voice expansion/breed/sex (`GUI 0x100b82c2`), `/rp` VisualFlags (`0x100b21dd`),
tip level (`0x100b6f0d`) and alien-bar expansion (`0x1006afed`) also use effective values.
Perk-point level counts remain binary-proven raw (`GC 0x10052bfc`, `0x10052ca3`).
`N3Msg_GetSkill(mode 2)` must not be confused with the direct stat-holder vtable's mode 2:
the latter reads raw values; its mode 0 routes through `0x10058e52` to effective `0x1006554c`.
Thus direct raw AggDef (`GC 0x10026c2f`), map gates (`0x10057ca5`), vehicle/sneak
(`0x100293c0`) and crawl-state gates (`0x100278c9`) retain their original raw semantics.
Regression `ncu_modifier_updates_real_hud_and_window_title` applies MaxNCU +12 and checks
the real wing and NCU title while leaving raw MaxNCU unchanged.
Inventory data: `FullCharacter` inventory is decoded (`InventoryEntry{slot,a,b,id,AcgItem{low,high,level}}`, `Zone.inventory`, replaced by each `FullCharacterIIR_t`; layout from `FUN_1002a41a/1002a04a`, `GameData::operator>>(ACGItem_t)` 0x1000e9d7; no capture has items → synthetic entries in the tests, real rdb records). Slots: 0..0x3f equipment pages, bag from 0x40 (30 slots); cell id→slot: weapons `id` (Right/Left hand 6/8), armor `0x10+id`, implants `0x20+id` (loop 0x20..0x2f `FUN_10046e35`), social `0x30+id` (hands 13/15 → 0x3d/0x3f, `FUN_10047873`). Wear tab tables (corrects §11.4): weapons = order `1,15,2,3..14`, armor `1..15`, implants `1..13`, social `1..15`. Item icon = rdb 1000020[low_id] stat `Icon` (79) → rdb 1010008 PNG (48×48), the code path of the hotbar icons (`hud_bar::icon_image`).

## 11.10 Skills window behaviour (`play/hud_stats.rs`, `hud_stats/skill_model.rs`) — HudStats3
