Everything below is read from GUI.dll (`/tmp/aomac-ghidra/dsky/gui`) unless marked INFERENCE.
* **Row** = `StatRow` `FUN_100fe956` (ButtonBase over a hidden-border `BorderView`, HLayout): name `TextView` (left border 3, colour `FUN_100f8f93` by `GetSkillCostLevel`: `<6` 0x7dcc5e, `<11` 0x00cc88, `<16` 0x00cccc, 16..20 0x0088cc, `>20` 0x075aff), spacer, value `TextView`, the two arrow `Button_c`s (view with borders 3, order dec, inc), then for info rows a `PowerbarView_c(0xe0 SMALL_EMPTY, 0xe1 SMALL_BLUE, dir right)` (right border 5). Fields: `+0x1a4` stat, `+0x1a8` `GetSkill(stat,2)`, `+0x1ac` raw `GetSkill(stat,0)`, `+0x1b0` pending, `+0x1b4` `GetSkillMax`, `+0x1b8` last price, `+0x1cc` disabled (group 10: stats 138/140).
  Arrow art `FUN_100f8ff1`: no pending ⇒ `GFX_GUI_INC_SKILL` 0x1d3 / `DEC_SKILL` 0x1d5, pending ≠ 0 ⇒ `…_CHANGED` 0x1d4 / 0x1d6 (same image for normal/pressed/hover). We paint both arrows on one `CanvasView` per row (`btn<stat>`, left half dec, right half inc; the engine's `Button` has no custom art).
* **`FUN_100fde49(delta)`** (the one place pending changes): `pending += delta`; price `FUN_100f90ef` = Σ_{i<pending} `trunc(GetSkillCost(stat, raw + i))`; if `delta ≠ 0` ask the window slot with `price - lastPrice` (`FUN_100fce6a`): refused ⇒ one point is taken back and the feedback `Skill_LackIP` (text.mdb 502: "You lack the IP needed to increase further in this skill.") is emitted; a larger delta is cut point by point until accepted. Abilities (0x10..0x15, row visible) also call `N3Msg_SetSkillTmp(stat, pending)` (trickle-down and maxima see the pending ability points, `Model::character`). Value text = `pending + value`, bar = `(raw + pending) / max`.
  **The IP slot `FUN_100fa185`**: `rem = remaining - price; if (0 < rem) { remaining = rem; true }` — the window never lets the last IP be spent (strict `>`, ported literally). `FUN_100fa7ba` (stat `IP` 0x35 changed): `remaining = newIP - (oldIP - remaining)`; any other stat refreshes that row (`FUN_100fe418`, `FUN_100fe478`: when the maximum shrank take surplus pending points back). `Model::remaining` = server IP − Σ row prices.
  Increase button `FUN_100fe261`: disabled row ⇒ literal text "This skill has been disabled and can not be increased."; `max < pending+1+raw` ⇒ `FUN_100fd5b9` (`N3Msg_SkillMaxFeedback`); else `FUN_100fde49(1)` + 500 ms timer (then every 50 ms `FUN_100fe5d7`: `can_increase` (`FUN_100f908e`) ? +1 : max feedback). Decrease `FUN_100fe0ca`: `pending-1 >= 0` ⇒ `FUN_100fde49(-1)` (+ timers). Button release with a qualifier key (`View::GetQualifiers ≠ 0`): increase ⇒ `FUN_100fde49(max - pending - raw)`, decrease ⇒ `FUN_100fde49(-pending)`.
  Implemented through the actual raw input → `HudStats::input` → `Gui::input` → `CanvasPress` / `CanvasRelease` path, not synthetic `CanvasClick` modifiers. `skills_input` preserves the current Host qualifiers on every input, so a modifier changed while held is sampled at release. Press applies one point immediately; captured holds repeat after 500 ms and every 50 ms, including at the minimum/maximum; release anywhere stops capture. Release handlers `100fe361` / `100fe1b4` stop the timer and apply bulk max/undo for any qualifier; disabled rows never start the timer or bulk adjust. `skill_arrows_press_repeat_release_and_qualifier_bulk` covers raw GUI input, timing boundaries, outside release, Shift/Ctrl/Alt bulk changes, max feedback and no implicit save (authored, not exercised in this sweep).
* **Maximum feedback** is Gamecode `10064cde`, not GUI at that address. GUI `100fd5b9` passes `(stat, output string, raw + pending + 1)` and emits the result through row signal `+0x18c`. The port reuses `SkillTables` cap/cost/title data: skills test actual buffed value against the title cap first (`Feedback_ReachedYourTitleMax`), then per-level/title-limited allowance (`Feedback_MaximumIncreaseInSkill`, argument B or 5 above level 200); abilities test absolute cap first (`Feedback_AbsoluteAbilityMax`), then level cap (`Feedback_MaximumIncreaseInAbility`, argument 3 or cost-class 20/15/10 above level 200); otherwise positive `trunc(trickle(mask 6) * 8)` reached produces `Feedback_BaseAbilitiesTooLow`. Mask 6 adds pending abilities but not their spell bonuses (`10064ac2`). Category 110 is resolved by `1003807c`; integer insertion uses the existing LDB formatter, with no authored feedback prose. Data addresses `10160170/198/1bc/1d8/200` identify those exact keys. Formula regressions cover the five branches.
* **Details panel** `FUN_100f97a6` (row click): `statName` `<center>`GetText(0x7d2)`</center>`, `statdesc` GetText(0x7d1), `buffed` = `+0x1a8 + pending`, `base` = `GetSkill(stat,1) + pending`, `pointdelta` = pending, `unitcost` = `GetSkillCost(stat, raw + pending)` (float, `FUN_100f90cd`), `totalcost` = `FUN_100f90ef`, `maxskill` = `GetSkill(stat,1) + (max - raw)`, all `<div align="right">`; `disabledLbl` only for group-10 stats; `resetselect` page 0 (non-disabled: "Remaining reset points" = `GetSkill(199 RP, 2)` + "Reset this skill") / page 1 ("Reset this skill (free)").
* **Save** `FUN_100fa5dd` → `skill_ip_adjust` (§11.8), then `FUN_100f91d7` clears pending points. PRK replies with **`SkillIIR_t`**, not `StatIIR_t`: Gamecode `0x10079494` applies its signed map through target stat setter `vtable+0x40`; the inbound `Dynel::Skill` updates character values and remaining IP. **Reset**: GUI `100f9247` / `100f9392` constructs the shared DialogBox with literal names `SkillResetAll` / `SkillReset`, category502 questions and **Yes/No**, not OK/Cancel. Body borders15,5,15,20; right-packed buttons have8px side borders and5px bottom margin (`1012aa23`/`1012ad5f`). HUD dialogs use glyph-area aspect2 wrapping (`101623ea`), centre final preferred dimensions, capture `esc_dialogs` on opening and have no implicit Enter default (`10156885` initializes default=null; creation/Go never installs one). Only answer0 sends ResetSkill; No/Escape/frame-close do not. Published Progress5f9ed24 fixed skill arithmetic/arrow state, not this former dialog gap. `native_100f9392_individual_reset_samples_escape_and_waits_for_yes` and the existing full-reset mouse fixture passed in the retained UI gate below.
  Regression `hud_stats::tests::skills_pending_points_save_and_reset` also requires the enabled Save Changes button to remain inside the outer window after selecting a skill and populating its details (`Skills.xml:189–193` places Save/Close after the info area). It retains the real mouse-input save/reset checks; selector layout repairs belong in `ao-gui`, not in IP arithmetic or synthetic event bypasses.
  Gamecode `FUN_10064ac2` distinguishes actual abilities plus bonuses (mask 3, skill modes 1/2) from temporary ability increases (mask 4, maximum calculation). Pending abilities therefore raise dependent skill caps, but do not change another row's Base/Total until the server applies them. Regression `pending_abilities_raise_maximum_without_changing_mode_one_or_two_values` covers this distinction. `FullCharacter::stat` follows native `0x10073a2f` application order (a, b, byte, signed short), last valid value wins and sentinel `0x499602d2` is ignored; `full_character_stat_application_order` covers duplicate IP and signed short values.
  Live capture `docs/captures/skill_ip_adjust_ithaca.rec` records Aomacfixr (identity 33588) saving Strength and Agility 6→7: the ascending outgoing map at 112285 ms is echoed at 112478 ms, followed by an authoritative three-entry map `{16:7,17:7,53:9476}`. The pre-fix real window displayed pending values 7/7 and 9476 IP, then incorrectly reverted to 6/6 and 9500 after Save because the inbound class was not decoded/applied.
  Regression `skills_close_discards_pending_and_save_waits_for_server_confirmation` uses real GUI mouse events through the full HUD: the IP notice opens Skills and clears `got_ip`; Close and frame-X reject pending changes without sending or mutating server stats; Save Changes sends one frame without predicting stats; own `SkillIIR`/`StatIIR` refresh displayed Base/Total/IP, foreign `SkillIIR` remains foreign. `skill_ip_reply_captured` decodes both exact captured server replies and rejects malformed counts.
  Fixed-client live verification used the offscreen harness after the user reserved the Mac: real-window acceptance **not done (user using Mac)**. On relog the frame showed Strength 7, Agility 7 and 9476 IP (the earlier real-window save persisted). Increasing Strength once showed 8 and 9462 pending IP; Save restored normal arrow art while retaining 8/9462, and `watch` logged `16: Some(7)->8 53: Some(9476)->9462`. A second clean login frame again showed Strength 8, Agility 7, 9462 IP. Both runs completed `/camp` to the login screen before their processes exited and the live lock was released. The final three capture records retain the exact Strength 8 save/echo/IP reply.
* **Suggested IP distribution** `FUN_100faec0` → `FUN_100fac49` (`suggest_ip`, available until level 20): `RecommendIPUse` (`FUN_100f8d69`) loads `data/ipdist.xml`; per row `FUN_100f8e0d(level, profession)` finds `<profession name=text 0x7d4[profession id]>` / `<stat name=text 0x7d3[stat]>` / the `levelrange` containing the level ⇒ `pri`; rows are bucketed in a `std::map<pri, vector<row>>` (`FUN_100ff5d1`), the bucket 0 is skipped, buckets walked in ascending order; inside a bucket a cursor walks the rows round-robin: `FUN_100fe478` (refresh maximum) then `FUN_100f908e` false ⇒ erase the row, else `FUN_100fde49(1)` — refused (no IP) ends the **whole** loop — and the stat is recorded (once) for the list. The `autodist` panel (`infoselect` page 2) shows the recorded names in `col1` / `col2` (first half / second half). `Model::suggest`; test `suggested_ip_distribution_spends_by_priority` on the captured level 1 Soldier (real rdb tables): all six abilities reach the level-1 maximum 9 (+3), Str/Agi/Sta/Body Dev. raised, 310 IP of the 1500 left because every pri > 0 row hit its maximum; `model_on_real_tables`: Body Development 5 → 8 costs 5 + 5 + 8 = 18 IP (`trunc(5.5·n/…)` per point), maximum 10.
* **Feedback display**: row signal `+0x18c` (`100fcefd`) is connected for both full and compact rows by the SkillWindow constructor `100fc18e` to slot `100f919e`; its assembly finds child `"status"` (`101a99d0`, xref `100f91b4`) and forwards the emitted string to TextView setter `10164d7b`. Thus `Skill_LackIP`, disabled-row literals and max feedback use the existing status view, not chat or a new dialog.
* **Compact Minimized mode** is implemented using the shipped `<prefix>_view` containers, not a replacement layout. `100faaa0` adds literal icon-menu checkbox `"Minimized view"` (`101c1ef8`); `100fa1ab` hides `info_area`, `top_line`, `remaining_ip_lbl` and closes any expanded inline group when switching modes. `100f9552` expands at most one inline group and collapses it when clicked again. The two `StatRow` instances per stat in `100fc18e` differ by constructor argument `+0x1c4`: compact rows omit the Powerbar (`100fe956`), but retain names, values and arrow behavior. Both handles now display the same pending model, including timer and qualifier changes, so mode changes do not lose IP edits. `100fab51` saves Bool `"Minimized"` in `"SkillConfig"` and `100fc18e` restores it; the port preserves other archive fields and uses the existing window-config subsystem for frame/pin persistence (§6.5). `compact_skills_toggle_inline_rows_preserve_pending_and_save_config` covers raw pointer menu selection, group expansion/collapse, compact arrows, pending transfer and saved Bool. No new real-window or automated verification was run in this sweep.
  Compact rows require a vertical client inside each empty shipped `BorderView` (`Skills.xml:22–53`), not direct attachment to the border's fill-all base layout. Without that client, every ability occupied the same bounds despite distinct handles and correct values. The skill builder now creates that client before appending rows; preferred height comes from the child rows, with no hardcoded row spacing. The compact GUI regression additionally requires six distinct subtree handles and six nonempty, vertically ordered, nonoverlapping arrow rectangles after the real Abilities click.
  Row labels retain their instance-local `"name"` and native handle-based event dispatch. A window-global `"name"` lookup resolves the hidden compact label before the visible full row; fixtures must instead use `Gui::frame_in` with that stat's visible `ViewHandle`, translated by the window client origin. Both full-HUD and stats fixtures now click the scoped Strength label without weakening their point-delta assertions; the compact regression additionally clicks scoped Agility and Strength labels in each mode and requires the actual selected stat and its correct pending delta. The coordinate helper is test-only; production names and event routing are unchanged.
* **Buffed values: traced and implemented (§11.14).** The server sends the stored (unmodified) stat; the client adds the bonus / percent maps `FUN_1006460b` / `FUN_10063d39` read, which the spell executor `FUN_100026d0` fills from the `FullCharacterIIR_t` spell list and `ApplySpellsIIR_t` (writers `FUN_100644c8` / `FUN_1006469b` / `FUN_1006497b` / `FUN_10064999`, callers `FUN_100a4c24`, `FUN_100a87b5`, `FUN_100a88ee`, `FUN_100a8a96`). Value colour: `FUN_100fde49` compares `GetSkill(stat,2)` with `GetSkill(stat,1)`: white `0xFFFFFF` equal, green `0x00FF00` when buffed > base, red `0xFF0000` when lower. Not covered: worn items' Wear events and quality interpolation (UNRESOLVED, §11.14).

## 11.11 Stat / wear / inventory windows: contents and placement — HudStats3
* **Default windows** (`prefs/NewChar/Prefs.xml` `<Value name="stat_window" value="true">`, `wear_window` true; `skill_window` / `inventory_window` false; also `nano_window`, `ncu_window`, `team_view` true, not implemented here): `Hud::new` opens wear and stat. The template docks both in the `RollupArea` (`DockAreas/RollupArea.xml`: pages friends 215 px, wear 317, nano 205, stat 279 = the 4th page; `StatViewConfig` / `WearViewConfig` `DockableViewDockName = "RollupArea"`); the dock pages are **not implemented** (§10), so the wear window stands at `(W - 225, 20)` (the rollup column, `InitialiseMessage` 0x1006a968) and the stat window below it. The inventory is `DockArea0` (`DockTabbedWindow`, `WindowFrame` Rect(1196,636,1382,811) = 187 × 176, `selected_tab` -1); we open a free window at that position, clamped like `MoveInsideScreen` (the template comes from a bigger screen). Skills: `Rect(200,180)` (§11.3).
* **Wear items:** see §11.4 for the native BG1/BG2 assets, cell bounds, tab selection and front/back label toggle. The item canvas uses full ACG+flags redraw signatures, so quality/high-endpoint and disabled changes update a stationary grid.
* **Inventory:** 30 bag slots0x40..0x5d; list Icon16 / Name200 / Count30 / Quality100. Owned Count is `InventoryEntry.b as u16` (GC1002a7d2), never MaxEnergy212 or shop MultipleCount412. Grid spacing/layout follow §11.5 and `inv_grid` (`101349f6`, `10136423`); no Wear9px vertical gap remains. Title, icon, list name, carried-item metadata, loot/container titles, trade/tradeskill callbacks and shortcut drops now use `Items::info(AcgItem)` keyed by `(low,high,level)`, reusing `interpolate_item_templates`. Native GUI1004178f gets title via GC10019b0e→10049772→1002a82b, while GUI1003eb30 reads Icon79 from the instance. GC1007cd23/10082a3d load the nearest template, ties high; metadata selection precedes stat-QL normalization100cc270. The threshold/default regression covers QL0/1/midpoint/high/512/-1; no visual-mesh rule was extrapolated to names.
* **Split / stack** (`hud_stats/item_ui.rs`, `item_dnd.rs`, `zone_inv.rs`): GUI `10040f22` enters count selection on Ctrl-left (qualifier mask `0xc`) over count > 1; `100412a0` adds truncated horizontal pointer displacement to an initial 1, clamps to `1..count-1`, and paints a yellow count in a transient style-3 text window at the press origin. `10040fd1` releases selection into the carried item drag with `split_count`; this is not an invented numeric-entry dialog. `100cbc21` accepts that drag only onto an empty inventory-grid cell (list mode uses the first free position); ordinary same-template/same-QL stackable drops join onto the target. GC `N3Msg_SplitItem` `1001bc8a` rejects Can bit `0x1000000`, invalid split count and a full inventory, then calls `10049e8f` **locally before** constructing CharacterAction `0x34` (`a=item,b={0,count}`) and `SendIIRToObservers`. `10049e8f` decrements the source and clones its ACG/count into the first free bag slot >= `0x40`, emitting local inventory signals. The port queues one `(Identity,count)` from the accepted Stats split; `Hud::input` drains it through the existing `Zone::split_inventory_item` before forwarding the outgoing frame. Existing pending-drop placement puts that new entry at the selected visual cell. This is the native pre-send mutation, not a synthetic acknowledgement or guessed optimistic update. GC `N3Msg_JoinItems` `1001baa1` requires Can `0x200` on both items, matching low/high IDs and QL, and a combined count at most 50000; it performs **no local mutation**. Join remains CharacterAction `0x35` (`a=target,b=dragged`), applied only on a real own reply through `1005d50e` -> `10049cfe`. Incoming split dispatch `1005d4f6` -> `10049e8f` remains unchanged; no speculative echo deduplication is introduced.
  Confirmed loot `ContainerAdd` uses GC `1004a7b3` / `1002a5ff` / `1002a2bc`: automatic destinations first search the bag for an identical ACG entry with template-entry bit 1, only when source Can is stackable and not cannot-split; merging up to 50000 works even with no free bag slot. An oversized candidate follows the native cell-swap move instead of overflowing. Item Can metadata uses a separate 1000020 cache sharing the world's existing RecordStore; nano templates are 1040005 and must not be conflated.
  Can/Flags lookup takes the complete owned `AcgItem`, not just `low_id`: it caches the existing `interpolate_item_templates` result by `(low_id,high_id,level)`, preserving GC `10082a3d` / `100cc270` nearest-endpoint metadata (ties select high) and rejecting missing/malformed endpoints without a low-template fallback. Regression `acg_endpoint_can_and_flags_follow_quality_and_tie_high_for_stack_eligibility` uses different endpoint Can/NoDrop flags and checks low, midpoint and high QLs; it was not run by this worker.
  Inventory UI split/join continues to use `Items::acg_stat`'s canonical full-ACG EquipCriteria cache; presentation metadata has its own full-ACG icon/name cache, not a low-ID fallback. `ShopInfo`'s outward tuple and all native eligibility predicates remain unchanged.
  Evidence correcting the prior wait-for-split-echo assumption: `/tmp/LiveCheck8/split-join-final-redacted.rec` sent CharacterAction52 at 19784 ms and had no incoming52/InventoryUpdate through 44000 ms; the server-persisted split appeared on relog as 299+1 from 300. `/tmp/LiveCheck8/join-wire-acceptance.log` records the actual server-confirmed join 1+299 -> 300. The old stats-only regression injected its own split request as an incoming frame, hiding the missing `Play::input` -> `Hud::input` local mutation boundary. Unrun regression sources now include `play_ctrl_split_mutates_locally_without_echo_and_join_waits_for_ack` (real Play input, 300 -> 299+1 immediately, one outgoing0x34, no incoming split, selected-cell placement, then join unchanged until incoming0x35), `ctrl_horizontal_selection_queues_local_split_then_drag_rejoins_on_server_reply`, `confirmed_loot_joins_full_bag_only_with_native_can_and_matching_acg`, `split_local_mutation_and_join_confirmation_reject_invalid_or_full_moves`, and `split_count_must_leave_one_and_respect_cannot_split`. No build/test/formatter/live/window/input verification was performed for this correction. The count selector queues a logical-window pointer warp back to its origin (`100412a0`); the synthesized origin event contributes zero to the count.
  `Hud::take_pointer_warp` forwards this one-shot request during `Play::input` into `Host::pointer_warp`. The viewer drains it after input/frame cursor synchronization, converts GUI pixels to backing pixels using the same interface scale as `CursorMoved`, and calls only its owned window's `set_cursor_position` while focused and outside mouse-look capture. Unfocused/captured requests are discarded, not replayed after focus returns; a successful warp updates the viewer's cached position for subsequent button events.

Setter1002a64f sets/clears0x40 and marks the slot dirty; all three discovered callers (10047d77 twice,1004a7b3 once) clear it during bag moves/takes. No native true-call writer was found; incoming wire flags remain authoritative.

That function checks the requested class (implant tab also accepts class5; Social accepts only classes1/2), gates **class3 only** with `10044b6e(0x10)` = own `Features` stat224 bit0x10, then dispatches weapons to virtual+64 (`10162a88 -> 1008198d`, criteria action8/Wield), other items to virtual+5c (`10162a80 -> 10081815`, action6/Wear). `10081815` additionally requires Can30 bit4 and checks social-armor incompatibility via `100805b5`. Drag/drop preview and the common move dispatcher share this gate; double/right click and hotbar follow the same DefaultPos dispatch. Generic usable items still send command3 and await server confirmation; no local inventory mutation. Nonweapons require Can30 bit4 and reject own Features224 bit`0x800`; weapons use their Unwield stream instead. `1004b80a` final move validation also evaluates the displaced item's removal requirements. Its assembly `1004c765..1004c82b` refuses occupied implant slots entirely, allows class5 only for Profession60=15 and class3 only for other professions, and checks Features`0x800` before wearing armor/implants. `1004c534` refuses already-worn items crossing into/out of Social. `1004c59b` refuses equipping a source stack count>1. `1004c570` requires temporary/overflow cell0 free (`10048829` identifies inventory+0x10 as kind`0x6e`; `1002a1b0(0)` scans for the first free cell). Belt slot7 cannot be worn or removed while deck slots9..14 contain items. All these checks now run through the same shared move dispatcher and drag preview; actual inventory mutations remain server-only.
* **Dual wield and Social combat are resolved, not server fallbacks:** `1004c6e1..1004c723` checks occupied opposite slot pairs6/8 and13/15 via `10068e28`. `10080541` rejects either AnimationSet353 in {2,3,6,8}; `10080568` uses the opposite item's stat420<1 to select MultiMelee101, otherwise MultiRanged134. Both requirements must be nonzero/non-invalid; `10068eaa..10068efb` / `10068fbd..10069013` compare the smaller pair requirement to the character's **raw mode0** skill, inclusive `>=`. If the opposite slot is the source object itself, the native identity-equality branch accepts it. Social wield/unwield (`1008198d`/`10081a4a`) require idle fight-controller `SimpleChar+1d4/+44 == 1` and stat410=0. That controller is already represented by `Zone::fight_target`; writers are `10067fa6`, `10069c68` and `10068b7f`, documented in `docs/zone/combat-log.md`. No unknown mode fallback remains.
* **Move busy gates:** assembly `1004b9c9..1004b9f3` refuses packet-backed moves while timer kinds6 or3 exist. Kind6 is the singleton created by `10047fde`, with ACG-resolved moved/displaced EquipDelay summed by `1004aed0`; it now gates requests as well as drawing the optional timer bar. Kind3 is the nano timer, not ordinary item use (`10050ed9`, PUSH3 at `10051098`); `HudStats::set_nano_cast_busy` consumes that canonical state from the HUD. Ordinary item use's kind4 is not substituted for kind3. Inventory reads completed-perk predicates93/103 directly from canonical `Zone::completed_perks`, with no mirrored cache or HUD setter. Unknown (`None`) is an explicit error when the selected Wear/Wield/Unwear/Unwield stream needs completion predicates, not an empty completed set; streams without those predicates remain usable before that snapshot arrives. Regression `unknown_completed_state_only_errors_for_completion_predicates` distinguishes unknown, known-empty and known-containing42. ## 14. List widgets: `DropdownMenu_c`, `ListViewBase_c`, `MultiListView_c` (`ao-gui` `gui/listview.rs`, `gui/hscroll.rs`, `widgets.rs`) — ChatWidgets

GUI.dll evidence (addresses = functions of GUI.dll, decompiled with Ghidra; XML factory registrations in `FUN_1012fa0b`: **`DropdownMenu`** = `DropdownMenu_c::Instantiate`, **`StringListView`** = `ScrolledListView_c::Instantiate`; `MultiListView_c` has **no** XML element, the code news it).

### Generic View mouse subscriptions
`Gui::set_view_mouse(window, name, enabled)` subscribes an existing view to `Event::ViewMouse` (native button 1/2) and `Event::ViewHover` (entered/left). Subscription defaults off, changes no art or native view flags, and does not capture the pointer or turn a plain row into a button. Child hits resolve to the nearest subscribed ancestor; changing hit children inside one row does not produce a spurious leave/enter.

Native evidence: `View` XML constructor 0x1014e21c creates the down signal at +0x74 and move signal at +0x7c, reading `view_flags` with the caller's default rather than adding a mouse flag. `Window::_FindView` 0x1015472a walks top children and tests visibility/intersection, not enabled state. `Window::_HandleMouseDown` 0x10154bda emits +0x74 before the virtual handler; `View::MouseDown` 0x1014c84b converts coordinates and emits the parent's +0x74 before forwarding. Neither generic path captures the mouse. `Window::_CallMouseMoved` 0x101559b3 emits +0x7c before the virtual move handler and tracks parent runs. Research's plain-row controller 0x100f556d connects down 0x100f5263 and hover-description 0x100f4c2c. The port exposes only explicitly subscribed application signals to preserve existing unrelated view behavior.

### 14.1 `DropdownMenu_c` (0x1012b7ae..0x1012c6ca; XML `<DropdownMenu name=..>`, used by `LFTView.xml` and `SignupTerminal.xml`)
* Layout: a `View` with an `HLayoutNode` (`Initialize` 0x1012bf10) holding **(1)** a `BorderView` (`SetGfx(6, 7, 8)` = `GFX_GUI_DROPDOWNMENU_LEFT` 10×22 / `_MIDDLE` 16×22 / `_RIGHT` 5×22, `SetLocalColor(DEFAULT)`; 3-slice mode of `BorderView_c::SetGfx(int,int,int)` 0x10125c1b: left = edge slot, middle = background slot, right = edge slot; its preferred size is at least `left + 1 + right + 1` × the art height, `CalculatePreferredSize` 0x10126517) whose client is the read-only label `TextView` with client borders **(6, 3, 6, 3)** (`_DAT_101b5234` = 6, `_DAT_101a96d4` = 3), and **(2)** a `BitmapView` with `AddBitmap(4)` / `(5)` = `GFX_GUI_DROPDOWNMENU_CLOSED` / `_OPEN` (26×22 each), `SetLocalColor(DEFAULT)`. Disabled: `View::SetColor(0x909090)` (enabled 0xffffff, `EnabledStateChanged` 0x1012b8b8).
* The label's min/max preferred size is `(_DAT_101c5d04 = 70, -1)` while empty and `(widest item text, -1)` otherwise (`RecalcMaxStringWidth` 0x1012ba12, `InsertItem` 0x1012c578, `Clear` 0x1012c4d4, `DeleteItem` 0x1012c3f9); `SetMaxStringWidth(w >= 0)` 0x1012bc2f fixes it (`+0x140`), a negative value follows the items again.
* Items (0x30 bytes: `Variant` id + `String`): `InsertItem(index, id, text)` clamps `index` to the count and selects item 0 (with the signal) while nothing is selected; `AppendItem`; `SelectByIndex(i, emit)` 0x1012bcda sets the label (out of range = empty, selection -1) and raises the signal (`+0x128`) when `emit`; `SelectByID` = `FindItemByID` + `SelectByIndex`; `GetSelection`, `GetCurrentText`, `GetItemID`.
* Mouse: `MouseDown` 0x1012be94: enabled and button 1 and popup closed → `Open(true)`; other buttons delegate to `View::MouseDown`. `SlotMenuOpening` 0x1012bb68 sets arrow OPEN and fills `PopupMenu_c(1)`; `SlotMenuOpened` 0x1012b7df places it at **(left+5,bottom+1)** via `Reposition`; close restores CLOSED and clears entries; selection **0x1012bed8** calls `SelectByIndex(id,true)`. The port uses `Gui::open_menu`, with the native PopupMenu skin described in §7.
* API: `Gui::dropdown_{insert, append, clear, delete, select_index, select_id, selected, selected_id, text, set_max_width}(window, name, ..)`; event `DropdownChanged { window, view, index, id }`.

### 14.2 `ListViewBase_c` / `ScrolledListView_c` / `StringListViewItem_c` (XML `<StringListView>`)
* `ScrolledListView_c(TiXmlElement*, int)` 0x10131dea = a `ScrollView_c` (attributes of `ScrollView`: `v_scrollbar_mode`, `h_scrollbar_mode`, ...) whose client is a `ListViewBase_c` (`SetClient(list, null)`), so the list scrolls through `ViewScrolled` 0x10131edb → `_Layout` (only the visible items get views, `_FindVisibleItems` 0x1013189c). The port builds `ScrollView > ScrollViewChild > List` and gives the list a `min_size` equal to its content (the same visible effect).
* `ListViewBase_c` (ctor 0x1013163b, `Initialize` 0x10130f7d: single select, select on mouse down): a tree of `ListViewBaseItem_c` under an always-open root (`SetRoot` 0x10131ee5). Item fields (ctor 0x10132339): `+0x30` id Variant, `+0x40` folder, `+0x41` open (default **closed**; the root is forced open), `+0x42` selectable (default true), `+0x43` selected, `+0x48/0x4c` size (extent), `+0x50` extent incl. open children, `+0x54` offset in the parent's children area, `+0x58` indent of the children (`_DAT_101b00e0` = **15**). Rows are contiguous (`offset(next) = offset + extent + 1.0`); an item's x is the sum of its ancestors' indents.
* `MouseDown` 0x101312c6 (any button): `FindChild(y)` → a folder toggles `OpenFolder(!open)`; with select-on-down (always) `Select(item, multi ? !selected : true, emit)` (`ListViewBaseItem_c::Select` 0x101324f5: needs `selectable`, single selection first deselects the current one with its signal); then the item-mouse signal (`+0x14c`, 5 args: item, view, local point, button, flags) fires. Other signals: `ItemSelected` 0x1013118d (`+0x148` item + bool; `+0x150` when multi or selected; also the `Message` `item_id` / `is_selected`). There is **no keyboard handling** (no key / wheel overrides in `ListViewBase_c`); the wheel is the `ScrollView`'s.
* `StringListViewItem_c(Variant id, String text, int icon, int icon2)` 0x1014502d: label colours `+0x84 = 0xffffff`, `+0x88 = 0xc0c0c0`; `SetLabelColor(a, b)` 0x10144c51. Item view `StrLstItemView_c` (`FUN_10144d0f`/`FUN_10144aab`): HLayout of `BitmapView` (icon) + spacer (5 px when there is an icon, `_DAT_101a8b98`) + `TextView` + `HLayoutSpacer`. Text colour: selectable and not selected → `+0x88`, otherwise (selected or not selectable, e.g. folders) `+0x84`. Icon: `icon` alone when `icon2 <= 0`; else plain items show `icon` while selected / `icon2` otherwise, folders `icon` while open / `icon2` while closed. `HideIcon(true)` hides the bitmap; `FlashIcon(true)` runs an `EventTimer_c(500000 µs)` toggling its visibility (**0.5 s half period**, confirms the earlier GUESS). The friends window folders use `(0xd5, 0xd4)` = `GFX_GUI_EXPAND_BUTTON_OPEN` / `_CLOSED` (11×11), i.e. open icon first: the former "order is a GUESS" is resolved.
* API: `Gui::list_{add, clear, set_multi, remove_children, update, item, open_folder, select, selected}(window, name, ..)`, item type `view::ListItem`; events `ListSelected`, `ListItemMouse { button, clicks, x, y }` (`clicks` = 2 for a repeated press on the same row within `DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME`; the original's double click is not part of this view).

### 14.3 `MultiListView_c` (list layout; 0x10133350..0x10137a98) and `ColumnHeaderView_c`
* Ctor 0x10136423: a `View` holding `ColumnHeaderView_c` (when not grid mode; `UpdateHeaderView` 0x101374ae) above a `ScrollView_c(2, 2)` (both bars automatic, `SetSnapToScrollWheelStep`) whose client is the item view. Defaults: column gap `+0x1a0` = `_DAT_101a8b98` = **5**, row gap `+0x1a4` = `_DAT_101a96d4` = **3**; total row width `+0x260` starts at `-(5 + 1)` and grows by `width + 1.0 + 5` per visible column. `AddColumn(id, label, width, flags)` 0x101378a7: flags bit 0 hidden, 1 resizable (`2`), 2 label (`4`), 3 sortable (`8`); the first sortable column becomes the sort column (order 0 = ascending). The LFT window (`FUN_100f03fb`) builds `MultiListView_c(Rect, 0x40 = single selection, 0, 0)`, `SetLayoutMode(1)` (list), six columns with flags `0xe` and both scroll modes 2.
* Rows (`LayoutList` 0x1013520e, `AddItem` 0x10135b85): `vpos(next) = vpos + height + 1.0 + 3`; `LFTCandidateItem_c` (ctor 0x100f1456, vtable 0x101c0ef0) has height `_DAT_101b00e0` = **15** (vtable +0x18) → 16 px rows on a **19 px pitch**. Its row view `StringListColView_c` (`FUN_100f1842`) holds a `TextView` per column (`FUN_100f18f8`) laid out by `FUN_100f171e` at `x += width + 1.0 + 5`; a selected row gets a `ViewSurface_c` of colour **0x88aadd** behind the cells (`FUN_100f15ef`). Compare (vtable +4, `FUN_100ef4a1`): columns 0, 2, 3, 4 `std::string::compare`, column 1 the integer level, column 5 equal. `AddItem(.., sorted = true)` inserts behind the equal rows at the sort position while the list is sorted (`+0x16c`), else appends and clears the flag.
* Inventory image cells: `InventoryListViewMultiColView_c::Layout` (`FUN_1003e3ee`) creates column 0's `ItemIconView_c` via `FUN_1003dca1` and assigns `Rect(0,0,_DAT_101b00e0,_DAT_101b00e0)` at the column x: **16×16 pixels**, independent of resized column width. `FUN_1003fa39` loads the icon surface (flag `0x80` for RDB icons); `FUN_1003f2ea` stretches it to the view bounds. `MultiCell::image(GfxId)` carries image content separately from `MultiKey` sorting metadata, accepts skin and `Gui::add_image` images, and draws the full source into that frame. Wider columns retain their original widths and use the existing automatic horizontal bar/header offset; ScrollView content clips to its client viewport, excluding scrollbar tracks. Regression: `multi_image_cells_scroll_with_original_wide_columns`.
* Selection: `MultiListViewItem_c::Select(sel, emit)` 0x1013b4b3 → `ItemSelectionStateChanged` 0x1013476a (needs flags `0x40` / `0x80`; unless `0x80` the current selection is cleared without a signal; signal `+0x144` when `emit`). The view does **not** select on a click by itself: `MouseDown` 0x10133b63 captures the mouse and fires the 5-arg mouse signal (grid pos, item, button, drag object, flags); the LFT window connects `FUN_100efacd`, which calls `Select(true, true)` of the row (the port's `MultiMouse` event + `multi_select`).
* Header layout **0x1013a516**, ctor **0x1013a8c3**: visible columns and trailing empty button follow row widths/gap and horizontal scroll. Buttons **0x10139a17** use progress01 art `155/157/150/152/153/156/154/151/14f`, label inset3, preferred height18 (19 pixels; **0x10139b82**). Mouse **0x1013b098**: within2px of a resizable edge, drag resizes; double-click calls **ResizeColumnToFit 0x10135a0b**, which takes max row preferred extent, excluding header. Inventory virtual **0x1003d2bf** returns image extent15 and text/numeric `GetStringSize` pixel width−1; the port now preserves both instead of cloning strings and measuring image cells as empty text. Regression `double_click_column_edge_fits_row_extents_not_header_or_icon_text` drives real header clicks and checks resize events. Elsewhere, release within3px selects/sorts (**0x1013746c**), drag swaps columns; right-click opens column visibility checks (**0x1013af86**). Sort-direction marker remains unresolved (no art/drawing found).
* **Grid layout** (`LayoutGrid`, `SetGridIconSize`, slot art `GFX_GUI_MULTILISTVIEW_SLOT_*`, drag and drop) is **not** part of this port's widget: the inventory / wear / shortcut grids stay in HUD code (§11).
* API: `Gui::multi_{set_flags, add_column, clear, add_row, sort, row_ids, selected, sort_state, columns, select, set_column_width}(window, name, ..)`, row cells `view::MultiCell::{text, num, unsorted, image}`; events `MultiSelected`, `MultiMouse { id, button, clicks, x, y }`, `MultiColumnResized { col, width }` (the LFT window persists the widths in `LFTWindowConfig`). XML: `<MultiListView name=.. feature_flags=..>` is engine-only (the original builds it in code).

### 14.4 Horizontal scroll bar (`gui/hscroll.rs`)
`ScrollView` honours `h_scrollbar_mode` (auto shows the bar when content is wider than the viewport; the bars shrink each other's viewport): offset `x`, arrows ±13px, track pages, thumb drag. The unrotated `GFX_GUI_SCROLLBAR_GRAY_*` art is intentional native behavior: `ScrollBar_c` **0x10141d6a** selects the same default sprite IDs for either orientation and changes destination geometry/anchoring, not image rotation. Existing horizontal scrolling/multilist regressions exercise the reachable implementation; no replacement horizontal artwork is invented.

### 14.5 Tests / screenshots
`cargo test --release -p ao-gui --test listview` (real mouse events on the three widgets); `cargo test --release -p aomac social_win` (Friends window, LFT window); `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/x cargo test --release -p aomac social_win_shot` → `social.png`.

**Missions/shared list layout (bugs3/9):** The adapter's stacked header had no
preferred height, placing row zero at the header origin. It now embeds a
height-constrained native MultiListView header, reusing `ColumnHeaderView_c`
paint (`0x1013a516`, `ColumnHeaderButton_c` `0x10139a17`: progress01 art
0x14f..0x157, label borders 3). Rows use native extent 15, pitch 19 and
column gap 5 (`LayoutList` `0x1013520e` / `AddItem` `0x10135b85`), retaining
tooltip and press identities. Fixed columns advance by width + 1 + 5
(`FUN_100f171e`); fixed cells and intervening gaps have explicit size limits,
and a trailing horizontal spacer alone absorbs enlarged-window slack.
Row cells align to the top instead of the HLayout's default vertical
centre. The embedded header's flexible scroll body also consumed height slack:
`max_size` is a preferred-size floor, not a limit. An explicit
`max_size_limit` caps that wrapper to the native header extent so only the
root's existing vertical spacer absorbs height slack, keeping the painted
header at the scroll origin and row zero immediately below it.
`list_header_reserves_height_before_first_row_and_icon` and
`mission_window_lists_quests_and_acts` cover top/left anchoring, fixed column
offsets and native row pitch at initial and enlarged window sizes.

**Team body (bug1):** `TeamView` constructor `0x100785f0` explicitly adds a
stretched `GFX_GUI_TAB_BACKGROUND` surface (0x198) over `GetBounds`. The port
omitted it. Its status `BorderView` has no `SetGfx`; the port's XML default
inset border was unintended and is replaced by a plain layout wrapper.
`TeamBar` `0x100791f7` derives HLayout height from text with 2-pixel vertical
borders and two powerbars with 2-pixel outer borders. The port derives this
preferred height from installed font/art rather than guessing 16.
`team_native_background_and_rows_stay_inside_client` covers background and
row containment.

**Friends/docked-page body (bug22):** `FriendListView` `0x100a9c58` has no
own background because its docked parent owns it. `register_window` omitted
the `RollupPage` surfaces that `open_page` supplied. `RollupPage` constructor
`0x10049389` loads stretched TAB_BACKGROUND (0x198) in DEFAULT colour without
a local-alpha override, plus a solid 0x404040 surface. Layout `0x100490e4`
places that solid after the child-client bottom: it fills the five-pixel
bottom border, not the whole body. Registered docked pages now share this
paint; free/restore transitions remove the dock-only surface, leaving the
style-0 `DockWindow` frame (`0x1003bdd4`). `open_page` uses the same helper,
removing its former whole-body solid and guessed .85 image alpha. Inherited
window fade remains intact.
`friends_wrapper_paints_native_body_only_while_docked` covers transitions.

## 11.12 HudWinA: Programs / NCU / Mission windows (`play/hud_nano.rs`, `hud_ncu.rs`, `hud_listview.rs`, `hud_nanodb.rs`, `own_nanos.rs`, `ao-net n3/{nano,quest}.rs`)
Evidence: GUI.dll `NanoView_c` ctor `FUN_100d496c`, `NCUView_c` `FUN_100d6dd6`, `MissionView_c` `FUN_100d2c7e` (module slots `InventoryGUIModule_c::Slot*ViewActivated` 0x100c6d2a / 0x100c6f7d / 0x100c6da1); retail shots (ao-universe.com Basic GUI Guide: nanoprograms.jpg, ncuwindow.jpg, missions.jpg).
* **Programs**: school selector (Favorites, Combat, Medical, Prot, Psi, Space; text keys `Favorites`, `NanoSchool_*`) over a 32 px icon grid (`SetGridIconSize(0)`, spacing 10, borders 6, art `GFX_GUI_MULTILISTVIEW_SLOT_32_*`); list columns 0,1,9,10,13,11,5,6,7,16 (keys `Tab_*`, stats 407/54/8/287/294/210/0x227). Page = stat `School`(0x195)-1, else Favorites. Double click = `N3Msg_CastNanoSpell` (action 0x13, a = target, b = `{0xCF1B, id}`); right click = HideNano/UnHideNano. Program list = `FullCharacter.list_18` (`FUN_10073a2f`), changed by actions 0xcc/0xcd (apply cases 0x56/0x57, GC 0x1005e7e6/0x1005e846).
* **Nanocrystal upload is server-authoritative**: inventory use emits `GenericCmd_t` command 3 (`N3Msg_UseItem`, GC 0x100286f8; `hud_stats/item_ui.rs::run`), not a local append. Re-inspected the installed Gamecode.dll action remap bytes at `0x1005efdb + action + 3`: actions `0xcc` / `0xcd` map to apply cases `0x56` / `0x57`. Assembly `0x1005e7e6..0x1005e7f3` loads `[EBP+0x14]`, then its `+4` instance, and calls `0x1004fbbc`: the learned id is `identity_b.instance`. `Zone::on_frame` feeds this decoded message into `OwnNanos`, increments its serial once for a new program, and the already-open Programs window refreshes from that serial. Regression `hud_nano::tests::server_upload_refreshes_open_programs_and_casts` covers the decoded reply, refreshed real Shadow Touch row, duplicate reply, and exact cast packet; it was added but not executed in this change. Live upload proof still requires an inventory nanocrystal for a not-yet-owned usable program and the resulting server `0xcc` reply; no upload capture was available here.
* **Shared nano admission and original wire target (2026-10-15 RE, constructor correction 2026-10-16):** GC `1001b54b` calls `1004f6a0` and sends the caller's original target through `1007253f`; no selection stays `{0,0}` even when local validation resolves self. Programs and hotbar use the same `HudNano::cast` gate: known-program `1004eb01`, action3 criteria (`1004f935` → `100811ac` → `10081149`), source/target Vehicle662, Features224, affordability, resolved character, movement, school/family locks, replacement, geometry and final friendly/hostile permission. Admission and InfoView share `nano_cost_multiplier`: `1004e3e1` reads effective318 and floors at breed1/2=50, breed3=45, breed4=55, except actual Flags0x10000000 (mirrored into actor+0x21c by `100583eb`/`10058415`) skips the floor. `1004f827..1004f833` multiplies template407, divides100 and truncates; affordability reads raw214. Actual SimpleChar `1005cb6a` creates modifier holder `10064194`, copying preset `102e32c4` through `10002208` → `1002e358`: absent server slots214/318/381 therefore read constructor0/100/0, not INVALID. Only unlisted slots retain INVALID1234567890 (`1002e46a`); explicit server writes remain authoritative and received maps stay unseeded. Effective318/381 retain percent/bonus containers (`10058e52`, `100654e1`, `1006554c`). `10058e52` replaces Features224 bit0x800 with actual `IsGrid`, regardless of the received bit; area1000029 resolves zone0 through `zone_to_area[0]` using proxy area identity (`10121d84`, `1011eeb4`).
* **Motion, source and target state:** `10059ac4` is **IsMoving**, not concurrent casting: PlayerVehicle vtable+0x9c → `1006efe1` → FSM vtable+0x1c=`1006c469`. `Player` publishes that actual FSM predicate, mode (`100574e2` → `1006ed98` → `100704e6`), and vehicle falling+0x52 (`Vehicle1000a1a7`, `LandNow1000a74f`); modes4/5/8/9/11/12 refuse. The full-update `flag_32f` initializes input lock+0x21d (`10077e13`), then the existing movement fear/control state owns changes (`10058d6c`, `10058d7d`); no parallel crowd-control approximation is added. +0x21c is the actual NPC discriminator; friendly normal-player nanos require a player or an own pet (`100523c3`). +0x80 is N3 `IsDying` (`1000111a`), not Health: own removal prevents further control-dynel casts, and source teardown clears initiation/cast state. The early target read at `1004f9d8` is **Vehicle662**, not a guessed dead-health test. Hostile final admission uses the same authoritative `combat::module::can_attack` as normal/special attacks (`1004e7eb` → `10069556`), including the existing native dead flag, surrender, Features, team and district state.
* **Concurrent execution, lockouts and replacement:** actual same-school concurrency is `1004ee95`, not the movement virtual. `Dynels` retains execution independently of visual completion through attack, authoritative result action0x6b (`identity_b.kind=result`, `.instance=program`, `1005d8bd` → `1004effe`), and recharge (`10050ed9`, `100500ca`, `10050570`, `1004e68c`). Elapsed attack time does not invent a success result or release the initiation visual. `1004ebb5` checks the native controller+0x40 vector: successful execution appends the exact program identity with duration254/100 (`10050232..1005027f`), followed by `(family75,546..550; duration643..648/100)` entries (`10051aba`); both use the same whole-second expiry pulse (`1005122c`). Skill/perk locks are not substituted. `1004f626` / `1004f649` share exact six-family overlap and rank551 (`1004e1ca`) with authoritative active nano snapshots, including zero-time entries until BuffIIR removal, and result-resolved pending self-casts in recharge (`1004f368`). Stronger target entries refuse; equal rank may refresh; no-replace bit0x800000 additionally tests **source** entries and pending source self-casts at any rank. Native default family75 is the program ID, rank551=0 (`10085bf8`), not one shared zero family.
* **Range, playfield, visibility and final permission:** actual dynel playfield identities (`N3 GetPlayfield100042d0` / `GetPlayfieldInstanceID10001016`, dynel+0x60) are retained from full updates and compared, separately from geometry/area resource keys. `10086044` uses `trunc(base287 + wrapping_i32(base287 * effective381)/100)`, capped40; native NanoItem base range is20 (`10085bf8`), and actual SimpleChar's constructor preset381=0 applies when not received. Distance uses current 3D feet positions minus both actual scaled CAT torso collision radii; `1004e7eb` compares unrounded surface distance. `10058908` uses loaded playfield LOS at feet+1.6m; self needs no ray. Missing live vehicle, announced playfield identity, collision sphere or collision world produces a diagnostic/refusal, not a fabricated radius or clear ray. Friendly permission uses actual district queries, own-pet and ordinary-team/raid membership, Side33/TeamSide213, battlestation identity range0x111c..0x1124 and BattlestationSide668 (`1004e7eb`, `100657d1`, `100657e9`, `1006581f`, `10065865`); actual SimpleChar ctor `1005cb6a` seeds668=2, not0. Self bypass follows the outer range guard. Regression `captured_own_body_boost_uses_native_presets_and_actual_motion` decodes `zone_own_full_character_ithaca.rec`, asserts318/381 absent from received stats, and checks installed Body Boost29091 cost11/range20 and self admission with initialized motion. Authored, not run by this worker.
* **Local initiation is cosmetic, not predicted gameplay:** final `1004e177` creates source effect366 under category4 and stores controller+8/+0xc. Accepted requests now hand this real selector to the existing authored effect renderer; native default49999 creates no handle (`10085bf8`). This is separate from relay start effect428 and from execution/result/recharge bookkeeping. `1004eef0` / `1007ac9a` clear the local handle when attack releases; named/unnamed cancellation, queue clear and source/world teardown also retire it. No local buff application, healing, damage, debuff, pet creation, pool debit or invented server result is added. NanoItem+bc is constructor-zero (`100861e0`); inspected defaults/loader (`10085bf8`, `1008622a`) supply no force-self property, so no such property is inferred from gameplay effects.
* **Regression boundary and prerequisites:** added native motion/input-lock, friendly NPC/pet/team/battlestation, exact radius/range/overflow, active cancellation/recast/equal-rank/no-replace, result/concurrent/recharge/program-and-family-lock and initiation lifecycle checks. The eight installed-template fixtures also assert the real effect366 selector and unchanged original wire identities; metadata-only fixtures do not instantiate a Player/collision world and are not claimed as full geometry proof. Tests are authored, **not executed** in this change. Criteria93/103 now consume the canonical received completed-perk projection shared with inventory/perks; only a criterion that actually needs that still-unknown snapshot diagnoses it. Unsupported evaluator operators also diagnose instead of pretending success. Live geometry requires the initialized Player and actual source/target playfield identities, loaded CAT sphere and collision world; these have concrete existing writers and are wired above, not deferred native-state gaps. Server execution outcomes and ordinary gameplay effects remain server-authoritative.
* **Requested nano-type fixtures:** the same shared guard regression additionally loads Minor Healing26465, Ice Flechette28612, Lesser Anger Manifestation43324, Feet of Stone56027 and Lethargy26707 from the installed RDB, exercising unchanged empty/own/other selected wire identities, real strict Use3 skill requirements, profession checks and template nano-point costs. The pet template's actual `[251,1,66]` requirement uses the existing evaluator extended with GC `100065d8` case0x42: select nibble `(abs(value)/1000)&7` from the stat, compare below `abs(value)%1000`, invert for a negative operand. Its fixture checks that a packed attack-pet count of1 refuses another summon; it does not invent or simulate a server-spawned pet. Separate small regression covers the signed and nonzero-nibble operator forms. Added tests remain unexecuted in this change.
* **Upload visibility / learned-nano signal**: the existing list append alone left the Programs UI on Favorites. Retail `FUN_1004fbbc` returns success only for a new known nano; the `0xcc` apply branch then emits feedback code `0x0f` through `FUN_100112fb` → `FUN_1000482e`, which emits `GlobalSignals+0x10c` carrying the nano identity. GUI `OpenNanoWindow` observer `0x100bfeb6` connects slot `0x100bfc98`: it sets `nano_window=true`, then emits `+0x110`. `NanoView_c` constructor `0x100d496c` connects `0x100d4319` / `0x100d435b`; they find/add the row (`0x100d3712` / `0x100d3eed`) and select its saved page or `School-1` (`0x100d418d`). `OwnNanos::take_learned` now exposes only new upload events (not the login list or duplicate replies); `HudNano::select_learned` selects that actual page. The regression starts on Favorites and checks a learned Shadow Touch becomes visible on Space. Shortcut option evidence: `ShortcutBarView_c` `0x100d8c12` conditionally connects `0x100d7999` for `ShortcutNewNanos`; it gates on the primary radio button (`0x100d7829`, field `+0x1d0`, toggle gfx `0x15a/0x159`), then uses `MultiListView_c::GetFirstFreePos` of the entire bar list, not the displayed row, and adds type 4 via `0x100d78e5`.
* **Integrated upload consumers**: `Hud::update` drains the learned event once; `OpenNanoWindow` opens Programs when enabled, and an already-open Programs window selects the uploaded program's school even with auto-open disabled. `ShortcutNewNanos` adds the real `{0xcf1b,id}` nano shortcut to the primary bar's first free full-list position; using that slot runs the same cast encoder as Programs. Integration regression `hud::tests::server_learned_nano_opens_programs_at_its_school` covers these consumers. No executed-test or live-upload claim is made here.
* **Real-window baseline (2026-10-06, ItemsNanos)**: muted `origin/main` app, 960×680 window, approved Aomacvolk, PID-scoped CGEvent mouse/keyboard and CGWindowID-only captures. Shift-click first carried item and right-click rifle opened no InfoView; dragging the medical kit onto empty hotbar slot 4 left it empty. Double-clicking the first blue crystal produced real chat `Body Boost has been uploaded.` while Programs did not open, no nano shortcut appeared, and the crystal remained visible. This reproduces the reported upload visibility/consumption symptom without synthetic button dispatch. The app was closed and lock released. Fixed real-window inspection/upload/cast/drop acceptance was not run because the user requested wrap-up; no real-window PASS is claimed.
* **Server consumption/deletion**: incoming `CharacterActionIIR_t` action `0x70` was not applied by the port, leaving used/deleted inventory entries present. The retail remap at `0x1005efdf + 0x70 - 1` is `0x21`; jump-table entry at `0x1005ee43 + 0x21*4` points to `0x1005d8f1`. Assembly `0x1005d8f1..0x1005d909` passes `identity_a` to `FUN_1004a191`, with `param_4 = identity_b.kind > 0` and `param_5 = false`. The helper refuses worn page kinds via `FUN_10047242`; for a bag entry it removes the item, except positive extra kind consumes one unit of a stack (`FUN_1002a7d2` reads its second u16; `FUN_1002ad45` writes the decremented count). `Zone::on_frame` now applies this own-character reply to bag and stored container entries. Regression `server_delete_consumes_stack_or_removes_item_without_learning_a_nano` covers decoded server replies, ownership, worn refusal, single/whole-stack removal and no invented nano. No crystal-use capture proves which consume reply PRK sends yet; this repairs the concrete missing retail receive path, not a guessed upload acknowledgement.
* **Visible runtime stack count**: the inventory list now reads the second inventory u16 (`InventoryEntry.b`, the `FUN_1002a7d2` accessor) rather than the cached template's stat 212. Count participates in the list's rebuild signature, so consumption that leaves the same slot/template still refreshes its Count cell. Template stat 212 remains only in shop-template metadata; it is not a fallback for an owned item. Regression `inventory_list_refreshes_server_consumed_stack_count` feeds a decoded single-unit consume reply and checks that the already-open list changes from 3 to 2. Added checks remain unexecuted by this worker.
* **NCU**: title and left-wing text use effective `CurrentNCU`/`MaxNCU` stats 180/181. Rows read the `OwnNanos` lifecycle, not GUI-owned timers: CharacterAction `0x62`/`0xb1` add/replace effects (`FUN_100512af`), while `BuffIIR` kind 0 removes them (`FUN_10050afd`). Action duration is `identity_b.instance` centiseconds, scaled by stat 464; login `SimpleCharFullUpdate.effects` supplies total/remaining centiseconds in `b`/`c` (`a` ignored). Runtime timing uses integer GameTime seconds; opening NCU neither expires effects nor discards entries with missing records. Remaining/Time cells use lifecycle remaining/total duration, while Programs retains template `TimeExist`. Double click emits `N3Msg_RemoveBuff` (action 0x41); frame from `DockArea2.xml`.
* **NCU display metadata (2026-10-15 RE):** GUI `1003eacb` classifies Flags bit `0x8000` as Hostile, otherwise event0 containing function `0xcf4a` as Team (`N3Msg_IsTeamNano`, GC `10016544` → `10002483`), otherwise Self only when NanoItem+bc is set, else Friendly. Static templates leave +bc false (`100861a4`, `1008622a`), so no self-only flag is invented. Shared Programs/NCU cell formatter supplies column8 and tooltip prefix using the exact coloured labels of GUI `1003cbcb`. GUI tooltip `1004518b` requests `N3Msg_GetModifierString` with list0; GC `100236fa` delegates to the same spell-list conversion used by InfoView. NanoDb caches parsed event0 spells, and NCU reuses the existing InfoView opcode formatter for its conditional `Modifies:` section. Radius is **not** a missing formula parser: GC `100164f2` returns NanoItem+b8, initialized to0 by `10085bf8` and unchanged by static deserialization `1008622a`/`1008070e`; both windows correctly show0 for these static records. A runtime writer of +b8/+bc remains unrepresented. Added real-record regression covers a friendly modifier nano and hostile28612 (Ice Flechette), shared Type/Radius cells, and modifier tooltip text. Timed25982 is separately asserted Friendly by the native Type predicate despite being non-removable under the distinct cancellation mask0xc100. The parent gate exposed the original fixture's mistaken Hostile expectation; no checks/live runs executed by this worker after correction.
* Encoders with byte tests: `ao_net::n3::nano::{cast_nano, remove_buff, delete_nano}`. Quest list decoder `ao_net::n3::quest` (Quest_t v7..15, RewardBox, QuestAction, WorldPos; BinaryStream facts from BinaryStream.dll) with layout-derived tests only (no capture holds a mission).
* **Mission** (`play/hud_mission.rs`): `MissionView_c` ctor `0x100d2c7e` (title "Missions", `mission_window`), `MissionListView_c` `0x100d3431`: list columns Icon16 / Name200 / Remaining55 (id15). Refresh `0x100d2ab7` reads `N3Msg_GetQuestIds`; `0x100d2f92` updates integer seconds every1s. Interfaces `0x1000a874` finds the last nonzero future action deadline`+0x5c` against server Unix time (`GameTimeIIR.arg4`, GC`0x10016b9b`). Double-click `0x100d2334` opens `itemid://kind/instance`; popup`0x100d275d` offers Look@Mission, Upload2Map when GC`0x1001ab61` succeeds, team update when bit8 of`Quest+0xa4` is set (`0x1001740d`), and DeleteMission with confirmation`0x100d2517`. Team request is `GiveQuestToMembersIIR_t`77230927 (quest instance, writer`0x100ac9a3`); removal is `QuestIIR_t`212C487A (`1,0,Identity quest,Identity{0,0}`, writer`0x100765ad`), except kept bit10 (`0x10019e5a`). Single removals synchronize from Zone. Native size/cell/coordinates/feedback/order evidence and regressions follow; no new live-capture claim.
  Remaining-cell correction: GUI `FUN_1003cdf6` case `0xf`, assembly `0x1003cf68..0x1003cf90`, formats `%02d:%02d` from integer seconds (`seconds/3600`, `(seconds/60)%60`). The former NCU-style hours:minutes:seconds formatting and centisecond multiplication were incorrect. Regression `remaining_cell_uses_native_hours_and_minutes_without_centisecond_overflow` covers minute/hour boundaries, 100-hour values and `u32::MAX`; the window regression checks the displayed native cells. Tests not executed by this worker yet.
  Marker coordinates resolved: GC `N3Msg_GetQuestWorldPos` `0x1001ab61` copies first-action `+0x74..0x7c` to its first vector output and `+0x80..0x88` (the decoded `WorldPos.local`) to its second. GUI `FUN_100d2409` passes the **second** output (`EBP-0x2c`) through `GlobalSignals+0x158`, not the integer world-cell coordinates. Upload2Map now uses that local vector's X/Z. `mission_window_lists_quests_and_acts` distinguishes integer coordinates `(1500,2500)` from local `(123.5,45,678.25)` and checks the uploaded marker `(123.5,678.25)`.
  Initial size resolved: `MissionView_c` `0x100d2c7e` calls `View::ResizeTo(FUN_100cf72a())`; that helper reads `0x101bd710=167.0`, `0x101bdfa4=112.0`. The normal unsaved DockableView placement remains centred. `mission_window_lists_quests_and_acts` rejects the former300px viewport.
  Kept-quest feedback resolved and wired: GC `0x10019ea7` pushes `"Quest_CannotDelete"` (`0x10157628`) and category`0x66`; `N3Msg_RemoveQuest` emits it on `GlobalSignals+0x184` without sending the remove request. The Missions dialog now forwards that database text into the HUD's existing System-line consumer. The window regression checks no outbound request and exactly-once feedback consumption.
  Row ordering resolved: `0x10040854(15,0)` sets **grid**, not list, sorting. Base `MultiListView_c` ctor`0x10136423` starts list sort at−1; AddColumn`0x101378a7` selects the first sortable column0. Comparator`0x1003cc19` compares grid positions for0, case-insensitive names for1, raw integers for2..16. New mission rows`0x100d2275` use `GetFirstFreePos`; initial list order is insertion order. The port preserves that order and explicitly sorts Remaining by raw seconds, not the lossy hours:minutes strings. The window regression covers default order, explicit Remaining order and differing seconds with identical displayed minutes in both directions.
  Refresh ordering follows `0x100d2ab7`: insert missing quests using `0x100d2275`/`GetFirstFreePos` **before** deleting stale entries. Open-window grid slots therefore survive server enumeration changes; new entries reuse holes left by prior refreshes, not slots being removed in the current refresh. Closing/reopening reconstructs the list. `mission_refresh_retains_grid_slots_before_deleting_stale_rows` checks both ordering and hole reuse; explicit column-0 descending reverses the retained positions.
* **Not done / UNRESOLVED**: rollup docking (HudItems); InfoView (`LookAtNano`); drag to hotbar; recharge overlay; runtime NanoItem+b8/+bc overrides; prefs persistence; no screenshots or live run were made for these metadata changes; the Zone hook is `Zone.nanos`.

## 10.5 HudHotbar: hotbar semantics, special-action list, hot keys

Evidence: GUI.dll / Gamecode.dll (Ghidra, `/tmp/HudHotbar/gp` copy of the shared projects). **Status: compiled and tested** (`cargo test --release -p aomac`: `hud_special`, `hud_keys`, `hud::tests::hotbar_first_login_and_interaction`, `actions_window_uses_drags_and_recharges`; clippy 0 warnings); no live session.
* **Press / release** (`MultiListView_c::MouseDown` 0x10133b63 captures the mouse and records the cell; `MouseUp` 0x10133c28 emits the click only for the same cell). The hotbar list sets flag +0x319 (`FUN_100d8c12` 0x100d8eb8, arg 1) and `UseNewMouseFunc` is true (LoginPrefs): the down handler `FUN_100410a0` arms a timer of `MouseButtonHoldDelay` (0.3 s) for a left press on an item, its slot `FUN_1004173a` = `EndMouseClick` + drag-begin; the click handler `FUN_10040a17` (left): Shift -> info signal, else **use**. So use fires on release, a 0.3 s hold drags without moving, right button does nothing for the hotbar (old mouse mode: right = use, left = pick up). Implemented in `hud_bar.rs` (`input`/`update`). UNRESOLVED: whether the pending timer is cancelled by the release (EventTimer_c lives in Utils.dll, no project).
* **Click qualifiers**: GUI `FUN_10040a17` tests the modifier mask with `& 3`, i.e. Shift only, for the info signal; GUI `FUN_100bc5cf` distinguishes Ctrl (`0xc`) from Alt (`0x30`), so Ctrl is outside that test and a Ctrl-click takes the normal path. Programs (`FUN_100d496c`) inherits the `FUN_100419f8` base, which registers `FUN_10040a17`; the item-click handler is shared, so the hotbar and the Actions window behave the same. Regressions `programs_raw_ctrl_selects_and_casts_but_shift_inspects` (`hud_nano.rs`), `ctrl_click_uses_hotbar_slot_while_shift_click_does_not` (`hud_bar.rs`), `ctrl_click_uses_action_while_shift_click_does_not` (`hud_actionwin.rs`); not run during this edit.
* **Hot keys**: providers `SHORTUCT_BAR_ACTIVE_n` (digits, default), `_ROW_n` (Shift+digit), `_SELECT_n` (Alt+digit), `SHORTCUT_BAR_b_s` (no default); handlers `FUN_100d85cf/100d884c/100d7d8f/100d7d6d` act when the bool is 0 (key press). `hud_keys.rs` builds the map from the DValue `KeyBindings` archive (defaults + saved `prefs/<account>/Char<id>/Prefs.xml`). The earlier F-key limitation is stale: physical input uses `controls::key_id` → `Hud::key_press`, not `Key::Letter`; function keys follow the same row/slot binding path. Existing `hud_keys::saved_bindings_override` covers an F5 slot binding.
* **Special-action list** (`hud_special.rs`): built by the client (`GC10043005`, `1009e301`, `100428ad`); full action/template table in `ACTION_TEMPLATES`. Toggles follow own state and shortcuts follow identity changes (`10042da4`, `1006d196`). Unlisted identity gives `Feedback_ActionIsNotAvailable`. Crawl's enter/leave actions `0x14/0x8d` are directly confirmed by `1006e646/1006e3ff`, not inferred from Sneak. Profession alt actions `0x8a/0x8b` require `1003e278`: assembly `1003e27e..1003e29e` admits professions1–15 and directly reads two dwords at `0x102e2fc8 + profession*8`, not through a pointer/vector header. The accessed pairs span `0x102e2fd0..0x102e3047`; the leading eight bytes are not accessed by this getter. These addresses are in the PE loader-zero `.data` virtual tail (`VA0x101bd000`, virtual size`0x12d45c`, raw size`0x10a800`), not 32 stored zero dwords. Raw absolute-reference/HIGHLOW relocation scanning finds only getter operands `1003e28b/1003e29a` referencing the region, with no exported table alias. Adjacent `102e2fc4` is the exported TeleportTrier singleton; initializer `101523d0` separately zeros `102e3048/102e304c`. No runtime writer/templates were recovered; computed runtime population remains unproved, so no guessed IDs are exposed. `10044b6e/10044eb1/10044f38` prove their state flag is stat224 mask`0x40000000`. Reload, Backstab bit20 and unequip gaps remain separately documented; lock/timer semantics follow below.
* **Not done**: item 6(c) live AGG/DEF check, 6(e) map ownership wiring (§12.2 unchanged), §12 UNRESOLVED list. Actions window, consumers, row paging and the recharge feed: §10.7.

## 10.7 HudAct: Actions window, special-action consumers, hotbar rows, recharge feed (`play/hud_actionwin.rs`, `hud_use.rs`, `hud_bar.rs`, `hud_special.rs`)
Evidence: GUI.dll `SpecialActionView_c` 0x100dc082 (layout as listed in the §12.2 note), `ShortcutBarView_c` ctor `FUN_100d8c12`, Gamecode (Ghidra, headless decompile of the shared projects); retail screenshots were not available.
* **Actions window** (`WindowKind::Actions`, Ctrl+2, rollup page `specialaction_window`): `DockableView` "Actions", 3-column 32 px grid (minimum 21 cells, view 167 × 167), hosted by `ListWindow` / `Rollup::open_page`. Items show the current `SpecialList` action with native rdb name/icon and `GFX_GUI_ICONFADER32` recharge overlay. Short release performs the action; a hold drags it to a shortcut. **Shift-click inspection** now forwards `itemid://57008/<template>` to the existing InfoView, matching Actions `100dba7f` and hotbar `100d765d` (macros excluded); Ctrl alone still uses the action. Regressions `ctrl_click_uses_action_while_shift_click_does_not`, `ctrl_click_uses_hotbar_slot_while_shift_click_does_not`. Canvas clipping remains intersected with the enclosing ScrollView.
* **Actions timer labels**: `GUI1003eebe` prints no text when native action progress equals 1.0 (including a newly started full lock or a flag-only disabled action); otherwise `%2ds` through 60 seconds, then integer minutes `%2dm`. `1003f2ea` centres the BOLD yellow label above the fader. Actions and shortcuts respect `IconTimerText` independently of `IconTimers`; disabling the latter leaves a full fader on disabled actions, not an enabled icon. Absence uses `Option`, not a numeric sentinel, preserving legitimate signed seconds such as `-1s`. ListView labels share the Canvas icons' `cell_pos` coordinates instead of reconstructing a second row/spacer grid whose repeated rounding drifts when Actions' fill spacing is fractional. Regressions `action_recharge_labels_follow_text_setting_independently_of_fader` and `shortcut_disabled_fader_and_timer_text_use_distinct_native_state` cover Burst at 29/30 seconds, every populated cell's label centring, both settings, initial full-lock text suppression, flag-only disability and signed timer text.
* **Actions popup audit — bounded native match / PASS (static RE)**: constructor assembly `100dc254..100dc2b3` connects signals `+2d8` (drag → `100db87c`), `+2dc` (drop → `100db945`), `+2e4` (inspection → `100dba7f`), `+2e0` (use → `100db704`), not popup `+2e8`. New-mouse right-click `10040a17` emits `+2e8` via `10041c66` (`10040acb..10040ad2`). The `ItemListViewBase` constructor `100419f8` initializes this signal; Actions population `100dbf77` and activation `100c6e8f` add no popup connection. All29 GUI scalar`0x2e8` uses were classified: popup subscriptions belong to Inventory, Knowledge, Missions and Nanos, while remaining uses are signal lifecycle/emitters or unrelated fields. Actions' inherited menu virtuals `10038931/32` are no-ops, `1003899d` returns an empty string and `100389ba` returns 0. Leaving this Actions item-popup signal unconnected matches the inspected native paths; a missing port menu is not established. This static result does not rule out unsearched generic/dynamically computed consumers or claim retail-window verification. Remaining Actions limitation: saved `item_position_map` / free icon rearrangement.
* **Consumers** (`GC1004256c`): Sneak `0x13` checks MechData stat662, then sends CharacterAction `0xa3`; Stop Sneaking `0x4f` sends movement `0x24`. Crawl `0x14/0x8d` uses movement `0x1b/0x28`, refusing swimming or entry while polymorphed (`N3Msg_CrawlToggle`, `100278c9`). Forage `0x89` sends action `0xca`. Search `0x86` (`1003fa2c`) checks **positive remaining stat136 seconds**, prints native skill-lock feedback while positive, otherwise `Feedback_SearchingForHiddenObjects` plus action `0x42`; its local effect `0xb01c` is not created. Reload `0x6e` sends action `0xd2` only while the native weak reload timer is absent (`100694aa`, `10029e66`); completion `10068301` clears it and prints `Feedback_ReloadingCompleted` (docs/zone/combat-timers.md §3). Use `3` delegates the selected character/object identity to `Interact::use_item` (`N3Msg_UseItem`, `100286f8`). Pick-Up `1` (`10027beb`) checks bag slots `0x40..0x5d`, otherwise `Feedback_InventoryFull`, then sends `ClientGetItemIIR_t` for that same selected identity (`10015258`). `Zone::selected_target` retains both character and world-object selection; with no selection these consumers currently send nothing, unlike retail's empty identity. The server answer to Sneak `0xa3` remains untraced. Wire field tests live in `ao_net::n3::{action,inventory}`; registered-owner dispatch and flag admission follow below.
* **Row paging**: the handlers address slot `n` of the shown row as `n + row * 10` (`FUN_100d7cef`); `SHORTUCT_BAR_ROW_n` (`FUN_100d884c` -> `FUN_100d85fb`) scrolls the list to row `n` (`Scroll(-(cell height * n))`, horizontal bars). `ShortcutBar` keeps `ROWS` rows, `set_row` swaps the shown one; the toolbar's up / down buttons (art `GFX_GUI_TOOLBAR_BUTTON_UP/DOWN`) step one row, the row keys call `set_row` on the active bar; identity changes of toggles are applied to every row. UNRESOLVED GUESS: the row count 10 (`FUN_100d8c12` sets `SetViewCellCounts((10,1),(10,1))`, never `SetMaxItemCount`; the content height that clamps the scroll was not traced) and the buttons' own handler (one row per click assumed).
* **Native lock time and routing**: `GC100275f7` reads signed integer seconds from skill locks for shown actions142..999, perk locks for actions≥1000, and no progress timer below142. `Zone` owns both vectors and their shared GameTime pulse; HUD only projects snapshots, so overlays do not drift continuously between integer ticks. FullCharacter groups0/1 (`+0x28/+0x2c`) append to the skill/perk lock vectors (`10073a2f`). `10064495/1005334d` subtract exactly1 from remaining and erase values<1, under GameTime+`0x18` in `1005b016`; `1000b214` emits at most one pulse per frame, strictly after1second, resetting the accumulator without catch-up. CharacterAction`0x14` (`1005e2cd→100655d3`) extends the first skill lock's total/remaining; `0xaa→10064a6e` only adds a missing lock. Search reads its raw stat136 lock independently of icon progress. Regression `native_action_locks_restore_relay_extend_and_share_second_pulse` covers snapshot transfer, own-only relays, extension, nonextension and shared pulse boundaries.
* **Recharge arithmetic**: `GC1006389c`, assembly`10063920..10063939`, executes signed32-bit `IMUL` before double division100, adds duration and0.5, then truncates. The Rust path uses `wrapping_mul`, including its large-duration regression. The raw stat382 modifier floors at−50 and is ignored for actions`0x8e,0x90,0x92..0x94,0x96,0x97,0xa7,0x243,0x2a1`; extension adds with native32-bit wrapping.
* **Perk lock identity and entry flags**: `10052aca/10052b0a` scan perk locks, resolve perk ID `+4` through catalogue runtime `+0x14` (RDB1000036 word4), and return total `+8` / remaining `+0xc`. Learned-map/research timers are unrelated. CharacterAction `0xcf` (`1005e297→10053619`) and applied spell `CFC3` (`100a8b04`, stat0 = perk ID, stat39 = seconds) append unscaled signed-second locks, including duplicate IDs; the first matching record drives progress. Undo does not cancel them. Both live ApplySpells and the initial spell list share this hook. CharacterAction `0xfc` (`1005e7b3→100535ae`) clears flags for locked perk actions, empties the vector, and resets stat577 to zero. `0xb2` clears a shown action's entry flag (`1005e2b4→1003f065`, identity_b.instance). Action IDs are decoded from byte table `1005efdf` and pointer table `1005ee43`. `1003f156` disables actions142..999 by skill-lock membership, otherwise by perk-lock membership or the entry flag word; flag-only disability never fabricates a timer. The Zone relay/snapshot regression covers CFC3 apply/undo, duplicate IDs and the reset; `native_lock_ranges_and_entry_flags_are_independent_of_timer_text` covers routing boundaries and flags.
* **Dynamic registered-owner dispatch**: CharacterAction `0xb4` supplies three independent values: `identity_b.kind = Action_e`, `identity_b.instance = owner instance`, `identity_a.instance = icon/name template` (`1005e49b..1005e4b2→10042e16`). The owner kind is `0xc78e`; it is not reconstructed from a learned perk item. `1004256c` sends CharacterAction **`0xb3`**, identity_a = own character and identity_b = `{shown action, registered owner}`, param0 / empty text / pass-on0. `Entry::registered_action` and the Play consumer retain and send that raw owner. Flagbit0 is checked before static or dynamic dispatch; registrations and flag events retain packet order. Synthetic encoded regressions `registered_perk_feed_reaches_menu_and_identity_lookup`, `registration_and_flag_changes_preserve_native_packet_order` and `registered_owner_activation_and_flag_refusal_reach_actual_play_dispatch` cover Zone/menu lookup, exact activation bytes, raw signed owner bits, ordering, catalogue-resolved locks and the native flag-clear admission path. No live perk-bearing capture was exercised.
* **Native refusal feedback**: `10064301`, assembly `10064356..100643ca`, selects table1000 keys at `10160114` = `UnableToPerformActionPerk`, `10160130` = `UnableToPerformActionSkill`, `1016014c` = `UnableToPerformAction`; optional raw perk-item name or table2003 stat name precedes integer hours/minutes/seconds. The shared Play feedback helper handles Search and flagged action refusals; a missing lock falls back to `Feedback_ActionIsNotAvailable` as in `1004256c` / `10052a80` / `100529ea`.
* **Flight control-action flags**: enter-Fly Apply `1006dfaf` sets bit0 for actions `4d,4c,11,12,13,4f,14,8d`; explicit LeaveFly Apply `1006e115` clears them. Frozen Apply `1006e4d8` does not clear these flags. Movement therefore exposes an allocation-free generation/value pair written by those actual Apply handlers; Player passes it to the HUD, which changes entries only for a new generation. No mode-derived overwrite erases a later server flag clear, and repeated enter-Fly events still apply. Regression `flight_action_flags_follow_apply_events_not_mode_inference` covers repeated Apply, intervening flag clears, Frozen and explicit LeaveFly.
* **2026-10-16 headless integration verification**: `cargo test --release --workspace -j2` passed 1957 tests across 40 suites (63 ignored). After the signed-label `Option` and fixed-array legacy-decoder refinements, the final affected suites passed 31 tests (`hud_bar` 15, `hud_actionwin` 2, `hud_special` 9, `hud_actions` 3, actual Play activation/refusal 1, Zone lock snapshot/pulse 1). `cargo clippy --release --workspace --all-targets -j2 -- -D warnings` passed. No login, real-window input, screenshots or live perk-bearing character were used; the initial dynamic-registration replay and profession table's runtime writer remain unproved.

## 10.6 Live acceptance (HudLive2)
Four sessions on Ithaca with `Aomacvolk` (level 1, playfield 4582 = the crashed-ship beach, not the Arrival Hall; no items, no nanos, 1,000 credits), offscreen harness `play/flow/live.rs`, 1280 × 800. Retail references: AO-Universe Basic GUI Guide (`wear.jpg`, `sidebar.jpg`, window table with the hot keys). New harness step `agg=<v>` (drags the knob with mouse events, prints the local stat, deletes the locally applied stat 0x33 and waits 4 s for a server echo).
* **Bars / values**: Stats window and left bars agree (health "34 / 34" full red bar, nano "32 / 32" full blue bar, XP-type rows 0 / 1450, 0 / 1500, 0 / 2000 ×3); the harness's own-stats dump is taken after `Pools::apply` ran: `Life`(1) = 34, 27 = 34, so server value vs. formula was not separable live. NOT exercised: any change of health under damage (no hostile in reach on this char, no combat started; unit tests in `hud_pools.rs` cover the ratio).
* **Hotbar** (Start Combat, Walk/Run, Sit/Stand, Follow, five empty slots, Exit), **compass** (top right, "S" centred, facing yaw −π), **left / right wings** (6 buttons, target bars, `NCU 0/8`, `CRED 1,000`), **rollup column** (Wear, then Stats / Programs / Inventory pages) render as the guide's descriptions. Opening Programs (Ctrl+8), NCU (Ctrl+0) and Inventory (I) works: Programs shows the six school buttons and an empty 4 × 3 icon grid, NCU shows `NCU (0/8)` with its grid docked bottom right (over the AGG/DEF bar, as the template's `DockArea2` frame (1838, 1078) is pulled inside the screen), Inventory its 3-column grid. All three are EMPTY because the account's character has no nanos / buffs / items, so "real nano data" and "real inventory items" could not be seen live (the decoders are covered by the synthetic-fixture tests of §11.9 / §11.12).
* **Differences seen in that session**: (1) the fourth Wear tab was incorrectly emitted as a separate row and was half hidden by the next rollup page (a stray "Social" at y ≈ 365); **subsequently fixed** by the four-tab strip in §11.4. (2) The AGG/DEF fill in the guide is teal; the 0.7.2 `AGGDEF_SLIDER_BACKGROUND` (0x92) is a yellow → orange gradient, which is what we draw (the guide is an older skin); not changed because the art is the client's own. (3) When Ctrl+9 (close Stats) and Ctrl+0 were sent in one batch with only 1 s of waiting the NCU page was not yet on screen in the frame (`d.png`), with 2 s it was; not investigated further.
* **AGG/DEF** (6c): `FullCharacterIIR` carries `AggDef` (stat 0x33) = **100** at every login (the slider starts at the right end, `AGG`). Dragging the knob with mouse events (press inside the knob, 10 move steps, release) produced `Zone::stats[0x33]` = 48 / −1 / −61 / 100 for the requests 50 / 0 / −60 / 100 (the off-by-one-or-two were my harness's sub-pixel rounding, since fixed; the release floors as `FUN_1006e9c5` does). The `SetStatIIR_t` frame was sent each time (`Hud::take_outbox`, bytes tested by `set_stat_bytes`), but **no server reply was seen**: with the locally applied value deleted, stat 0x33 stayed absent for 4 s in all four drags (no `StatIIR` 0x33 echo; the only chat traffic was other players' Global chat), and the logins following drags to 0 and −61 still delivered 100, i.e. the server either ignores the message or does not persist it (UNRESOLVED which). That the slider follows the stat is covered by the unit test `aggdef_drag_sends_set_stat_on_release`, not re-observed live. The last drag asked for 100, the value the server delivers at login anyway.
* **Equip / unequip / drag and drop** (7-live): not possible live, the character has no item.
