No capture in `docs/captures/*.rec` contains `ClientMoveItemToInventoryIIR_t` (0x5469373F), `ClientContainerAddItemIIR_t`, `ContainerAddItemIIR_t` (0x47537A24), `ItemReplacedIIR_c` (0x3A223B50) or `InventoryUpdatedIIR_t`, so the encoders in `ao_net::n3::inventory` (`move_item_to_inventory`, `container_add_item`) and the decoders only rest on the client RE and the hash tests (`keys_are_the_class_name_hashes`) and byte-layout tests. Remaining acceptance: a character that owns an item (a starting-pack item or a looted one) to watch `ContainerAddItem` / `ItemReplaced` after a wear-slot drop.
* Screenshots inspected and deleted; scratch in `/tmp/HudLive2` removed.

## 11.14 HudBuff: buffed skill values (`ao_formats::stats::buffs`, `play/hud_stats/buffs.rs`, `ao_net::n3::spells`)
**What the server sends.** `StatIIR` / `FullCharacterIIR` stats are stored unmodified: `SetStat` (stat object vtable `0x1015f9ec` slot 0x40 = `FUN_10059e6a`) writes the value that `GetStat(stat, 2)` (slot 0x3c = `FUN_10058e52`) returns. `GetStat(stat, mode)` mode 0 → `FUN_1006554c(stat,1,0)` (= `N3Msg_GetSkill` mode 2, "buffed"), 1 → `FUN_100654e1` (mode 1, "base"), 3 → `FUN_10064800`, 4 → `FUN_10065576`, 2 → the stored value; `N3Msg_GetSkill` [GC 0x10026d66] mode 0 → stored value, 1/2/3/4 → `FUN_100654e1` / `FUN_1006554c(stat,1,0)` / `FUN_10064800` / `FUN_1006554c(stat,1,1)`. Nothing in the stat messages carries a buff flag: the buffs are the spells below. No capture has an active spell (the own `FullCharacterIIR_t` spell list is empty in all of them); the formulas and the spell layout are therefore verified on the decompilation and on real item records (below), not on a live buff.

**Formulas** (`ao_formats::stats::buffs`, tests incl. the colour rule):
* maps of the modifier object (`SimpleChar+0x1bc`): bonus map `this+4`, percent map `this+0x10` (`std::map<int,int>`, node value at `+0x10`).
* `FUN_1006460b(stat, value, 1)` = `bonus[stat] - extra`, `extra = trunc(lock * value / 10000.0)` (double math, `_DAT_10160168`) when `lock = GetStat(0xf7, 2) > 0` (`FUN_10062526`) and stat ∈ 100..=168 ∪ 16..=21; reading a missing node reads 0.
* `FUN_10063d39(stat)` = `f32((percent[stat] + 100.0) / 100.0)` (`_DAT_10158670` = 100.0), 1.0 without node or when 0.
* `GetSkill(stat,1)` `FUN_100654e1` = `trunc(raw * multiplier)` (only when ≠ 1.0) + `trunc(FUN_10064ac2(stat, 3))`; mode 3 of the trickle-down feeds it the **buffed abilities** `a + FUN_1006460b(ability, a, 1)` (`_DAT_1015df60` = 0.25 as before).
* `GetSkill(stat,2)` `FUN_1006554c(stat,1,0)` = `GetSkill(stat,1) + FUN_1006460b(stat, GetSkill(stat,1), 1)`.
* Skills window (`FUN_100fde49`, GUI): value text = `pending + GetSkill(stat,2)`, colour `GetSkill(stat,2)` vs `FUN_100f90b6 = GetSkill(stat,1)`: equal `0xFFFFFF`, base < value `0x00FF00`, value < base `0xFF0000`. Details panel: `buffed` = value + pending, `base` = `GetSkill(stat,1)` + pending (`Row::value` / `Row::base`, `Model::refresh_buffs`).

**Writers of the maps.** `FUN_100026d0` (vtable `0x1015f9ec` slot 0x30; `FUN_10073a2f` calls it on the `FullCharacterIIR_t` spell list `+0x78` after the stats) runs each `SpellData_t` through `FUN_100a59f5`'s `switch`; the handlers that change the maps (stat = `SpellData::GetStat(0)`, amount = `GetStat(0x27)`): `0xcf14` / `0xcf35` `FUN_100a4c24` (bonus `+=` via `FUN_100644c8`, undo `FUN_1006469b`), `0xcfb7` `FUN_100a87b5` (bonus `+= trunc(level·amount/200.0)`, `_DAT_1015f358` = 200.0, level = `GetStat(0x36,2)`), `0xcff5` `FUN_100a88ee` (bonus `+= trunc(f32(amount/100.0) · GetStat(stat,0))`), `0xcfc0` `FUN_100a8a96` (percent `+=` via `FUN_1006497b`, undo `FUN_10064999`). The fourth map `this+0x14` is `N3Msg_SetSkillTmp` (`FUN_100649b7`, the window's pending ability points, already modelled). Message sources: `FullCharacterIIR_t` spell list (now decoded: `FullCharacter::spells`), `ApplySpellsIIR_t` (apply 1 / undo 0, `Zone::apply_effects`), item events. `Zone::active_spells` keeps the running spells; `hud_stats/buffs.rs::modifiers` replays them into the maps. Spells with `Target` (standard argument 0x20) 0xe / 0x17 act on the owner / pet relation (`FUN_100026d0`) and are dropped.

**Spell record, corrected.** `SpellFormat_c::SpellFormat_c(bool,bool,int)` [GD 0x1000fa93] adds four standard arguments `(ComplexType 0, SpellStat 3, 1)`, `(0, 4, 0)`, `(4, 0x20, 0)`, `(0, 0x23, -1)` before the per-type list, so a record is `type, id, version 4 | criteria (count + 3 ints each) | std ×4 | type args`. Real record rdb 1000020:101103 (Eye Implant: Sharp Obj, Faded, element `{2, 14}` = one `SpellData_t`): `0xcf35 0 4 | 0 | 1 0 2 9 | 108 2` (stat 108, amount 2); :101104 amount 42, :101105 stat 101 amount 3, :101110 stat 113 amount 105 (only the last two words vary). This also resolves the "tail `1 4 x 0`" of docs/zone/static.md §5: the corpse's `cf27` record is `1 0 0 0` (std) + the seven arguments `0 0 0x1f7 1 4 x 0`. `ao_net::n3::spells` (shared with the movement code) now reads the standard arguments (tests `real_item_spell_words`, `full_character_spell_list`).

**Item events in the data** (survey of rdb 1000020, `id % 503 == 0`): element `{2, n}` is the spell list of event `n`: 0 = Use (nano crystals), 14 = Wear (armor, implants, clusters), 20 / 24 / 26 / 3 / 4 / 10 / 16 rare. The element walk after the stats and the name continues with `{0x14}` / `{0xe}` multimaps (`key, size word, i32s`), `{0x16,*}` (`FUN_1008a007`, attribute entries) and `{4,4}` (`FUN_1002e21e`) whose entry layouts are not decoded here.

**Worn item modifiers.** Wear is element `(2,14)` and weapon SkillPst is `(0x13,0xc)`. Parsed templates and interpolated instances are cached by template IDs/QL and refreshed when equipment changes; social/bag slots do not apply wear effects. Gamecode `FUN_100cba71` matches modifier spells by function and `GetStat(0)`. `FUN_100cb3b2` interpolates from the smaller value's QL endpoint, adding 0.5 before truncation; CF14/CF35/CFB7/CFF5 interpolate argument 39 only (`0x1016b190`: 39, 170 terminator), while CFC0 preserves its argument. `FUN_100cb801` requires equal ordered SkillPst keys and interpolates their values the same way. Section discovery validates a complete sized spell/weight section and rejects ambiguous matches; the full intervening item-element walker remains unported.

**Timed nano modifiers and own health.** Native `FUN_100512af` reads template events 4 and 0 (`10051487`/`10051497` push 4; `100514c7`/`100514d3` push 0), then executes each via `FUN_1004e770` (`100514c2`/`100514ff`), which calls the Beholder stat executor at vtable slot `+0x30`. `FUN_1005064c`, reached from removal `10050afd`, undoes events 4/0 before running removal events 20/28. Login `FUN_100516c8` restores entries through `100512af`. Thus timed templates are an independent source of modifier spells, not a substitute for the FullCharacter/ApplySpells lists; the port does not deduplicate distinct executions by equal spell values. `TimedEffects` reuses the validated template event parser and caches the combined running modifier list until the active nano identities or immediate spells change. Body Boost `1040005:29091`, spell at `0x162`, is `CF35`, target 3, stat 1, amount 20: raw client-computed Life 40 must project as effective max health 60, not 40, without replacing the own pool formula with the dynel header. Regression `body_boost_timed_template_health_lifecycle` covers own/foreign add, refresh without stacking, restored entries, timer-zero retention, independent immediate spells and authoritative removal. No live or automated verification was run for this change.

The skill information page reads the modifier maps published by the HUD executor for Base and next-point IP Cost as well as Total. It does not replay equipment/nano templates on an information click, which would incorrectly resnapshot CFB7/CFF5 against today's level or skills. Regression `timed_ability_and_percent_modifiers_keep_info_base_cost_and_total_consistent` covers the shared formula inputs.

**Apply-time deltas, not frame-time formulas.** `100a59f5` dispatches CFB7 to `100a87b5` and CFF5 to `100a88ee`; both undo handlers read spell-object `+0x18` when `+0x1c` indicates a saved delta. `100026d0` restores that delta from the execution's stat-result map before undo (`1000290f`/`10002997` and equivalent target branches), and records the handler's returned delta after apply (`10002be9..10002c10`). The port's single `AppliedEffects` owner retains each applied occurrence's delta across skill/level refreshes. Existing FullCharacter replacement, ApplySpells undo, nano removal/refresh and changed equipped-item occurrences drive undo/reapply; equal records are not new identities and are not deduplicated across sources. Received messages complete this projection before the next packet can change its inputs. Tests `applied_deltas_survive_level_and_source_changes_and_undo_before_refresh` and `equal_instances_undo_the_selected_original_delta` cover changed source values/level, original-delta undo, replacement snapshots and equal-instance selection. Added, not run in this sweep.

**Life-undo health clamp, before StatIIR feedback.** `FUN_1006469b` subtracts the bonus, then for stat 1 with its fourth argument false compares stored Health (`GetStat(27,2)`) with `GetStat(1,3)` and calls `SetStat(27,new_limit)` when Health exceeds it. `FUN_100a4c24` passes false on CF14/CF35 undo. Direct stat-holder mode 3 (`10064800`) is raw plus bonus, **without percent or ability trickle**. Add `100644c8` instead adjusts the Life bonus to enforce minimum Life 1; it does not write Health. The timed undo path now processes the ordered Remove events before projecting the final active nano list, so a same-nano refresh clamps during its intermediate undo even though the re-added maximum is unchanged. The flow applies this between individual received messages using the existing HudStats tables/cache, writes Zone raw/projected/character/dynel Health, and silently updates Combat's stored Health baseline; no damage classification or formatter is suppressed. Before the in-world HUD exists, login headers initialize Health and the unused undo queue is discarded.

`captures/body_boost_remove.rec` retains actual own initial nano/SCFU/FullCharacter and Health packets from `/tmp/LevelUp/final.rec`, then BuffIIR at 15071 ms and Health StatIIR at 15163 ms: raw Life 46, Health 55 → local clamp 46 → packet delta 0. `captures/body_boost_refresh.rec` retains actual initialization and the same-batch 17275 ms BuffIIR → add 29091 → Health 40 sequence from `/tmp/LevelUp/body5.rec`: raw Life 40, Health 52 → clamp 40 → maximum restored to 60. Regression `captured_life_undo_clamps_zone_and_combat_before_same_batch_stat_feedback` replays both without a render tick and checks no false combat log/floating feedback; replaying the same lower Health packet without undo still produces ordinary negative-delta feedback. `captured_same_nano_refresh_clamps_before_readding_unchanged_maximum` also replays the captured recast action against the old active entry and checks its intermediate 40 clamp before the maximum returns to 60. These checks were added, not run.

SCFU re-placement is authoritative restoration, not a timed undo after its new Health header. Its visual Remove events therefore do not enter the modifier-undo queue; final modifier projection still refreshes restored entries. The own raw Health is assigned from the SCFU header immediately (raw Life remains client-computed). `captured_scfu_replacement_preserves_authoritative_health_with_hud_present` replays the actual final-capture SCFU against an initialized HUD with an old active Body Boost: max 66 minus encoded delta 14 yields Health 52, which must survive above raw Life 46 without a following FullCharacter/StatIIR masking a mistaken 46 clamp. The added regression checks raw/projected/character/dynel and Combat Health and restored max 66; it was not run by this worker.

**CF16 delayed local stat undo, implemented.** `100026d0` queues CF16 through `10002595` into the stat holder's event9 list; `SimpleChar::Run` (`1005b59a`) calls `10002fc7` every character frame, which executes these instances and erases execution-count-zero entries. Assembly `100a5a6b..100a5a8a` gates initial apply by `abs(GetStat(4))` against `trunc((GameTime+0x28 - spell-object+8) * 100.0)` (`double 0x10158670 = 100.0`). `100a5af1..100a5b41` adds once; `100a5b46..100a5b80` undoes at `abs(GetStat(4)) + GetStat(0x19)` and zeros the execution count. Thus arguments4/25 are centiseconds, not NCU seconds. An explicit undo-flagged CF16 returns without cancelling the original queued execution (`100a5aeb..100a5aef`). `100a4874` / `100a49c0` initialize its start to current game time; count>1 and positive interval get the native same-clock integer-seconds backdating (`100a5a4e..100a5a68`). FullCharacter's existing `+0x78` list is read by `10073881` → `100a6c58` → `100a5429` as SpellData (fresh start, not a serialized remaining NCU time), then `10073a2f` executes it through the same queue. There is no additional wire block to decode.

`AppliedEffects` owns these queued executions alongside its ordinary apply-time deltas. Source removal/refresh does not cancel or refresh an already queued CF16; a new source occurrence queues a distinct execution. Its frame tick performs local undo and the existing ordered Life/Health clamp, forwarding corrected Health silently to Combat. It never removes an authoritative NCU entry. Regression `cf16_queue_delays_undoes_locally_and_survives_source_undo_and_replacement` covers initial delay, expiration, explicit-undo survival, FullCharacter-style reapplication, equal source replacement and intermediate Life limits before a replacement adds. No checks were run.

**UNRESOLVED.** `FUN_100644c8` minimum-Life bonus adjustment and ability-change signal side effects remain unported. Stat `0xf7` (`FUN_10062526`, lock percentage) has no name in the client's stat table.


## 11.13 HudWinB: Team, Perks and Faction windows (`play/hud_team.rs`, `hud_perks.rs`, `hud_faction.rs`, `hud_winb.rs`, `hud_dialog.rs`; ao-net `n3/team.rs`)
All GUI.dll / Gamecode.dll addresses below were decompiled (Ghidra copies in /tmp, project `GUI.dll` + `Gamecode.dll`). Text keys are text.mdb category 10000 (`GetText(0x2710, key)`).

**Team window `team_view`** (`TeamView_c` ctor `FUN_100785f0`, `TeamViewModule_c` 0x1007b9be). Title key `Team`; a `DockableView` initially in `DockArea1` (`prefs/NewChar/DockAreas/DockArea1.xml`, frame origin (1836, 893)), now managed by the same grouped docking controller as the other dockable windows (§6.5). Children in order: header `BorderView` (client borders 4,2,4,2) with `NoTeam` or `TeamLvlTeamSide` (`FUN_100779db`: level, `GetText(0x7d5, side)`), button row (borders 0,5,0,5; `FUN_10077fb3`: not in team = spacer + `Recruit`; in team = spacer + `Leave` (borders 5,0,5,0) + for the leader `Recruit`), six `TeamBar_c` rows (`FUN_100791f7`): `BitmapView 0xd6` separators (the first row also on top), index text (borders 3,2,0,2), name text (5,2,5,2), two `PowerbarView` (health bg 0x1b / fill 0x1a, nano 0x1d / 0x1c; values `GetSkill(0x1b)/GetSkill(1)` and `(0xd6)/(0xdd)`, `FUN_100775ac`). Name colour: leader 0x11eeaa, known dynel 0xffffff, unknown 0x888888; the targeted member gets a 0x88aadd fill from x = 20 (`FUN_10078cad`). Left click = `TargetMember` (`AFCM::Send(0x1e,0x127,id)`), right click = popup `FUN_10079c16`: `InfoOn`+name (`InfoViewModule_c::ShowURL("charid://kind/instance")`, `FUN_10079a2a`), and for the leader `MakeXLeader` (`N3Msg_TransferTeamLeadership`) / `Kick_`+name (`N3Msg_KickTeamMember`). Recruit: `FUN_10077c8e` (no target `MustSelectChar2Join`, self `CantInviteSelf`, else `N3Msg_TeamJoinRequest(target,false)` + system line `JoinTeamRequestSentTo`+name); it is enabled for a selected non-self, non-NPC, non-member (`FUN_1007791c`). Leave = `N3Msg_LeaveTeam`.
Wire (ao-net `n3/team.rs`, byte tests there): `TeamMemberIIR_t` 0x46312D2E (read `FUN_10079fa1`: Identity member, Identity team, i32, i32, u16, i32-len name), `TeamMemberInfoIIR_t` 0x28784248 (`FUN_1007a1fc`: member, nano, max nano, max health, health -> stats 0xd6/0xdd/1/0x1b), `TeamInviteIIR_t` 0x4D2A313B (`FUN_100a4002`), actions via `CharacterActionIIR_t`: kick 0x16, leave 0x18, transfer 0x19, join request 0x1a (`b={0,bool}`), reply `N3Msg_RequestReply` 0x1c (invitation Yes/No dialog `Want2JoinXsTeam`, `FUN_1007a1f6`); received 0x20 = member removed, 0x23 = leader (`FUN_1005d0d8` cases 8, 9). No capture contains team traffic: tests use frames built by the encoders.
**Window sizes / places:** Team restores the `DockArea1.xml` outer frame (195×158 inclusive Rect) and moves inside the screen. `TeamBar_c` **0x100791f7** derives its six row heights from horizontal index/name/spacers and a vertical health/nano pair, with2px borders; the port now uses that layout instead of fixed16px stacked rows. Content preferred min/max, not the template frame, govern resize (§6.6). Faction cells use `GFX_GUI_HOR_BAR_EMPTY` (179×23), and initial placement is centred when bars are built (`HudFaction::update`). Tests `hud_team::tests::opens_inside_the_screen`, `hud_faction::tests::window_holds_seven_bars_and_is_centred`.
**Team leader:** Gamecode constructor `FUN_100658b9` initializes leader identity fields `+0x14/+0x18` to zero; `FUN_10065718` sets them from the action `0x23` identity and recomputes the own-leader flag. `TeamState::on_member` never infers leadership from member order; regression `join_info_leader_and_leave` requires the authoritative action before own-leader controls become available.
Team menu `InfoOn` requests (`GUI 0x10079a2a`, `charid://kind/instance`) drain through `Hud::take_info_urls` into the same Chat InfoView flow as other HUD links; regression `team_info_urls_reach_shared_info_view_queue` covers delivery and one-shot draining.
**Team header values:** `GetTeamLevel` (`GUI 0x10079fae`) reads module `+0xec`, initialized to 1 (`0x1007b9be`), replaced by event `0x10`'s identity instance (`0x1007a5f2`) and reset to 1 on own leave/removal. Its Gamecode emitter is `0x1005d2b5`: received character action **0x1b** (byte table `0x1005efdf[0x1b-1] = 6`, jump table `0x1005ee43[6] = 0x1005d2b5`) forwards `identity_a` only for the controlled character. Member levels never determine team level. `GetTeamSide` (`GUI 0x10079fa7`) reads `+0xe8`, initially 0, updated by event `0x36`; Gamecode `0x10004bf5` emits that event for own **TeamSide stat 213 / 0xd5** updates, with the new value in the identity instance. The port reads that already-replicated own stat, not Side stat 33. Regressions `team_level_uses_own_action_value_and_resets_on_leave` and `team_header_uses_team_side_instead_of_character_side` cover the header contract (added, not run in this headless sweep).

**Resolved retained team UI:** `10078cad`, specifically `10078cf7`, copies the name TextView at `TeamBar+0x180`'s parent-local frame, overwrites only left20 (`101b4e08`) and right `TeamBar.bounds.right - health.frame.Width() - 6` (double `101b36a8`). Top/bottom are the name bounds, not the full horizontal container. The first row's extra top separator must not shift that copied frame; the canvas adapter translates coordinates back to the TeamBar. `native_10078cad_highlight_copies_name_bounds_even_above_first_separator` covers rows0/1 and target changes. Invitation `1007a4eb` supplies category10000 **`JoinTeam?`** (`101b6970`) as constructor name, `Want2JoinXsTeam` as body and Yes/No. **PASS, stale visible-title gap:** style1 does not draw a title strip; the name now passes through existing creation naming, with no invented caption or unused semantic-title state. `native_1007a1f6_invitation_text_title_key_and_answer_events` covers the native title key/body and actual Yes/No mouse request events. No new live/visual verification is claimed. TeamMember field semantics are not changed by this UI work.
Fresh native recheck (2026-10-15): `TeamBar_c` **0x100791f7** installs a vertical root, a horizontal index/spacer/name/spacer/bar-pair child, and separator art **0xd6** (also above the first row). Both text views and the vertical health/nano container receive top/bottom borders **2**; the index left border is **3**, name left/right **5**, bar container left/right **2**. There is no fixed row-height assignment. The existing content-derived implementation therefore closes the former 16px guess; `team_native_background_and_rows_stay_inside_client` additionally checks each horizontal row is `max(normal text height, health art height + nano art height) + 4` pixels, both bars retain their art heights, and nano immediately follows health.

**Faction window `faction_window`** (`FactionView_c` `FUN_1005dc02`, `FactionBar_c` `FUN_1005d94e`/`FUN_1005d68c`). Title literal "Faction"; seven bars for stats 0x236..0x23c (GOS .. ClanRedeemed), borders (2,10,2,1), (2,1,2,1), last (2,1,2,2). Bar `PowerbarView(bg 0xdb, fill 0xdc right / 0xdd left for negative, stat 0x236 always 0xde)`; left label = `GetFactionStr(stat,false)` short name (map built by `FUN_10035c99`: Guardian, Followers, Operators, Unredeemed, Devoted, Conservers, Redeemed), right label `<font color=#ffff88>`title or number (pref `IsFactionTitleShown`, default 1, click toggles, `FUN_1005d647`). Fill = `(v-min)/(max-min)` of the band (`N3Msg_GetFactionRange`), mirrored for v<0. The bands are rdb **1000037** (12 records `i32 min, i32 max, string, string`, loaded by `FUN_100c63bd`; checked in `real_bands_match_the_titles`); titles = band index 1..12 in the map of `FUN_10035c99` (Dreaded .. Family). UNRESOLVED GUESS: window position (centre; no saved frame), label font/placement over the bar, the pref is not persisted.

**Perks window `perk_window`** (`PerkWindowModule_c` 0x10063373, window ctor `FUN_1006372d`: title `PerkWindow`, tabs `ProfessionPerks` / `GroupPerks` / `GeneralPerks`, `SelectedTab`; `PerkView_c` `FUN_100611dc`: labels `UsedPerkPoints`, `AvailablePerkPoints` (+ `...Alien...`) coloured 0xccffff, `RemovePerk` toggle, `UntrainPerkTimeout` `%02u:%02u:%02u`/`--:--:--`). Catalogue = rdb **1000036** (2062 records of 9 LE u32: item (rdb 1000020), precursor, flags, profession mask, ...; `FUN_1002babd`). Listing rules `LoadPerks` `FUN_10062f21`: flag bit0 never shown; alien (bit1) needs Expansion&8, others need flags&0xc0==0 and Expansion&2; flag bits 2..5 restrict the breed; lines = precursor chains; tab by mask (`IsProfessionPerk`: mask == own profession bit; `IsGroupPerk`: bit in mask; `IsGeneralPerk`: mask 0). Points: all = level/10 (<200) else level-180 (`FUN_10052bfc`), used = known perks with flags&0xc2==0, alien available = stat 0xa9 - used alien. Own perks: perk map of `FullCharacterIIR_t` (`FUN_100537a5`/`FUN_10052859`, `parse_perk_map`) and `PerkUpdateIIR` 0x435F7023 (`FUN_10061f79`, apply `FUN_10063942`). Click flow `FUN_100607f6`/`FUN_10060ea7` with the real texts (`NoPerkPts`, `YouKnowThisPerk`, `MustTrainPrecursoryPerk`, `ReallyTrainPerk` (hours literal 2), `ReallyRemovePerk`, `MustKnowPerk2Untrain`); Yes sends `N3Msg_TrainPerk` (action 0xbb, `b={0,perk}`, `FUN_10052ede`) / `N3Msg_UntrainPerk` (0xbc). Names = item name + " " + position in the line (`FUN_1005366e`).
**Alien progression coverage:** shipped `ActionMenu/CharMenu.xml:27` routes normal level≥10 or AlienLevel≥1 (with Expansion bits `0x2` / `0x8`) to this same `perk_window`. Alien points/green-labelled catalogue lines belong to the native Perks owner, not a fabricated separate AI progression panel; Research has its distinct `research_window` entry at line39 (Expansion bit32).
Perk line roots stack in the scrolling left column; selecting one populates the fixed two-column rank grid on the right (`0x10060702`, `0x1005ec59`). Native global label widths and five-row preferred height survive resize rather than stretching ranks or laying every chain across one long row. `hud_perks::tests::perk_lines_stack_in_the_scroll_view` covers line containment, line selection, rank-column placement and fixed rank dimensions after growing the frame.
**Authoritative train/untrain relays:** GC `1005e4f9..1005e508` and `1005e512..1005e521` both load `[EBP+0x14]`, push `[EAX+4]` (the incoming `identity_b.instance`), resolve the receiving character's perk holder through `10058ab1`, then call `100533b3` / `1005303e`. These are state mutations, not UI-only signals. Own-character `0xbb` now resolves the catalogue and existing native eligibility evaluator, expansion and available points before inserting the learned entry; unknown IDs, foreign identity kinds/instances and duplicate trains cannot manufacture entries or stack effects. `0xbc` removes only a learned entry, updates completed-perk consumers/point counts and records stat577 only when native GameTime state exists. The completed snapshot also reaches inventory equip criteria (ops93/103) through the existing HUD update.
`1005303e` reads **GameTime+0xb4**, the server Unix clock (`Zone::server_now`), not the wrapping +0x50 day clock (`Zone::game_seconds`) or host wall time. The reset label uses the same authoritative clock. PerkUpdate `10063942` also writes stat265 from `stat_109` and clamps the entry timer to zero. Regression `authoritative_perk_relays_update_points_effects_and_reset_time` sends encoded train/untrain/PerkUpdate frames and covers eligibility, invalid IDs, foreign routing, duplicates, point counts, effects and absent/server reset clocks.
The canonical completed-perk projection is `Zone::completed_perks: Option<Vec<u32>>`. Native `GC100051d8` first resolves the catalogue: flag0x80 selects a global goal's timer for current Side33, otherwise it selects the own learned timer; only zero timers pass. The projection retains that source distinction, excludes unknown catalogue IDs, and combines the valid own/global completions. It refreshes with reused vector allocations only after accepted personal mutations, ResearchUpdate or own side changes, not unrelated frames. Native criterion dispatcher `100065d8` case0x5d (93) calls `100051d8` positively; case0x67 (103) calls the **same helper** and negates its result through `LAB10007914`. Thus both use this projection, not a separate own-membership predicate. `None` distinguishes unavailable state from known empty. Inventory, nano, root perk and Research/Mech criteria all consume the same projection; the Research/Mech private completion cache is removed. `completed_projection_tracks_authoritative_timer_frames` covers encoded timer/research/side frames, wrong-source and unknown IDs, and retention of global completion across personal updates (authored, not run).
**Perk spell integration / remaining prerequisite:** zero-timer train calls native wear `10080a79`; zero-timer untrain calls undo `10080cd0`, both executing item event14 through virtual+0x2c. The port reuses the RDB event parser, shared criteria evaluator, `Zone::apply_effects` modifier storage and `OwnEvent::Spells` movement/visual consumers; unsupported predicates are explicit errors, not unconditional grants. Native item class-mask flags, item ownership, event-created item bookkeeping and executor operations not represented by those shared consumers remain unresolved prerequisites for complete wear/undo parity (`10080a79..10080cbf`, `10080cd0..10080da4`); no synthetic state substitutes for them.

**Learned perk actions / dynamic registry (2026-10-06):** `GC 1002ba03` reads catalogue word 4 into runtime `Perk_t+0x14`; `10052aca` compares that word with an `Action_e`. Actual RDB 1000036:111 is `(211656,110,0,32852,10111,0,0,0,0)`; its item 211656 has neither stat 588 nor stat 59. It is therefore wrong to use the learned perk's item as its special-action template. The catalogue retains the action word; the regression checks learned IDs against that word without inventing templates.
The authoritative template registration is `GC10042e16`: it writes the action→template map and adds owner identity kind `0xc78e`. Its caller `1005e49b..1005e4b2` supplies the raw template, owner and action independently; CharacterAction `0xb4` registers them and `0xb3` activates the retained owner (§10.7). `Entry::template` supplies ActionMenu stat588 metadata, Actions icons and hotbar identity lookup. Category1 comes from the registered template, never from the learned perk item.
Availability and progress now use the native lock vectors (§10.7), not learned-map membership: `GC10052aca` resolves action IDs only while scanning active perk locks. Server action-template registration remains authoritative and independent of lock identity. A real perk-bearing capture is still required to demonstrate initial dynamic registration replay against retail; no absent template pairing is reconstructed from learned items. Regression sources include `registered_perk_template_is_authoritative`, `registered_perk_feed_reaches_menu_and_identity_lookup` and the lock/time regressions above.

**Native catalogue eligibility:** GC `10052904` resolves the item's template through `1002bc34`/`1002bb26` and compares the virtual `+0x5c` result specifically with **2**. The vtable is `10162a24` (constructor `10081f4d`), its `+0x5c` implementation `10081815`. Its first argument is the character pointer, not catalogue flags. Item Flags stat30 must contain mask `0x4`; character runtime `+138` mask `0x2` rejects nonexempt armor unless Flags `0x10000000` or ItemType76 is 3/5. `10080abc` sets that runtime mask when equipping class stat0 `0x100` below slot `0x30`; `10080da4` clears it on removal. Action6 criteria are evaluated by `100095a5`/`100065d8`: op0 equality, op2 strict greater, op3 OR, op4 AND, op93 completed perk (`100051d8`, personal timer zero or side-completed global), op108 deliberately true. These are every operator found in the 2062 catalogue records' wear lists. The final `100805b5` returns2 unless class `0x100` conflicts with worn armor stat702, excluding Flags `0x10000000`. `PerkCriteria` parses once using the existing bounded item parser and evaluates without allocation; unknown operators and malformed postfix expressions are errors, not enabled entries. `PerkEquipment` shares a transactional fixed-slot armor cache between the perk and research/vehicle consumers. `10052a24`'s profession enumeration separately scans action6 op0 stat60, accepting any matching profession (or every profession if no such entry). The Perks window's trainability colors and click guard use the same evaluator; GUI `100607f6` shows `DoNotMeetPerkReq` when it fails rather than opening the train confirmation. Regressions `native_catalogue_wear_criteria` and `perk_training_uses_native_item_requirements` cover real item212164, catalogue100/item210830 and every catalogue stream; not executed during this integration.

**Native perk presentation:** `LoadPerks` `10062f21` sorts roots through `10064147`; comparator `10063ad6` reads cached `MeetsPerkCriteria` at model+0x48 first (true first), `HasPerk` at +0x49 second (true first), then `std::string::compare` on unnumbered root names. `1005e8c3` fills those fields; model constructor `100634f5` copies the seven-word runtime catalogue record. `1005f708` recursively obtains the root name and optionally appends the rank number, rather than naming each rank from its own item. `PerkLineView` constructor `1005e941` installs a separately tinted `StatusIcon` (`1005f7de`, shipped GFX **0x121**), uses owner-set toggle state and disables click-driven toggling. `1005e36b` sets learned rank buttons pressed, line buttons selected independently, and adds alien-only label markup **#30ff30**; `1005e2bf(0, criteria, known)` tints only the status icon: learned **0x006e00**, criteria-met **0x646464**, otherwise **0x960000**. Labels retain native button text colours, not the former guessed green/white/gray palette. Bitmap `SetIconView` support follows `1012779d`, `LayoutLabel` `101278d6` and preferred sizing `10127ee4`; ordinary buttons remain unchanged. Regression `real_catalogue_native_order_and_status_icons` covers real catalogue ordering and emitted status-art tints; not executed by this worker.

**Retained training confirmation:** GUI `100607f6` always opens a DialogBox for an eligible, unlearned next rank; `10060ce4` passes category10000 `Info` (`101b3b84`) as constructor name, `ReallyTrainPerk` formats root/rank name and literal2 hours, and Yes/No return indices0/1. No skip-confirm user preference is read in this complete function or present in shipped CharPrefs/LoginPrefs; none is invented. The actual setting is `esc_dialogs`, sampled by native Go `1012a3bf`; HUD synchronization forwards it to Perks/Team. Style1 title visibility is a stale PASS, not a new caption. `native_100607f6_train_confirmation_and_1012a3bf_escape_setting` passed body, No/Escape, disabled Escape, real Yes mouse input and waiting for the unchanged authoritative relay. Native training runtime, catalogue order and Progress5f9ed24 remain intact.

**Retained UI verification (2026-10-16):** rebased onto `366d309` including published Inventory/Relations/Widgets/Progress/Knowledge/body changes. Explicit coordinator-granted headless gates: `cargo test --release -p aomac native_ -j 2` **165 passed,8 ignored** (`artifact://35696`); existing `hud_stats` **65**, `hud_perks` **10**, `hud_team` **10**, `social_win` **9 passed** (`artifact://35700`). Full `cargo test --release --workspace -j 2`: **2132 passed,64 ignored,40 suites**, exit0 (`artifact://35745`). `cargo clippy --release --workspace --all-targets -j 2 -- -D warnings`: **PASS**, exit0 (`artifact://35748`). Private target/prefs, `RUST_TEST_THREADS=2`, muted audio; no login, desktop window/input, screenshots or rendered-caption/live acceptance claims.



The parent offscreen inspection exposed literal alien `<font>` tags because the existing Button renderer draws plain labels. Both root and rank builders now emit plain text plus an independent `label_color=0x30ff30`, preserving status-icon and border colours. The regression asserts markup-free label attributes and actual green glyph commands; this correction has not been rendered or checked by the worker.

Offline frame fixture: `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/progression-shot cargo test --release -p aomac progression_offline_window_shots -- --nocapture` uses the existing offscreen HUD helpers and installed catalogue/art. It writes `offline-perks-native-state.png` (profession7, level10, learned catalogue100), `offline-research-{allocate,available,finished}.png` (empty server research snapshot, no invented completion) and `offline-skills-{full,compact}.png`. This worker did not execute or inspect these frames.

**UNRESOLVED / GUESS (perks):** DialogBox title ("Info"). Constructor geometry, line/rank sizing, eligibility, sort, status art/state, authoritative relays and server reset clock are traced above; no generic cell redesign is used.

Tests: `cargo test --release -p ao-net team`, `-p aomac hud_team hud_perks hud_faction`. No live session / screenshots were taken (see yield notes).

## 10.8 Control-centre fade groups and the rollup backdrop (`ao-gui` `gui/fade.rs`, `play/hud.rs::apply_fades`) — GuiFidelity.FadeGroups

**Retail look:** the wings, bottom bars and the two menus are dimmed until the pointer is over their side (user reference screenshot: both sides dim).
Defaults come from `LoginPrefs.xml`: `CCFadeHigh` 0.85, `CCFadeLow` 0.33, `CCFadeDelay` 2.0 s (options panel sliders `#ControlCenterLow/High/Fade`,
`OptionPanel/Root.xml` 168-170). Nothing about the look is "fixed" to match the reference: the shipped defaults reproduce it.

**XML.** `fade_group` is read by `View::View(TiXmlElement*)` 0x1014e21c (string at 0x101c83e4) and stored with `View::SetFadeGroup` 0x1014ac6d.
`Views/ControlCenter.xml` sets `cc_left_fade_group` on `LeftWingDock`, `LeftBarDock` and the left `CCMenu`, `cc_right_fade_group` on `RightWingDock`,
`RightBarDock` and the right `CCMenu` (never nested, so the alpha does not multiply twice). Engine: `View::fade_group` (parsed in `apply_view_attrs`).

**`FadeGroupController_c`** (ctor 0x1012d4e9, singleton accessor 0x1012d6bf, `Looper` named `fade_group_controller`; created by the first
`SetFadeGroup`, destroyed by `FUN_1012d41c` when the last member leaves). Group table = a `std::map<String, Group>` (`FUN_1012df8e` = `operator[]`).
A `Group` (embedded at node +0x30) = member list, current / from / to alpha (+0xc / +0x10 / +0x14), duration in µs (+0x18, 64 bit), start time (+0x20, 64 bit).
* **Initial state (not an inference):** the group constructor `FUN_1012d2fb` reads `CCFadeLow` into current, from and to (the decompile shows three
  `GetDValue("CCFadeLow")`; the middle one could be a decompiler duplicate but all three are literal), duration 0. So **every group starts dim**; registering a member
  (`FUN_1012d48d`) on an idle group sets a 1 µs fade to the current value so the new member receives it on the next frame.
* **Per frame** (`FUN_1012cfbf` loops the groups -> `FUN_1012cf26`, then `Looper::FrameProcess`): a group with duration != 0 does nothing until
  `now > start`, then `cur = from + (to - from) * elapsed / duration` (linear), and on `elapsed >= duration` `cur = from = to`, duration = 0; every member
  gets `cur` through `vtable+0x2c` (= `View::SetAlpha`, ours `View::alpha`, multiplied down the tree in `draw_view`).
* **Pointer** (mouse handler `FUN_1012cffb`): walk from the hovered view up the parents to the first non-empty fade group (none = ""). If it differs from the
  hot group: the old hot group is faded with `FUN_1012cea2(CCFadeLow, delay = CCFadeDelay * 1e6 µs, duration 1e6 µs)` (asm 0x1012d0fa-0x1012d15b: the
  `PUSH 0 / PUSH 0xf4240` before `AsDouble` are the duration arguments of the non-cleaning call), and the new hot group is poked
  `from = cur, to = CCFadeHigh, duration = 200000 µs, start = now` (no delay).
* **DValue observer** (`FUN_1012d1f4`, observes `CCFadeLow` / `CCFadeHigh`, ctor stores both in the controller): the changed value is stored, then for
  **every** group `FUN_1012cea2(value, 0, 0, 0, 0)` snaps it to that value (all zeros = immediate set + apply to members, asm 0x1012d265-0x1012d275), then the
  hot group fades to `CCFadeHigh` over 0.2 s from now and all others to `CCFadeLow` over 1 s starting **2 s** later (literal 0x1e8480 µs, not `CCFadeDelay`).
  `CCFadeDelay` is not observed; it is read at every pointer change.
* Engine: `gui/fade.rs` `FadeCtl` (pure logic, unit tests `hover_brightens_then_dims_after_delay`, `moving_between_groups_swaps_them`, `params_apply_live`)
  driven by `Gui::frame` (`tick_fade_groups`) and `Gui::update_hover` (called on every mouse move/press: `fade_hover`). `Gui::set_fade_params(low, high, delay)`;
  `Hud::apply_fades` feeds it from `Hud.dvalues` each frame, so changing the dvalues (`/option`, the options panel) applies at once (the `FadeCtl` runs the observer
  only when low/high differ from the stored values). Tests: `cargo test --release -p ao-gui --test fade` (hover walk-up, 0.2 s rise, delay + 1 s fall, live
  parameters), `cargo test --release -p aomac fade_groups` (HUD: dim at start, left side bright on hover, dims after 2 s + 1 s, dvalue change, rollup backdrop; PNGs
  `fade-dim`, `fade-hover-left`, `fade-live` in `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR`).
* **UNRESOLVED:** which view the mouse signal passes to `FUN_1012cffb` (its caller / hit test was not decompiled). We hit-test every visible view of the topmost
  window with a view under the pointer, treating bare layout containers (`Kind::View` without a group) as transparent, because the control centre's screen-sized
  splitter views would otherwise hide the docks. Whether the hover is re-evaluated when the layout changes under a still pointer (we only re-evaluate on input events,
  like a mouse-move handler).

**`cc_rollup_controller_fade_level`** (CharPrefs default 0.0, min 0 / max 1, options slider `#SidebarTranspa`, `OptionPanel/Root.xml` 217): string at 0x101b0d84,
referenced only by the `RollupController_c` factory `FUN_10048346`: the controller owns a `DistributedValue_c` of that name (+0x1c0), creates a `ViewSurface_c`
(+0x210, `Rect(0, 0, _DAT_101b0bcc, _DAT_101b0bcc)`), `SetColor(surface, 0)`, `View::AddRenderSurface(view, surface, true)`, and keeps its alpha at the dvalue: the
slot `FUN_1004814c` (`ViewSurface_c::SetAlpha(+0x210, float)`) is called once with the current value and connected to the dvalue-changed signal
(`FUN_10049dd7` / `FUN_10049e26`). I.e. a **black backdrop behind the whole rollup column (RollupArea) whose opacity is the "Sidebar transparency" option**;
with the default 0.0 it is invisible. Engine: `View::backdrop` (black solid over the view's rect before its content, `draw_view`), `Gui::set_backdrop`, set by
`Hud::apply_fades` on the `RollupArea` view of `fit_rollup_dock`. UNRESOLVED: the surface rect constant `_DAT_101b0bcc` (we paint exactly the area frame, 192 px x
area height, and the pages are separate windows drawn above it); that surface colour 0 is black and the alpha is the only visible part (colour 0 with the original
`ViewSurface_c` blend was not traced). The `MouseWheel` dvalue at +0x160 of the same controller is the wheel-scroll switch (`FUN_1004816f`), not a fade item.

Docked rollup page windows use a screen-space viewport `[W−191, 20, W, H−224)` (clamped to the screen); freeing a page removes it.
The inclusive retail area is `Rect(W−191, 20, W, H−225)`, established by GUI `InitialiseMessage` 0x1006a968,
asm 0x1006ab32..0x1006ab75 and constants 0x101b4e00/08/10. Shared parent clipping is traced in
`View::_CallRender` 0x1014d2e3: `ConvertToScreen` bounds intersect the inherited rect (`Rect::operator&`), which is passed recursively to children.
Flag `0x1` applies the floored intersection through `RViewPort_t::SetViewRect` with right/bottom +1, then restores the previous viewport.
**[INFERENCE]** applying that containment to the rollup area: the page parenting / clip-flag setup has not been established by this trace.
Nested canvas, scroll, editable-text, tab, list-header and fading-text clips
intersect and restore the window viewport; clipped-away points do not receive mouse hits, tooltips or fade hover.
Fully outside pages are culled against the area, not the whole screen; free windows and post-window overlays remain unclipped.

Full-height page regression: commit `f78c6e5`, `play/hud_rollup.rs:196`, replaced the stacked `body` View
(content beside a fixed-size `body_bg` sibling) with a vertical `body` CanvasView. Child elastic maxima then propagated
into the page's preferred height: `ao-gui/src/layout.rs::pref_plain` computes `max(child_preferred, min_size, max_size)`
before clamping to `max_limit`, so `max_size` is a preferred-size floor, not a ceiling.
Source-history bisection on wrapper shape identifies `f78c6e5`; a frozen old/new-wrapper regression isolates this transition.
The rollup controller must impose the configured body height as a hard ceiling while docked, and release that ceiling
for a free DockWindow; clipping alone hides the overflow but does not restore the following pages' positions.

Screenshot regression: `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/rollup-shot cargo test --release -p aomac scrolled_appended_page_offscreen_clip`
renders a scrolled appended shop-like page with nested ScrollView/CanvasView to `rollup-clipped.png`, asserting visible green content
inside the area and no green pixels above y=20. The screenshot test requires the client install and `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR`.
`stacked_programs_wear_actions_respect_page_heights` additionally writes `rollup-stacked.png` through the same offscreen
`AOMAC_SHOT_DIR` pattern: fully expanded real Programs/Wear/Actions pages at 1280×1024 with template/natural heights,
checking body/header heights, stacking, visibility, restore and free/redock transitions. At 1280×800 it retains those
heights and checks overflow scrolling (`rollup-stacked-800.png`), rather than shrinking or collapsing pages to fit.
Its optional `rollup-before.png` recreates the missing-hard-ceiling behavior with the same real owners at 1280×800.

## Options window (`play/options.rs`, `options/{model,live}.rs`)

* **Opened by** the Windows menu entry `optionpanel_window` (`ActionMenu/Windows.xml`, `label="#Setting"`) and `/open Settings` (`chat/cmd.rs` table): the dvalue toggles `WindowKind::Options`. Module `OptionPanelModule_c` ctor GUI 0x100c2c03 (module name `optionpanel_window`), `ModuleActivated` 0x100c2b7a builds `OptionWindow_c` (`FUN_100c3c44`): style-0 `Window(Rect(200,200,600,400), flags 0x1000)`, tabs "Preferences" / "Fixed keys" / "Key bindings", configured by the `OptionWindowConfig` archive (`WindowFrame`, `open_panel_folders`, `selected_panel`, `selected_tab`, `panel_list_scroll_offset`; the installer template `prefs/NewChar/Prefs.xml` has Rect(200,200,651,670), `GUIControl Center`). We save the archive on close; frame/folders/selection restore (test `closing_and_reopening_restores_selection_and_frame`).
* **Layout**: `OptionCategoryPanel_c` `FUN_100c1053` = category tree (`StringListView`, folder icons 0xd5/0xd4, leaf 0xd8; level ids are the concatenated label segments, `FUN_100c3691`) + two buttons `Quit2Windows` / `Quit2Login` (text.mdb 10000; slots `LAB_100c1027/103d` = `AFCM::Send(10, 0x133 / 0x134)` = `/quit` / `/camp`) next to a `ViewSelector_c` with one page per `ScrollView` of `OptionPanel/Root.xml`. Labels `#Key` = text.mdb 700 (`FUN_100c22fc`), tooltips via `tooltip`/`tooltip_body`.
* **Population and selection regression**: all `Root.xml` pages remain present; category `ListSelected` events select the corresponding `pages` child, without reconstructing the window. Options/fixed-key/binding population uses `Gui::batch_updates` to measure each changed list and relayout each window once at the end (nested batches included); unchanged `KeyBindings` archives are not reparsed each frame. Layout reclamps scroll offsets after content shrinks, including collapsing category folders. Port regression tests: `category_selection_keeps_quit_buttons_inside_preferences` exercises emitted tree selection across every shipped page and both quit actions; `nested_population_batch_matches_immediate_list_layout` and `collapsing_scrolled_folder_clamps_offset_without_another_scroll_event` cover the shared widget paths. These tests require client data and skip when it is absent.
* **Selection root cause (GUI.dll)**: `ViewSelector_c::SetValue` 0x1015002a hides/shows pages and sets the selected child's frame to the selector's existing bounds. With default `use_max_size=true`, it does **not** notify a preferred-size change or reflow ancestors. The port's full-window relayout on both list selection and page selection let the Windows page's long NOTE minimum steal the category pane's width and move the quit buttons outside the frame. Selection now only repaints the list and sizes the selected page; population still establishes the initial root layout. `OptionWindow_c` 0x100c3c44 additionally caps the whole category panel (tree and quit buttons) to its minimum preferred width with height limit 16000 (`100c40f4..100c4125`, constant 0x101bcecc); `Gui::fit_view_width` reproduces this. Category scrolling reserves its vertical scrollbar (ctor 0x100c1053, mode 3).
* **Native all-page preferred sizing**: `ViewSelector_c::Initialize` 0x1014fd77 sets `use_max_size=true`; `CalculatePreferredSize` 0x1014fe68 takes the componentwise largest preferred size across every page, including hidden pages, for both minimum and maximum queries. The port now follows that contract rather than anchoring preferences to the first selected page. `SetValue` 0x1015002a still only changes visibility and sizes the selected child to the existing selector bounds; it does not reflow ancestors in this default mode. `selector_limits_include_every_page_componentwise_even_when_hidden` covers minimum/maximum aggregation and selection independence. Any previously noted Options text-minimum constraint must be investigated against its own native layout rather than suppressing other pages' preferred sizes.
* **Real-window validation (2026-10-06)**: muted `aomac play --server Ithaca`, owned-PID CGEvent input, 960×628 window, character Aomacrceg in playfield 4582. Before the change, clicking GUI → Window reproduced the missing tree and left-leaking quit buttons; after the change, switching Control Center → Window, collapsing/reopening GUI, and dragging the frame preserved both columns and buttons. `AOMAC_PERF=1` reports half-second frame aggregates: opening peaked at 106.92 ms/frame before, below 6 ms/frame after; drag aggregates were 30.95 / 24.74 ms before versus approximately 4.4–4.6 ms after (idle approximately 7.3 versus 4.4 ms). `sample <owned-pid> 5` before showed thousands of samples in `HudTeam::update → Gui::relayout_window → layout::pref/node_calc`; neither after sample contained a `relayout`/`node_calc` stack. These are observed workload measurements, not per-event latency or a general GPU benchmark. Window-only captures were inspected and deleted; both app instances were closed and the live lock released.
* **Controls** (`OptionControl_c` `FUN_100c22fc`): `opt_type` int/float = `IndependentPrefs` (login or char set by `opt_category`), else DValue; `OptionCheckBox_c` `FUN_100c1be9` (toggle Button art 0x8b/0x8a, `opt_bitnum` sets one bit, `FUN_100c1ae6`), `OptionSlider_c` `FUN_100c0aa9` (label, value text `String::Format(value_fmt, v * value_scale)` `FUN_100c082a`, `Slider_c` ctor 0x1014448a with the variable's min/max, every move writes via `FUN_100c08c5` -> `FUN_100c2052`), `OptionRadioButtonGroup_c` `FUN_100c2fd5`. `view_enable_expression` = `View::SetEnableExpression` (frame timer): the control is *disabled* (0x909090), not hidden. The stored value is mirrored back every frame (original: `DistributedValue_c::Observe` / pref callbacks `FUN_100c0934`).
* **Defaults** are the DValue/pref defaults (`DValues::new`); test `every_control_is_bound_to_a_real_variable_with_the_client_default` checks all 147 options exist. `AutoTargetMOB/PvP`, `DisableXPGain` are created by `MiscOptionsMonitor_c` (`FUN_100bff11`) [INFERENCE: DistributedValue_c(name) creates them; not persisted].
* **Session consumers** (`options/live.rs`, `MiscOptionsMonitor_c`): `ToggleAllEffects` sets the 13 effect prefs (`FUN_100bfa27`); stat 0x15d bits 1/3/4 <-> AutoTargetMOB/PvP/DisableXPGain and stat 0x2a1 <-> VisualFlags/VisiblePVPTitle send `N3Msg_EventFeedback` 0x3c / 0x46 (Gamecode 0x1001cbb4: CharacterActionIIR actions 0xa5 / 0xa6). `SoundOptionsMonitor_c` (0x100c4cc9): master/FX/music/on-off/BattlemusicMode go to `Audio::set_prefs` each frame, voice on/volume scale `play_sfx` (0x100c487a/489b).
* **Not ported / unresolved**: hotkey tabs: ported, see "Hot key pages" below; the "bit 5 while fighting -> Feedback_CantDoThisWhileFighting" refusal; `VoiceSndFxHearFriendsOn` has no reader in the original either (string referenced nowhere in GUI.dll, re-confirmed this session with `re.sh GUI DumpRefs.java "VoiceSndFxHearFriendsOn"`: no hits); ChatFontName/Style/Size are not in Root.xml (chat config window). Stored only, consumer not ported (feature genuinely absent here): `Advertisements`/`VideoAdds` (no reader in any shipped DLL/EXE), `LockEquipment` (read by Gamecode `FUN_1002654d` but not wired into the wear-window drops, see below), `AutoCloseWindows` (no reader in any shipped DLL/EXE either, re-confirmed this session with `re.sh GUI DumpRefs.java "AutoCloseWindows"`: no hits), `AutoRejectMentorInvites`, `UseOffscreenSurfaceTechnology`, and the `esc_*` options of windows/dialogs this client does not have (see "live consumers added later" below for the full list and for every other option a previous draft of this paragraph listed as stored-only, all of which now have a real consumer).
* Screenshots: `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR=/tmp/x cargo test --release -p aomac -- options_shots` (options_first / cc / effects / audio / chat / mouse).

### Hot key pages: "Fixed keys" and "Key bindings" (`options/keys.rs`, `options/keypage.rs`, `controls.rs`, `hud_keys.rs`)
RE (GUI.dll, all read in the decompile / asm of the install): `OptionWindow_c` `FUN_100c3c44` appends the tabs `"Preferences"`, `"Fixed keys"` (only when `OptionPanel/HotKeys.xml` loads: `HotKeyPanel_c` `FUN_100bef0a`) and `"Key bindings"` (`BindView_c` `FUN_100bc91b`); `selected_tab` and the `hotkey_config` sub-archive (`FUN_100bd921`: `open_hotkey_folders`, `selected_hotkey`, `list_scroll_offset`) live in `OptionWindowConfig`.
* **Two separate stores.** (1) The **fixed hot keys** are `InputConfig_t` `Hotkey_t`s parsed from a text block compiled into GUI.dll (`SetDefaultHotkeys` 0x1001ad88 -> `ParseFile` 0x1001ac19 -> `CreateHotkey` 0x1001a80a; lines `KEY_ID COMMAND [& modes ! modes] = KEY + KEY`, `~ KEY` = release). `Hotkey_t+8` = key id | SHIFT 0x20000 | CTRL 0x40000 | ALT 0x80000 (| 0x100000 release). `SetDefaultLoginPrefs` 0x10124b33 ends with one `InitDefaultInt(KEY_NAME, key, 0, 999999999)` per hot key that has a `KEY_` id (28 of them, `keys::FIXED`; names with the `_RELEASE` variants), so they are **`Login.cfg` ints** (`IndependentPrefs`, e.g. `KEY_NEXT_FRIENDLY_TARGET 262159`), and `Preferences_t::LoadKeyboard` 0x1012488b copies every `KEY_*` int over the default with `InputConfig_t::ChangeHotkeyKeystroke` 0x1001a6ba. (2) The **rebindable actions** are *providers* in the binding manager singleton (`FUN_10018b7c`, 0x28 bytes): provider map `hash -> Provider` (`FUN_10018788(name, label, group, mask, variant, 2 flags)`, hash = `FUN_10019132` = `controls::provider_hash` of the name) and input map `input -> vector<hash>` (+0x14), `Version` +0x20 (7), capture flag +0x24. Persisted as the char-prefs `KeyBindings` archive (`FUN_10018498`: `Version`, `Array Bind` of `Archive{Int32 Input, Int64 Provider*}`, inputs ascending, only inputs with a provider; loaded by `FUN_10018bbf`; `Reset All` = `DistributedValue_c::ResetToDefault("KeyBindings")` + reload).
* **Registry** (`keys::registry`): the 70 fixed providers of the call sites of 0x10018788 (`SetupProviders` 0x10068c38 windows/actions, `FlowControlModule_t` 0x1002abd6 movement/camera/pick-up, `TargetingModule_t` 0x10026322 self/pets, `TeamViewModule_c` 0x1007b9be team members, `TextInputModule_t` 0x100220aa clipboard, `BrowserModule_c` 0x1010bcbf) plus the 10 x (10 + 3) shortcut-bar providers (`FUN_100d9a81`; label = text `ShortcutBarKey|Select|Row|Active` of category 0x7db with the **1-based** bar / slot as `%02d`, group `#ShortcutBar`). All 99 provider hashes of the shipped `CharPrefs.xml` resolve to a registered name (test `shipped_archive_resolves_against_the_registry`). Texts: label `#Key` = `GetText(0x7db, Key)`, group = `GetText(0x7dc, Key)`, a name without `#` is literal (`"Daily Login"`).
* **Key names / display.** `m_asInputMethodIDTable` (127 `{name, id}` entries indexed by *position*, `GetInputModeName` 0x10019c77: `table[(input & 0x1ffff)*2]`, ids >= 0x80 give null) = `keys::NAMES` (mouse entries 2/3, 6/7, 10/11 keep the original's swapped order). Bind column / dialog text: `<font color=aqua>` + `CTRL+` `ALT+` `SHIFT+` (this order, none for the modifier keys 0x11..0x16, `FUN_1001888d`) + name + `</font>`; the Fixed list shows the modifiers always and `?` for an unnamed id (`FUN_100bdf77`); unbound = text `NoKey` (`<font color=red>NONE</font>`).
* **Fixed keys page.** `FUN_100be84b` walks `HotKeys.xml`: `<HotKeyGroup label mode>` = folder item (`HotKeyListItem_c` `FUN_100be24e`, non-selectable, `OpenFolder` when its path id is in `open_hotkey_folders`), `mode="fixed"` makes the rows non-selectable; `<HotKey down_id="id:KEY_*" label>` = row showing `GetHotkey(id)+8`. The shipped file has one outer `#FixedKeys` group with Chat (4), Targeting (4) and Camera keys 3rd person (8 rows). Port: `StringListView` tree, key text in aqua at a column after the widest label (`ListItem::aux`, `ao-gui`). **UNRESOLVED:** the exact `KeyListItemView_c` layout (our column x = indent + widest label + 15) and the separator `FUN_100bf37c` puts between the chained label ids (we use `/`; ids contain the label markup).
* **Key bindings page.** `MultiListView(flags 0x40)`, columns Category (100) / Function (200) / Key (100), flags 0xe; rows from `FUN_100bc07b`: for each provider in map order (**ascending hash**) one row per bound input (ascending), one `NoKey` row when unbound. Buttons (literal English): `Reset All`, `Add`, `Change`, `Clear`. Add / Change open the dialog `"Bind Key"` (`FUN_100bc60d`; text `BindDialogText` + `<br><br>` + the key, buttons `MsgBox_OK` / `MsgBox_Cancel`); while it is open the manager ignores input (+0x24) and the key slot `FUN_100bc5cf` (ALT if mask&0x30, CTRL &0xc, SHIFT &3) / mouse slot `FUN_100bc58e` (inputs 1/5/9/0x79/0x7a) update the shown key; OK (`FUN_100bc229`, button 0) commits: **Add** on a provider that already has a key = `FUN_10018af9` (adds the pair unless present) and a new row, otherwise (and **Change**) `FUN_10018b3a(oldInput, provider)` then `FUN_10018af9(new, provider)`; **Clear** (`FUN_100bbff6`) removes the pair and deletes the row when the provider has other keys, else the row shows `NoKey`. **There is no conflict rule**: one key may carry several providers (`FUN_100180fe` calls every provider of the input) and one provider several keys; only the identical pair is refused.
* **Port.** One table feeds every consumer: `Hud` (`sync_keys`) reloads `Bindings` from the DValue `KeyBindings` and `FixedKeys` from the `KEY_*` login ints; `Controls::set_keys` (movement, camera, combat, pick-up providers of `controls::PROVIDERS`, fixed camera keys), `hud_keys::KeyMap` (WINDOW_* incl. `WINDOW_OPTIONS` = F10, shortcut-bar providers, `KEY_OPEN_PERK_WINDOW`) and `hud_target` (`KEY_{NEXT,PREV}_{HOSTILE,FRIENDLY}_TARGET`) read it, so a rebind acts on the next key press. `Play::game_input` (physical key stream, any key) drives the HUD hot keys and the bind dialog (the old character-only `InputEvent::Key` path is gone). The page edits the DValue immediately (the original writes it later, FUN_100bbeb7). `Controls` presses every slot of a key and releases all of them with the key.
* **Deviations / gaps.** OK without a pressed key binds input 0 like the original (`FUN_100bc229` has no guard: Change = the key is removed and the row shows `NoKey`; the archive keeps an `Input 0` entry). Esc closes the dialog instead of being bound ([INFERENCE], `DialogBox_c::SlotEscPressed`); left / right mouse buttons click the dialog's buttons so they cannot be bound, X buttons do not exist in `ao_gui`. If the Change target is already bound to that function the old key stays removed (the original ignores the `FUN_10018b3a` result too, but leaves a stale row; ours shows the table). The literal `"Bind Key"` is the first `DialogBox_c` constructor argument (asm 0x100bc6c0); nothing shows that the style-1 dialog frame draws it as a caption (no retail dialog screenshot), so no caption is drawn. The `Version` upgrade pass at the end of `FUN_10018bbf` (re-adds defaults when the saved `Version` is lower) is not resolved; a saved archive replaces the defaults. Providers without a consumer here (acknowledged, bindable, no effect): `TARGET_SELECT*`, `TEXT_COPYTOCLIPBOARD`, `ACTION_USE/SNEAK/RELOAD/LOOKAT`, `WINDOW_LFT/RESEARCH/RAID/DL/BROWSER/ITEMSHOP`. Knowledge/CreateRef, shortcut visibility and Tradeskill providers now have real consumers (§12.1). Fixed-key consumers (all read `FixedKeys`, i.e. the `Login.cfg` `KEY_*` ints): camera numpad keys (`controls.rs`), target cycling (`hud_target.rs`), `KEY_OPEN_PERK_WINDOW`, chat `KEY_COMMAND_START_CHAT_INPUT` / `_CMD_INPUT` / `_REPLY_INPUT` (`chat.rs`; the SHIFT/ALT+ENTER and NUMPAD_DIV lines have no `KEY_` id and stay fixed), `KEY_COMMAND_CHAT_HISTORY_PAGE_UP/DOWN` (`Chat::scroll_history`: one page = the output view height, the original step is UNRESOLVED; PgUp/PgDn did nothing before) and `KEY_TOGGLE_CONTROL_CENTER` (flips `cc_rollup_panel`, which now shows/hides the `RollupArea` column; handler `SwitchLayoutMessage` 0x10067e7e is the only reader of that value besides `FUN_10049897`, the command -> message link is [INFERENCE]). The debug / `KEY_COMMAND_DEBUG_*` keys have no consumer.
* Tests: `options::keys::tests` (archive round trip, encoding, conflict rule, registry vs `CharPrefs.xml`, fixed table == the text inside GUI.dll, `Login.cfg` override), `controls::tests` (several providers per key, fixed camera keys from prefs), `hud_keys::tests` (any key, shipped table), `options::tests` (3 tabs, Fixed keys rows, rebind -> controls react, add / clear / reset, `hotkey_config` persistence, `key_page_shots` with `AOMAC_SHOT_DIR`: `options_fixed_keys.png`, `options_key_bindings.png`, `options_bind_key.png`). Side fix: `hud_dialog.rs` dialogs had a 16000 px tall text view (`max_size` height 16000 + text set after the size was fixed): now `max_size Point(w,-1)` with the text in `value`.

### Options window: renderer / world quality prefs, RE status (FadeCharacter round)
* **Ported with a live consumer**: `FadeCharacter*` (docs/zone/avatar.md §5, `RefreshAlpha` DisplaySystem 0x10073b7f), `UseNoBobCamera` (docs/zone/camera.md §3, N3 0x10020bdb), `FogMode` (`VisualFog_t::SetFogMode` DisplaySystem 0x10058409 via the changed callback `FUN_10058571`, registered by the `VisualFog_t` constructor 0x100585a1: mode 0 stores 0.1 at `VisualFog_t+0x30`, 1..3 store 1.0; `VisualFog_t::process` 0x10058443 starts with `D *= +0x30`, the fog end follows `far - (far - near - 5)·D`; modes 1..3 are identical, `Randy_t::SetFogParameters` 0x10041876 ignores both mode flags `FUN_100790d4` passes. Port: `ao_scene::fog_mode_density_scale` -> `Host::fog_density_scale` -> `Renderer::set_fog_density_scale` -> `FogModel::at_scaled`, every frame in `flow.rs`; test `fog_mode_zero_scales_the_density`. The "Off" label of mode 0 is what the options window shows, the binary only dims the fog to 10 %). `ViewDistance` / `DisplayCharViewDistance` were already live (docs/chat/dvalue.md).
* **`AspectRation` is an output, not a setting**: `DisplaySystem_t::ResizeViewPort` (0x10079258) writes the viewport aspect into the pref (`SetPrefFloat`, fire) and calls `RCamera_t::SetViewPlaneWindow(fov, aspect)`; `n3EngineClient_t::CreateCamera` (N3 0x10007842) reads it only as the initial aspect of the camera. Our lens takes the aspect from the render target every frame (`Lens::vertical_fov(aspect)`), the same effective behaviour; the pref value is not written back (`Login.cfg` keeps the default 1.3333).
* **Ported** (the ground-quality/ROAM merge added a real consumer; this paragraph's RE evidence is unchanged, re-checked this session against a fresh `re.sh N3 DumpFn.java 0x100083b2`): `DisplayGroundQuality` (100..200, default 200) / `DisplayGroundFullQualityRadius` (7..44, default 44, option shown as "%"): consumer `n3GroundRenderer_t` ctor (N3 0x100083b2) -> `SlotGroundQualityChanged` 0x10008167 / `SlotGroundFQRadiusChanged` 0x100081d3 -> `AnarchyGround_t::SetQuality` 0x10032546 (`+0x1a4`) / `SetHQRadius` 0x1003255b (`+0x1a8`) -> `FUN_1003216f` rebuilds the 256 entry split-threshold table at `+0x1ac`, `table[i] = (int)(2.25^(i · quality / 100) + radius²)` (doubles 100.0 @0x10089e48, 2.25 @0x1008bd70), then `Tesselate` 0x10035348 re-tessellates. The table drives a view-dependent binary-triangle (ROAM style) terrain tessellation: `FUN_10031f3c` splits a triangle while the camera-to-triangle squared distance (`FUN_10031d10`, camera cell at `+0x5d0/+0x5d4`) is below `table[variance level]`, and at the leaf level flags each half `+0x20` (high quality) when it lies within `radius` cells. Port: `ao_scene::ground_detail::thresholds` reproduces the same formula and `GroundDetail::update`/`select` reproduce the variance-split / high-quality-radius selection over a precomputed triangle tree (`crates/ao-scene/src/ground_detail.rs`); `flow.rs` reads `DisplayGroundQuality`/`DisplayGroundFullQualityRadius` every frame into `Host::ground_detail`, `Renderer::set_ground_detail` (`ao-render/src/lib.rs`) stores the clamped pair and `update_ground_detail` reruns the tessellation when the camera cell, quality or radius changes. Test `ao_scene::ground_detail::tests` (quality alone and radius alone each change the selected geometry, matching the native formula).
* `GroundRendering` (0..3, default 3) is live through Login IndependentPrefs → `Host.ground_rendering` → `Renderer::set_ground_rendering`. Native callback 0x100320b2 sets 0 → `+5dc=0` **without clearing `+5e0`**, 1 → `+5dc=1,+5e0=1`, 2/3 → `+5dc=1,+5e0=0`. Draw 0x10034dab first chooses valid `+668` when `!+5e0`, ignoring `+5dc`: fresh 0/2/3 therefore use the fused ADD-palette/MODULATE-tile path; 1 uses `+6ec` SELECTARG1 tile then `+770` ZERO/SRCCOLOR shadow-light multiplier (fog colour white); 1→0 uses `+5e4` and ambient-white material `+a0c`, not the ambient-black `+a08`. The port implements these actual GPU stages over the adaptive ground draws, with independent palette texture sampling rather than baked vertex shadow colours. Test `native_mode_histories` covers the stateful transitions; GPU output/live-window checks remain main-agent verification.

### Options window: live consumers added later (supersedes the "not live" items above)
* `ControlPrefs::from_dvalues` (controls.rs) feeds `Player::set_control_prefs` -> `Controls::set_prefs` / `Camera3p::set_prefs` every frame: `MouseTurnSensitivity`, `ZoomSpeed`, `LMBMouseLook`, `RMBMouseLook1st/3rd`, `ZoomTo1stPerson`, `ShowMyCharacter`, `MouseWheel`, `MouseLookInverted` (login pref). Camera mode state is untouched. Test `control_prefs_follow_the_dvalues`.
* `Hud::sync_options`: `Targetstarget`, `NumHotbars` (shortcut bars are created / closed to match, 1..10) and the target bars' criteria `dvalue:cc_section1 && dvalue:cc_friendly_health_bar` / `..._hostile_health_bar` (docs §10; `HudTarget::bars_enabled`) are read each frame.
* Esc: the window constructors read their `esc_*` DValue when the window is created (`FUN_100cc2ca` reads `esc_inventory` / `esc_chests` / `esc_backpacks`; the Root.xml note says open windows keep the old value) and then close on Esc. `HudOptions::arm_esc/take_esc` do this for inventory, wear, nano, perks, planet map, Research and the options window (test `esc_closes_only_windows_whose_option_was_set_when_they_opened`; Research's existing consumer is described in “Native Research and Vehicle windows” below). Other `esc_*` windows (backpacks, chests, corpses, city terminal, tradeskill, mission selection, tower build menu, vehicle, raid, lft, tips, npcchat, chatconfig) belong to windows this client does not have or owns elsewhere (dialogs/shops/trades use their own default-true paths); not wired.
* `AutoCloseWindows`: the string occurs in no client DLL (grep of all `*.dll`/`*.exe`, re-confirmed this session with `re.sh GUI DumpRefs.java "AutoCloseWindows"`: no hits), so the original has no reader either. `LockEquipment`: read only by Gamecode `FUN_1002654d`, called from `N3Msg_IsItemPossibleToUnWear` (0x10026763) and `N3Msg_IsItemPossibleToWear` (0x10026adb) (true, or an identity in the `EquipmentLocks` archive, refuses): NOT wired into the wear window drops (hud_stats/item_ui.rs) yet. `Advertisements`/`VideoAdds`, `AutoRejectMentorInvites`, `UseOffscreenSurfaceTechnology` remain genuinely stored only, no consumer in `crates/`.
* Every other option a previous draft of this doc listed as "stored only, consumer not ported" now has a real consumer and is **ported**: `LockRaidBars` (`hud_winb.rs`: raid bars ignore drag/resize while set), `ShowIPPerkbutton` (`level_notice.rs`, gates the skill/perk/research level-up notices), `ItemDescriptionTooltips` / `ShortcutShortTooltips` (`hud.rs`, `hud_stats.rs`, `interact_shop.rs`, `interact_ptrade.rs`: item/shortcut tooltip bodies), `NotificationWindowVisibility` (`hud_notification.rs`: frame on/off of the Notifications window), `ChatTextFadeDelay`/`ChatTextFadeTime`/`ChatIndicator`/`ChatWarnWhenSpeakingToUnsubGroups` (`chat.rs`, `chat/win.rs`), `AutoAFK` (`chat.rs`), `ShowDropItemDialog` (`hud_stats.rs`), `ShowHelpButtonWindow` (`hud_help.rs`: our `HelpButton` window, a native port of `HelpButtonWindow_c`), `ShowNanoTargetNCU`/`ShowFightTargetNCU` (`hud_target_ncu.rs`), `MouseLagFix`/`WaitForVertSync` (`flow.rs`, forwarded to the host), `DisplayCompressedTextures`/`GroundTextureQuality` (`flow.rs`/`mod.rs`, texture policy), `UseNoBobCamera` (`controls.rs` `ControlPrefs::no_bob_camera` -> `camera.rs` `Camera3p`, docs/zone/camera.md §3) and `FadeCharacter*` (`avatar.rs`/`player.rs`, docs/zone/avatar.md §5). `DisplayGroundQuality`/`DisplayGroundFullQualityRadius` and `GroundRendering` are ported, see above. `Targetstarget`, `NumHotbars` and `cc_friendly_health_bar`/`cc_hostile_health_bar` are likewise read every frame by `Hud::sync_options` above, not once at HUD creation.

### Contextual help: proven static mode, unresolved dispatch

`HelpButtonWindow_c` click 0x10072169 → `StartHelpLayerMessage` 0x1001bd39 sets static input mode **0x39** and emits `/showfile What 's-This General Help.html`. The port retains that mode for the HUD lifetime and opens the existing InfoView page; closing the Help floater does not clear it. `InputConfig_t::CheckObjectUnderMouse` 0x10019f00 and `FrameProcess` 0x1001ae14 suppress normal dynel and Shift cursor changes while this mode is set; `MousePointerModule_t` 0x10020120 resets the next-frame pointer to standard. The port therefore draws standard pointer 0x135 without action details in this mode. Regressions: `hud_help::tests::native_help_button_option_click_and_close`, `hud_target::tests::help_mode_keeps_standard_cursor_without_shift_details` (not exercised in this worker).

**UNRESOLVED: contextual target dispatch and mode exit.** `HotSpot_t::GetHelpText` 0x10022dec returns the optional string at +0x18; `SetHelpText` 0x1001dd0f assigns it. The only local setter caller found is `ActionView_t` 0x100247cf, assigning `/showfile Action window.html`; no local getter caller was found. Traced `FramePreProcess` 0x10019b6a (clears only transient modes), `FrameProcess` 0x1001ae14 (hotspot check precedes input), `CheckInput` 0x1001a4c1, `ProcessInput` 0x1001a31a, `CheckHotSpotList` 0x1001db89, GUIModule frame 0x1012f7cb, pointer frame 0x10020120, and Escape handlers 0x1002899e/0x1002bb28 do not establish a help pick/exit contract. The full compiled hotkey text at 0x101aab20 (0x31b8 bytes, parsed by 0x1001ac19) has no HelpMode binding; the `hotkeys.txt` pathname is not read. Installed GUI XML contains no `help`, `help_file`, `helptext`, or `help_text` attributes, and `View` XML constructor 0x1014e21c reads no help metadata. Thus existing tooltip text and border `SetHelpFile` are **not** substituted for HotSpot help. The shipped General Help page describes right-click cancellation, but no native handler implementing it was established; no fabricated exit binding or target→help regression is claimed.

### Exhaustive retail option wiring ledger (bug 12)

This ledger supersedes earlier option-status prose above, including the stored-only lists in “Not ported / unresolved” and “live consumers added later”. It records source wiring, **not** a build/test/live-window acceptance result. `LIVE` means a runtime reader exists, not that every retail branch is faithful; `HARDCODED` means the feature exists but ignores this control; `STORED ONLY` means changing/saving the preference is not implementation. `FEATURE ABSENT`, `STORE MISMATCH`, and `UNRESOLVED` are explicit gaps.

Inventory: installed `OptionPanel/Root.xml` has **153 active control occurrences** (including seven distinct `VisualFlags` bit controls and `esc_tips` on two pages), **146 distinct variable names**. The old “147 options” count is superseded. XML comments (including `AutoRejectMentorInvites`) are not active controls. Labels below retain the retail localization key rather than invent translations.

**GUI scale is not a retail Options control:** none of these 153 `Root.xml` controls defines `GuiScale` or another GUI-scale preference. Native macOS display/DPI scaling belongs to the window-to-GUI coordinate mapping, not an invented settings key. `CCFadeLow`, `CCFadeHigh`, `CCFadeDelay`, and sidebar transparency change opacity/timing, **not** GUI size. No fabricated scale slider is included in this ledger.

Store evidence: `options/model.rs::Opt::{from_xml,value}` and GUI `OptionControl_c` 0x100c22fc: `int`/`float` selects IndependentPrefs (`IP Login` → account `Login.cfg`, `IP Char` → character `Char.cfg`); any other/omitted `opt_type` selects a DValue regardless of `opt_category`. `DV Main/Login/Char` comes from the actual `{Main,Login,Char}Prefs.xml` registry, persisted in root/account/character `Prefs.xml`; `DV Variables` is session-only. Load/save: `dvalue.rs`, GUI 0x1006c371/0x1006bacd. Every control therefore has a store even when it has no consumer. Checkboxes/sliders/radios write through GUI 0x100c1ae6/0x100c08c5/0x100c2052. An unlocated consumer address is left unresolved, not borrowed from the generic widget writer.

Audio row detail (current implementation): `audio_prefs` → `Audio::set_prefs` updates live FX/music/player mixer buses, including existing one-shots, ambience, weather, emitters and fading music. `VoiceSndFxOn/Volume` use the player bus: SoundInterfaces 0x10006dfa/0x10006e4c computes master × voice × mute factors, **not** FX volume; master callback 0x10006d37 updates FX/music without refreshing cached player total, a retained retail callback quirk. Playlist 0x100025fc selects player versus FX gain by sound+0x284. `SoundOnOff` discards/rejects voices; re-enabling restarts the requested world layer/emitters, matching Miles teardown/reinitialization rather than just zero gain. `flow::apply_login_audio_prefs` / `options::login_audio_prefs` applies actual Main/Login archives before character-select/loading audio. `BattlemusicMode` reaches `CombatMusic::set_pref` (GUI 0x100c4862, SoundInterfaces 0x100019fc). Voice channel/type readers are `VoicePrefs` → `Chat::{heard_voice,voice_send}` (GUI 0x10086728/0x1008c953/0x1008c012). Retail has no separate ambient-volume control: ambience shares FX.

The fixed key rows and provider registry are exhaustive through the retail data-driven tables named above, rather than a second copied binding registry. Key-page limitations listed in the preceding “Deviations / gaps” paragraph remain applicable.

Headless acceptance hooks (`play/flow/live.rs`, added but not exercised by this documentation worker): `option=NativeVariable:value` selects the shipped category/control and dispatches checkbox/radio/slider input through `Play::input` / `HudOptions`, then checks the native store and available runtime consumers (controls/camera/avatar fade/chat/nametags/audio buses). `optioncheck=name:value` checks without mutation and can be used in a separate actual relog session. `optionpersist=name:name` saves actual user files and loads fresh `DValues` with `open_user` for the same root/account/character; **disk reload is not server relog**. Representative controls: `FXVolume:0.4`, `MouseTurnSensitivity:7`, `ZoomSpeed:12`, `FadeCharacterEndAlpha:0.4`, `ChatShowOGrpInTitleBar:0`, `ShowAllNames:0`. Live sessions require the live lock; all audio is muted. **Real-window acceptance: not done (user using Mac).**
